śrīlakṣmīdharaviracitaḥ
kṛtyakalpataruḥ
brahmacārikāṇḍam
śrīlakṣmīdharaviracitakṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthavivecanakāṇḍam
maṅgalācaraṇam
dharmanirmalahemakumbhaśiraso yasyāmarāṇāṃ gṛhāḥ
saṃkhyātītamumukṣubhikṣupariṣanmitrāṇi satrālayāḥ |
tīrtheṣu prathayanti dharmanagaradvārānukārāṃs tulāḥ
kāṇḍe tīrthavivecanaṃ vitanute lakṣmīdharaḥ so ‘ṣṭame ||
pratijñā
aṣṭame tīrthakāṇḍe ‘smiṃs tīrthayātrāvidhiḥ purā |
vārāṇasyāś ca māhātmyaṃ prayāgasya tataḥ param ||
gaṅgāyāś cāpi māhātmyaṃ gayāmāhātmyam eva ca |
kurukṣetrasya māhātmyaṃ tathā pṛthūdakasya ca ||
puṣkarasya ca māhātmyaṃ madhurāyās tathaiva ca |
ujjayinyā narmadāyās tathā kubjāmrakasya ca ||
śūkarasya ca māhātmyaṃ tathā kokāmukhasya ca |
badaryāśramamāhātmyaṃ mandārasya tathaiva ca ||
[2]        śālagrāmasya māhātmyaṃ tathaiva stutasvāminaḥ |
dvārakāyāś ca māhātmyaṃ tathā lohārgalasya ca ||
kedārasya naimiśasya nānātīrthāny ataḥ param |
mahāpathasya yātrā ca vistareṇa prakīrtitā ||
caturviṃśatiparvāṇi tīrthakāṇḍe kṛtāni tu |
[3]
1
atha tīrthayātrāvidhiḥ
tatra mahābhārate |
ṛṣibhiḥ kratavaḥ proktā vedeṣv iha yathākramam |
phalaṃ caiva tathā tattvaṃ pretya ceha ca sarvaśaḥ ||
na te śakyā daridreṇa yajñāḥ prāptuṃ mahīpate |
bahūpakaraṇā yajñā nānāsaṃbhāravistarāḥ ||
prāpyante parthivair etaiḥ samṛddhair vā naraiḥ kvacit |
nārthanyūnair avagaṇair ekātmabhir asaṃhitaiḥ ||
yo daridrair api vidhiḥ śakyaḥ prāptuṃ sureśvaraḥ |
[tulyo yajñaphalaiḥ puṇyais taṃ nibodha yudhiṣṭhira] ||
ṛṣīṇāṃ paramaṃ guhyam idaṃ bharatasattama |
tīrthābhigamanaṃ puṇyaṃ yajñair api viśiṣyate ||
anupoṣya trirātrāṇi tīrthāny anabhigamya ca |
adatvā kāñcanaṃ gāś ca daridro nāma jāyate ||
agniṣṭomādibhir yajñair iṣṭvā vipuladakṣiṇaiḥ |
[4]        na tat phalam avāpnoti tīrthābhigamanena yat ||
“avagaṇaiḥ” ukṣādisahāyarahitaiḥ | “ekātmabhiḥ” patnīrahitaiḥ “ “asaṃhataiḥ” ṛtvigādisahāyarahitaiḥ |
tathā |
yasya hastau ca pādau ca manaś caiva susaṃyatam |
vidyā tapaś ca kīrtiś ca sa tīrthaphalam aśnute ||
“hastau saṃyatau” adattādānādinivṛttyā, pādāv apy agamyadeśanivṛttyā | mano ‘pi saṃkalpanivṛttyā | “vidyā” tīrthaguṇādijñānam | “tapaḥ” tīrthanivāsopavāsādilakṣaṇam | “kīrtiḥ” saccaritatvādiprasiddhiḥ |
[tathā] |
pratigrahād upāvṛttaḥ saṃtuṣṭo yena kenacit |
ahaṃkāravimuktaś ca sa tīrthaphalam aśnute ||
akalpako nirārambho laghvāhāro jitendriyaḥ |
vimuktaḥ sarvasaṅgair yaḥ sa tīrthaphalam aśnute ||
[5] “akalpako” dambharahitaḥ | “nirārambho” atrārthārjanādivyāpārarahitaḥ |”sarvasaṅgaiḥ” saṅgo ‘trāvihitāsaktiḥ |
tathā |
akopanaś ca rājendra satyavādī dṛḍhavrataḥ |
ātmopamaś ca bhūteṣu sa tīrthaphalam aśnute ||
“ātmopamaś ca bhūteṣu” iti sarvabhūtahita ity arthaḥ |
śaṅkhaḥ |
yasya hastau ca pādau ca manaś caiva susaṃyatam |
vidyā tapaś ca kīrtiś ca tīrthaphalam aśnute ||
etac ca hastasaṃyamādi tīrthayātrāṅgam, mahābhārate tīrthayātrām upakramya vacanāt | tīrthasnānādikarmāṅgaṃ ca śaṅkhena yātrāprakaraṇam antareṇaiva “sa tīrthaphalam aśnue” iti sāmānyābhidhānāt |
vāyupurāṇe |
tīrthāny anusaran vīraḥ śraddadhānaḥ samāhitaḥ |
kṛtapāpaś ca śuddhyeta kiṃ punaḥ śuddhakarmakṛt ||
tiryagyoniṃ na gacchec ca kudeśe ca na jāyate |
svarge bhavati vai vipro mokṣopāyaṃ ca vindati ||
[6]        aśraddadhānaḥ pāpātmā nāstiko ‘cchinnasaṃśayaḥ |
hetuniṣṭhaś ca pañcaite na tīrthaphalabhāginaḥ ||
mahābhārate |
kāmaṃ krodhaṃ ca lobhaṃ ca yo jitvā tīrtham āvaset |
na tena kiṃcin na prāptaṃ tīrthābhigamanād bhavet ||
tīrthāni tu yathoktena vidhinā saṃcaranti ye |
sarvadvandvasahā dhīrās te narāḥ svargagāminaḥ ||
“sarvadvandvasahāḥ” śītātapādikleśasahiṣṇavaḥ |
bhīṣma uvāca –
śṛṇu tīrthāni gadato mānasāni mamānagha |
yeṣu samyaṅ naraḥ snātvā prayāti paramāṃ gatim ||
satyaṃ tīrthaṃ kṣamā tīrthaṃ tīrtham indriyanigrahaḥ |
sarvabhūtadayā tīrthaṃ tīrtham ārjavam eva ca ||
dānaṃ tīrthaṃ damas tīrthaṃ santoṣas tīrtham ucyate |
brahmacaryaṃ paraṃ tīrthaṃ tīrthaṃ ca priyavāditā ||
jñānaṃ tīrthaṃ dhṛtis tīrthaṃ tapas tīrtham udāhṛtam |
tīrthānām api yat tīrthaṃ viśuddhir manasaḥ parā ||
[7]        na jalāplutadehasya snānam ity abhidhīyate |
sa snāto yo damasnātaḥ śuciḥ śuddhamanomalaḥ ||
yo lubdhaḥ piśunaḥ krūro dāmbhiko viṣayātmakaḥ |
sarvatīrtheṣv api snātaḥ pāpo malina eva saḥ ||
na śarīramalatyāgān naro bhavati nirmalaḥ |
mānase tu male tyakte bhavaty antaḥ sunirmalaḥ ||
jāyante ca mriyante ca jaleṣv eva jalaukasaḥ |
na ca gacchanti te svargam aviśuddhamanomalāḥ ||
viṣayeṣv atisaṃrāgo mānaso mala ucyate |
teṣv eva hi virādo ‘sya nairmalyaṃ samudāhṛtam ||
cittam antargataṃ duṣṭaṃ tīrthasnānair na śuddhyati |
śataśo ‘tha jale dhautaṃ surābhāṇḍam ivāśuci ||
dānam iṣṭaṃ tapaḥ śaucaṃ tīrthasevā śrutaṃ tathā |
sarvāṇy etāny atīrthāni yadi bhāvo na nirmalaḥ ||
nirgṛhītendriyagrāmo yatraiva vasate naraḥ |
tatra tatra kurukṣetraṃ naimiśaṃ puṣkarāṇi ca ||
jñānapūte dhyānajale rāgadveṣamalāpahe |
yaḥ snāti mānase tīrthe sa yāti paramāṃ gatim ||
etat te kathitaṃ rājan mānasaṃ tīrthalakṣaṇam |
bhaumānām api tīrthānāṃ puṇyatve kāraṇaṃ śṛṇu ||
yathā śarīrasyoddeśāḥ kecit puṇyatamāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tathā pṛthivyām uddeśāḥ kecit puṇyatamāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
[8]        prabhāvād adbhutā bhūmeḥ salilasya ca tejasā |
parigrahān munīnāṃ ca tīrthānāṃ puṇyatā smṛtā ||
tasmād bhaumeṣu tīrtheṣu mānaseṣu ca nityaśaḥ |
ubhayeṣu ca yaḥ snāti sa yāti paramāṃ gatim ||
“salilasya tejasā” toyaprabhāveṇa |
brahmapurāṇe |
sarveṇa gāṅgena jalena samyaṅ
mṛtparvatenāpy atha bhāvaduṣṭaḥ |
ājanmanaḥ snānaparo ‘pi nityaṃ
na śuddhytīty eva vayaṃ vadāmaḥ ||
gaṅgāditīrtheṣ vasanti matsyā
devālaye pakṣisaṅghāś ca nityam |
bhavojjhitās te na phalaṃ labhante
tīrtāc ca devāyatanāc ca mukhyāt ||
bhāvaṃ tato hṛtkamale nidhāya
tīrthāni seveta samāhitātmā |
yā tīrthayātrā kathitā munīndraiḥ
kṛtā prayuktāpy anumoditā vā ||
tāṃ brahmacārī vidhivat karoti
susaṃyato guruṇā saṃniyuktaḥ |
sarvasvanāśē ‘py atha vā nṛpas tu
sadbrāhmaṇān agrat eva kṛtvā ||
[9]        yajñādhikāre ‘py atha vā nivṛtte
vipras tu tīrthāni paribhrameta |
tīrtheṣv alaṃ yajñaphale hi yasmāt
proktaṃ munīndrair amalasvabhāvaiḥ ||
yasyeṣṭiyajñeṣv adhikāritāsya
varaṃ gṛhaṃ gṛhadharmāś ca sarve ||
evaṃ gṛhasthāśramasaṃsthitasya
tīrthe gatiḥ pūrvatarair niṣiddhā |
sarvāṇi tīrthāny api cāgnihotra-
tulyāni naiveti vayaṃ vadāmaḥ ||
yo yaḥ kaścit tīrthayātrāṃ tu gacchet
susaṃyataḥ sa ca pūrvaṃ gṛhe sve |
kṛtopavāsaḥ śucir apramattaḥ
saṃpūjayed bhaktinamro gaṇeśam ||
devān pitṝn brāhmaṇāṃś caiva sādhūn
bhīmān pitṝn vittaśaktyā prayatnāt |
pratyāgataś cāpi tathā punas tu
devān pitṝn brāhmaṇān pūjayec ca |
evaṃ kurvatas tasya tīrthādyudakaṃ
phalaṃ tasmān nātra saṃdeham asti ||
“bhāvo” viśuddhā manovṛttiḥ | “devān pitṝn” iti tīrthayāñāṃ kariṣyanto devapitṛbrāhmaṇa-pūjanam āvaśyakam | “pitṝn [10] vittaśaktyā” ity anena ca viśeṣato dhanavatā pitṛpūjanaṃ kartavyam iti vidhīyate |
devīpurāṇe |
tīrtheṣu brāhmaṇaṃ naiva parīkṣeta kathaṃcana |
annārthinam anuprāptaṃ bhojyaṃ taṃ manur abravīt ||
saktubhiḥ piṇḍadānaṃ ca saṃyāvaiḥ pāyasena tu |
kartavyam ṛṣibhiḥ proktam eṣākena guḍena ca ||
tilapiṇyākaṃ deyaṃ tu bhaktimadbhir naraiḥ sadā |
śrāddhaṃ tatra tu kartavyam anvāvāhanavarjitam ||
śvadhvāṅkṣagṛdhrakākānāṃ naiva dṛṣṭihataṃ ca yat |
śrāddhaṃ tat tairthikaṃ proktaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tuṣṭikārakam ||
akāle ‘py atha vā kāle tīrthe śrāddhaṃ tathā naraiḥ |
prāptair eva sadā kāryaṃ kartavyaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam ||
piṇḍaṃ dānaṃ tu tac chastaṃ pitṝṇāṃ cātivallabham |
vilambo naiva kartavyo naiva vighnaṃ samācaret ||
[11] śaṅkhaḥ |
tīrthaṃ prāpyānuṣaṅgeṇa snānaṃ tīrthe samācaret |
snānajaṃ phalam āpnoti tīrthayātrāphalaṃ na tu ||
tathā |
nṛṇāṃ pāpakṛtāṃ tīrthe bhavet pāpasya saṃkṣayaḥ |
[yathoktaphaladaṃ tīrthaṃ bhavec chuddhātmanāṃ nṛṇām ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ sa labhate yaḥ parārthena gacchati ] |
ardhaṃ tīrthaphalaṃ tasya yaḥ prasaṅgena gacchati ||
prakṛtiṃ kuśamayīṃ tīrthavāriṇi majjayet |
majjayet tu yam uddiśya aṣṭabhāgaṃ labheta saḥ ||
atra prayāgavyatiriktatīrthagamane yānaniṣedhapramāṇadarśanān na tīrthāntare yānagamane nirodhaḥ | tīrthopavāsaś ca phalaviśeṣārthaḥ,
tīrtham abhigamya vratopavāsaniyamayuktas tryaham avagāhamānas trirātram uṣitvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate svastimāṃś ca bhavati |
iti devalavacanāt  |  ekasminn api divase tattattīrthaviśeṣaprāptau bahūny api śrāddhāni kartavyāni,
akāle ‘py atha vā kāle tīrthaśrāddhaṃ tathā naraiḥ |
prāptair eva sadā kāryaṃ kartavyaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam ||
iti devīpurāṇavacanāt |
[12]
2
atha vārāṇasīmāhātmyam
tatra matsyapurāṇe |
īśvara uvāca –
vārāṇasīha bhuvanatrayasārabhūtā
ramyā sadā mama purī girirājaputri |
atrāgatā vividhaduṣkṛtakāriṇo ‘pi
pāpakṣayād virajasaḥ pratibhānti martyāḥ ||
evaṃ smṛtaṃ priyatamaṃ mama devi nityaṃ
kṣetraṃ vicitratarugulmalatāsupuṣpam |
asmin mṛtās tanubhṛtaḥ padam āpnuvanti
mokṣākhyam eva manasāpi na saṃśayo ‘tra ||
tathā |
idaṃ guhyatamaṃ kṣetraṃ sadā vārāṇasī mama |
sarveṣām eva jantūnāṃ hetur mokṣasya sarvadā ||
asmin siddhāḥ sadā devi madīyavratam āsthitāḥ |
abhyasyanti paraṃ yogaṃ yuktātmāno jitendriyāḥ ||
[13] “nānāliṅgadharāḥ” mekhalājinādiliṅgadhāriṇaś catvāra āśramiṇa ity arthaḥ |
tathā |
manmanā mama bhaktaś ca mayi sarvārpitakriyaḥ |
yathā mokṣam ihāpnoti anyatra na tathā kvacit ||
tathā |
vimuktaṃ na mayā yasmān mokṣyate vā kadācana |
mahat kṣetram idaṃ tasmād avimuktam iti smṛtam ||
naimiśe ca kurukṣetre gaṅgādvāre ca puṣkare |
snānāt saṃsevanād vāpi [na] mokṣaḥ prāpyate yataḥ ||
iha saṃprāpyate yena tata etad viśiṣyate |
prayāge vā bhaven mokṣo mahākāle ‘tha vā priye ||
amarakaṇṭake tadvat tathā kāyāvarohaṇe |
kālañjare mahābhāga iha vā matparigrahāt ||
prayāgād api tīrthāgryād idam eva mahat smṛtam |
“idam eva mahat smṛtam” ity alpāyāsenaivātra mokṣaprāpteḥ |
tathā |
dhyāyatas tatra māṃ nityaṃ yogāgnir dīpyate bhṛśam |
kaivalyaṃ paramaṃ yāti devānām api durlabham ||
avyaktaliṅgair munibhiḥ sarvasiddhāntavedibhiḥ |
iha saṃprāpyate mokṣo durlabho ‘nyatra karhicit ||
[14]        tebhyaś cāhaṃ prayacchāmi yogaiśvaryam anuttamam |
ātmanaś caiva sāyujyam īpsitaṃ sthānam eva ca ||
kuberas tu mahāyakṣas tathā sarvārpitakriyaḥ |
kṣetrasaṃsevanād eva gaṇeśatvam avāpa ha ||
sarvato bhavitā yaś ca so ‘pi bhaktyā mamaiva tu |
ihaivārādhya māṃ devi siddhiṃ yāsyaty anuttamām ||
parāśarasuto yogī ṛṣir vyāso mahātapāḥ |
dharmayukto bhaviṣyaṃś ca vedasaṃsthāpravartakaḥ ||
vatsyate so ‘pi padmākṣi kṣetre ‘smin munipuṅgavaḥ |
brahmā devarṣibhiḥ sardhaṃ viṣṇur vāyur divākaraḥ ||
devarājas tathā śakras tathānye tridivaukaksaḥ |
upāsate mahātmānaḥ sarve mām iha suvratāḥ ||
anye ca yoginaḥ siddhāś channarūpā mahāvrate |
ananyamanaso bhūtvā mām ihopāsate sadā ||
alarkaś ca purīm etāṃ matprasādād avāpsyati |
sa caināṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā cāturvarṇāśramākulām ||
sphītāṃ janapadākīrṇāṃ bhuktvā ca suciraṃ nṛpaḥ |
mayi sarvārpitaprāṇo mām evaṃ pratipatsyate ||
tataḥ prabhṛti cārvaṅgi ye ‘pi kṣetranivāsinaḥ |
gṛhiṇo liṅgino vāpi madbhaktā matparāyaṇāḥ ||
matprasādād gamiṣyanti mokṣaṃ paramadurlabham |
viṣayāsaktacitto ‘pi tyaktadharmaratir naraḥ ||
[15]        iha kṣetre mṛtaḥ so ‘pi saṃsāraṃ na punar viśet |
ye punar nirmamā dhīrāḥ sattvasthā vijitendriyāḥ ||
vratinaś ca nirārambhāḥ sarve te mayi bhāvitāḥ |
dehabhedaṃ samāsādya dīpyante saṅgavarjitāḥ ||
gatā eva paraṃ mokṣaṃ prasādān mama suvratāḥ |
janmāntarasahasreṣu yuñjan yogī yam āpnuyāt ||
tam ihaiva paraṃ mokṣaṃ maraṇād adhigacchati |
etat saṃkṣepato devi kṣetrasyāsya mahat phalam ||
avimuktasya kathitaṃ mayā te guhyam uttamam |
“ihaiva” janmanīti śeṣaḥ |
tathā |
avimukte parā siddhir avimukte parā gatiḥ |
japtaṃ dattaṃ hutaṃ ceṣṭaṃ tapas taptaṃ kṛtaṃ ca yat ||
dānam adhyayanaṃ sarvaṃ yathā bhavati cākṣayam |
janmāntarasahasreṇa yat pāpaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam ||
avimuktaṃ praviṣṭasya tat sarvaṃ vrajati kṣayam |
avimuktāgninā dagdham agnau tūlam ivāhitam ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrā vai varṇasaṃkarāḥ |
kṛmimlecchāśca ye cānye saṃkīrṇāḥ pāpayonayaḥ ||
kīṭāḥ pipīlikāś caiva ye cānye mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ |
kālena nidhanaṃ prāptā avimukte śṛṇu priye ||
candrārdhamaulinaḥ sarve lalāṭākṣā vṛṣadhvajāḥ |
śive mama pure devi jāyante tatra mānavāḥ ||
[16]        akāmo vā sakāmo vā api tiryaggato ‘pi vā |
avimukte tyajan prāṇān mama loke mahīyate ||
avimukte yadā gacchet kadācit kālaparyayāt |
aśmanā caraṇau bhittvā tatraiva nidhanaṃ vrajet ||
tathā |
yāti tīrthāni śrūyante triṣu lokeṣu viśrute |
avimuktasya pāde tu nityaṃ saṃnihitāni vai ||
tathā |
ā dehapatanād yāvat tat kṣetraṃ yo na muñcati |
brahmacaryavrataiḥ samyak samyag iṣṭaṃ makhair bhavet ||
tathā |
yas tatra nivased vipraḥ saṃyatātmā samāhitaḥ |
traikālyam api bhuñjāno vāyubhakṣaḥ sadā bhavet ||
nimeṣamātram api yo hy avimukte tu bhaktimān |
[brahmacaryasamāyuktaḥ sa paraṃ prāpnuyāt tapaḥ ||
tatra māsaṃ vased vīro laghvāhāro jitendriyaḥ |]
samyak tena vrataṃ cīrṇaṃ divyaṃ pāśupataṃ mahat ||
japan mṛtyubhayaṃ tīrtvā sa yāti paramāṃ gatim |
naiḥśreyasīṃ gatiṃ puṇyāṃ tathā yogagatiṃ bhajet ||
na hi yogagatir labhyā janmāntaraśatair api |
prāpyate kṣetramāhātmyāt prabhāvāc chaṅkarasya ca ||
[17] “naiḥśreyasī gatiḥ” viśiṣṭādehaprāptir mokṣānukūlā |
brahmahā yo ‘bhigacchet tu avimuktaṃ kadācana |
tasya kṣetrasya māhātymād brahmahatyā nivartate ||
ā dehapatanād yāvat tat kṣetraṃ yo na muñcati |
na kevalaṃ brahmahatyā prākṛtaś ca nivartate ||
[prāpya viśveśvaraṃ devaṃ na sa bhūto vijāyate |]
“prākṛtaḥ” saṃsārabandhaḥ |
ananyamānaso bhūtvā yo ‘vimuktaṃ na muñcati |
tasya devaḥ sadā tuṣṭaḥ sarvān kāmān prayacchati ||
dvāraṃ yat sāṃkhyayogānāṃ sa tatra vasati prabhuḥ |
saguṇo hi bhaved devo bhaktānām anukampayā ||
avimuktaṃ paraṃ kṣetram avimukte parā gatiḥ |
avimukte parā siddhir avimukte paraṃ padam ||
avimuktaṃ niṣeveta devarṣigaṇasevitam |
yadīcchen mānavo dhīro na punarjanma ātmanaḥ ||
meroḥ śakyā guṇā vaktuṃ dvīpānāṃ ca tathaiva ca |
samudrāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ nāvimukte tu śakyate ||
antakāle manuṣyāṇāṃ chidyamāneṣu marmasu |
vāyunā preryamāṇānāṃ smṛtir naivopajāyate ||
avimukte hy antakāle bhaktānām īśvaraḥ svayam |
karmabhiḥ preyamāṇānāṃ karṇajāpaṃ prayacchati ||
īśvaraprerito yāti duṣprāpam akṛtātmabhiḥ |
aśāśvatam idaṃ jñātvā mānuṣyaṃ bahukilbiṣam ||
[18]        avimuktaṃ niṣeveta saṃsārabhayamocanam |
bhogamokṣapradaṃ divyaṃ bahupāpavināśanam ||
vidhnair āloḍyamāno ‘pi yo ‘vimuktaṃ na muñcati |
na muñcati jarāmṛtyuṃ janma caitad aśāśvatam ||
avimuktaprasādāt tu śivasāyujyam āpnuyāt |
tathā |
[yadi pāpī yadi śavo yadi vā dhārmiko naraḥ |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyaḥ avimuktaṃ vrajed yadi ||
tathā |]
sarvaguhyottamaṃ sthānaṃ mama priyatamaṃ śubham |
dhanyāḥ praviṣṭāḥ suśroṇi mama bhaktā dvijātayaḥ ||
madbhaktiparamā nityaṃ nānyabhaktāś ca ye narāḥ |
asmin prāṇān parityajya gacchanti paramāṃ gatim ||
sadā yajati sattreṇa sadā dānaṃ prayacchati |
sadā tapasvī bhavati avimukte sthito naraḥ ||
yo māṃ prayajate nityaṃ tasya tuṣyāmy ahaṃ priye |
sarvadānāni yo dadyāt sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ ||
sarvatīrthābhiṣekī ca sa prapadyeta mām iha |
avimuktaṃ sadā devi ye vrajanti suniścitāḥ ||
te tiṣthantīha suśroṇi mama bhaktās triviṣṭape |
matprasādāt tu te devi dīpyante śubhalocane ||
[19]        durdharṣā durdharāś caiva bhavanti vigatajvarāḥ |
tathā |
avimukte yajante tu madbhaktāḥ kṛtaniścayāḥ |
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttiḥ kalpakoṭiśatair api ||
[tathā] |
avimuktaṃ gatā ye vai mahāpuṇyakṛto narāḥ |
akṣarā hy ajarāś caiva adeyāś ca bhavanti te ||
matprasādād varārohe mām eva praviśanti te |
śākaparṇāśino dāntāḥ saṃprakṣālā marīcipāḥ ||
dantolūkhalinaś caiva aśmakuṭṭās tathā pare |
“saṃprakṣālāḥ” sadyaḥprakṣālakāḥ śvastanabhakṣarahitāḥ |
māsi māsi kuśāgreṇa jalam āsvādayanti ye |
vṛkṣamūlaniketāś ca śilāśayyās tathāpare ||
ādityavapuṣaḥ sarve jitakrodhā jitendriyāḥ |
evaṃ bahuvidhair anyair anyatra caritavratāḥ ||
traikālyam api bhuñjānā ye ‘vimuktanivāsinaḥ |
tapaś caranti cānyatra kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm ||
ye ‘vimukte vasantīha svarge ca praviśanti te |
[20]        matsamaḥ puruṣo nāsti tvatsamā nāsti yoṣitām ||
avimuktasamaṃ kṣetraṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati |
avimukte paro yogo hy avimukte parā gatiḥ ||
avimukte paro mokṣaḥ kṣetraṃ naivāsti tādṛśam |
paraṃ guhyaṃ pravakṣyāmi tattvena varavarṇini ||
avimukte mahākṣetre yad bhuktaṃ hi mayā purā |
janmāntaraśatair devi yogo ‘yaṃ yadi labhyate ||
mokṣaḥ śatasahasreṇa janmanā labhyate na vā |
avimukte vased devi madbhaktaḥ kṛtaniścayaḥ ||
ekena janmanā so ‘pi yogaṃ mokṣaṃ ca vindati |
tathā |
kṣetre mūrtaḥ sadā dharmas tasmin saṃnihitaḥ priye |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ gatis tu paramā smṛtā ||
tathā |
svarṇaśṛṅgīṃ raupyakhurāṃ cailakaṇṭhāṃ payasvinīm |
vārāṇasyāṃ tu yo dadyād ākarṇāyatalocane ||
gāṃ datvā tu varārohe brāhmaṇe vedapārage |
āsaptamaṃ kulaṃ tena pāvitaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
yo dadyād brāhmaṇe kuṃcit tasmin kṣetre varānane |
kanakaṃ rajataṃ vastram annādyaṃ bahu vistaram ||
[21]        akṣayaṃ cāvyayaṃ caiva syāt tatrasthaṃ sulocane |
śṛṇu tattvena tīrthasya vibhūtiṃ vyuṣṭim eva ca ||
tatra snātā mahābhāge bhavanti virajā janāḥ |
daśānām aśvamedhānāṃ yajñānāṃ yat phalaṃ bhavet ||
tad avāpnoti dharmātmā tatra snātvā varānane |
svalpam alpaṃ ca yo dadyād brāhmaṇe vedapārage ||
śubhāṃ gatim avāpnoti agnivac caiva dīpyate |
tathā |
upavāsaṃ tu yaḥ kṛtvā viprān saṃtarpayen naraḥ |
sa sautrāmaṇiyajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
ekāhāras tu yas tiṣṭhen māsaṃ tatra varānane |
yāvajjīvakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ māsenaikena naśyati ||
agnipraveśaṃ ye kuryur avimukte vidhānataḥ |
praviśanti sukhaṃ te māṃ na punar bhāvino janāḥ ||
kurvanty anaśanaṃ ye tu madbhaktāḥ kṛtaniścayāḥ |
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttiḥ kalpakoṭiśatair api ||
arcayed yas tu māṃ devi avimukte varānane |
tasya dharmaṃ pravakṣyāmi yāvad āpnoti mānavaḥ ||
daśa sauvarṇikaṃ puṇaṃ yo ‘vimukte prayacchati |
agnihotraphalaṃ dhūpe gandhadānena tac chṛṇu ||
[22]        bhūmidānena tattulyaṃ gopradānaphalaṃ smṛtam |
saṃmārjane pañcaśataṃ sahasram anulepane ||
mālāśatasahasraṃ tu anantaṃ gītavādanam |
brahmahatyāpahaṃ tīrthaṃ kṣetram etan mayā kṛtam ||
śmaśānam etad bhadraṃ te varaṃ ca varavarṇini |
kālo bhūtvā jagad idaṃ saṃharāmi sṛjāmi ca ||
deveśi sarvaguhyānāṃ sthānaṃ priyatmaṃ mama |
madbhaktās tatra gacchanti viṣṇubhaktās tathaiva ca ||
ye bhaktā bhāskare devi lokanāthe divākare |
atrastho yas tyajed dehaṃ mām eva praviśet tu saḥ ||
tathā |
jñāne vihitaniṣṭhānāṃ paramānandam icchatām |
yā gatir vihitā sadbhiḥ sāvimukte mṛtasya tu ||
bhavasya prītir atulā tv avimukte hy anaupamā |
asaṃkhyeyaṃ phalaṃ tatra hy akṣayā ca gatir bhavet ||
paraṃ guhyaṃ samākhyātaṃ śmaśānam iti saṃjñitam |
avimuktaṃ na sevante vañcitās te narā bhuvi ||
avimukte sthitaiḥ puṇyaiḥ pāṃsubhir vāyuneritaiḥ |
spṛṣṭvā duṣkṛtakarmāṇo yāsyanti paramāṃ gatim ||
avimuktaguṇān vaktuṃ devadānavamānavaiḥ |
na śakyante hy ameyatvāt svayaṃ yatra bhavaḥ sthitaḥ ||
[23]        nānāhitāgnir nāyajvā nāśucis taskaro ‘pi vā |
avimukte vased yas tu sa vased īśvarālaye ||
na tatrāpuṇyakṛt kaścit prabhāvād īśvarasya tu |
ajñānāj jñānato vāpi striyā vā puruṣeṇa vā ||
yat kiṃcid aśubhaṃ karma kṛtaṃ mānuṣabuddhinā |
avimukte praviṣṭasya tat sarvaṃ bhasmasād bhavet ||
tathā |
dṛṣṭvaitān dīnakṛpaṇān pāpān duṣkṛtakāriṇaḥ |
devo ‘nukampayā sarvān nayaty āśu parāṃ gatim ||
bhaktānukampī bhagavāṃs tiryagyonigatān api |
bhagavāṃs tatra nayati yatra yānti na yājñikaḥ ||
tathā |
na sā gatiḥ kurukṣetre gaṅgādvāre na puṣkare |
yā gatir vihitā puṃsām avimuktanivāsinām ||
tiryagyonigatāḥ sattvā ye ‘vimuktakṛtālayāḥ |
kālena nidhanaṃ prāptās te ‘pi yānti parāṃ gatim ||
merumandaramātro ‘pi rāśiḥ pāpasya karmaṇaḥ |
avimuktaṃ samāsādya tatkṣaṇād vrajati kṣayam ||
śmaśānam iti vikhyātam avimuktaṃ śivālayam |
tad gṛhaṃ devadevasya tat tīrthaṃ tat tapovanam ||
tatra brahmādayo devā nārāyaṇapurogamāḥ |
yoginaś ca tathā sādhyā bhagavantaṃ sanātanam ||
[24]        upāsate śivaṃ bhaktyā madbhaktā matparāyaṇāḥ |
yā gatir jñānatapasāṃ yā gatir yajñayāginām ||
avimukte mṛtānāṃ tu sā gatir vihitā śubhā |
tathā |
ādityopāsanaṃ kṛtvā viprāś cāmaratāṃ gatāḥ |
anye ‘pi tatra ye varṇā bhavabhaktāḥ samāhitāḥ ||
avimukte vimuktās te yāsyanti paramāṃ gatim |
aṣṭau māsān vihāraḥ syād yatīnāṃ saṃyatātmanām ||
ekatra caturo māsān ardhaṃ ca nivaset punaḥ |
avimukte praviṣṭānāṃ vihāras tu na vidyate ||
na doṣo ‘bhibhavet tatra dṛṣṭaṃ śāstre purātane |
mokṣo ‘py asaṃśayas tatra pañcatvaṃ tu gatasya vai ||
striyaḥ pativratā yāś ca bhavabhaktāḥ samāhitāḥ |
avimukte vimuktās tā yāsyanti paramāṃ gatim ||
anyā yāḥ kāmacāriṇyaḥ striyo bhogaparāyaṇāḥ |
kālena nidhanaṃ prāptās tāś ca yānti parāṃ gatim ||
yatra yogaś ca mokṣaś ca prāpyate durlabho naraiḥ |
avimuktaṃ samāsādya nānyad gacchet tapovanam ||
sarvātmanā tapaḥ satyaṃ prāṇināṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
avimukte vased yas tu mama tulyo bhaven naraḥ ||
yato mayā na vimuktam avimuktaṃ tataḥ smṛtam |
avimuktaṃ na sevante mūḍhā ye tāmasā narāḥ ||
[25]        viṇmūtraretasāṃ madhye te vasanti punaḥ punaḥ |
kāmaḥ krodho ‘tilobhaś ca dambhaḥ stambho ‘timatsaraḥ ||
nidrā tandrā tathālasyaṃ paiśunyam iti te daśa |
avimukte sthitā vighnāḥ śakreṇa vihitāḥ svayam ||
vināyakopasargaś ca satataṃ mūrdhni tiṣṭhati |
puṇyam etad bhavet sarvaṃ bhaktānām anukampayā ||
paraṃ guhyam iti jñātvā tataḥ śāstrānudarśanāt |
vyāhṛtaṃ devadevais tu munibhis tattvadarśibhiḥ ||
medhasā viplutā bhūmir avimukte tu varjitā |
pūtā samabhavat sarvā mahādevena rakṣitā ||
saṃskāras tena kriyate bhūmer apy atra sūribhiḥ ||
“stambho” garvaḥ | “atimatsaraḥ” atyantābhyasūyā | “paiśunyam” anyadoṣāvaham |
tathā |
taṃ vai prāpya mahādevam īśvarādhyuṣitaṃ śubham |
avimuktaṃ kṛtārtho ‘smīty ātmānam upalakṣayet ||
ṛṣidevāsuragaṇair japahomaparāyaṇaiḥ |
yatibhir mokṣakāmaiś ca avimuktaṃ niṣevyate ||
nāvimukte mṛtaḥ kaścin narakaṃ yāti kilbiṣī |
īśvarānugṛhītā hi sarve yānti parāṃ gatim ||
[26] tathā |
bhūloke cāntarikṣe ca divi tīrthāṇi yāni ca |
atītya vartate sarvāny avimuktaṃ prabhāvataḥ ||
ye tu dhyānaṃ samāsādya yuktātmānaḥ samāhitāḥ |
saṃniyamyendriyagrāmaṃ japanti śatarudriyam ||
avimukte sthitā nityaṃ kṛtārthās te dvijātayaḥ |
tathā |
hanyamāno ‘pi yo vidvān vased vighnaśatair api |
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra gatvā na śocati ||
janmamṛtyujarāyuktaḥ paraṃ yāti śivālayam |
apunarmaraṇānāṃ yā sā gatir mokṣakāṅkṣiṇām ||
yāṃ prāpya kṛtakṛtyaḥ syād itthaṃ manyanti paṇḍitāḥ ||
tathā |
na dānair na tapobhiś ca na yajñair nāpi vidyayā |
prāpyate gatir iṣṭā hi yāvimukte tu labhyate ||
nānāvarṇā vivarṇāś ca caṇḍālā ye jugupsitāḥ |
kilbiṣaiḥ pūrṇadehāś ca prakṛṣṭaiḥ pātakais tathā ||
bheṣajaṃ paramaṃ teṣām avimuktaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
[27]        jātyantarasahasreṣu avimukte mriyeta yaḥ ||
bhakto viśveśvare nityaṃ na sa bhūyo ‘bhijāyate |
atra ceṣṭaṃ hutaṃ dattaṃ tapas taptaṃ kṛtaṃ ca yat ||
sarvam akṣayam etasminn avimukte na saṃśayaḥ |
kālenoparatiṃ yāti bhavasāyujyam akṣayam ||
kṛtvā pāpasahasrāṇi paścāt santāpam etya vai |
yo ‘vimuktaṃ na vimuñcet sa yāti paramāṃ gatim ||
uttaraṃ dakṣiṇaṃ vāpi ayanaṃ na vikalpayet |
sarvas tatra śubhaḥ kālo hy avimukte mriyeta yaḥ ||
na tatra kālo mīmāṃsyaḥ śubho vā yadi vāśūbhaḥ |
tasya devasya māhātmyāt sthāṇor vā ‘dbhutakarmaṇaḥ ||
sarveṣām eva nāthasya sarveṣāṃ ca vibhoḥ svayam |
tathā |
na hi yogād ṛte mokṣaḥ prāpyate bhuvi mānavaiḥ |
avimukte tu vasatāṃ yogo mokṣaś ca sidhyati ||
anena janmanaiveha prāpyate gatir uttamā |
eṣa eva prabhāvo ‘sti kṣetrasya parameśvari ||
ekena janmanā devi mokṣaṃ yāsyaty anuttamam |
avimuktena vasatā vyāsenāmitatejasā ||
naiva labdhā kvacid bhikṣā bhrāmyamāṇena yatnataḥ |
[28]        kṣudhāviṣṭas tataḥ kruddho ‘cintayac chāpam uttamam ||
“mā bhūt tripuruṣī vidyā mā bhūt tripuruṣaṃ dhanam |
mā bhūt tripuruṣaṃ rājyaṃ vyāso vārāṇasīṃ śapat ||
avimutena vasatāṃ janānāṃ puṇyakarmaṇām |
vighnaṃ sṛjāmi sarveṣāṃ yena siddhir na vidyate” ||
vyāsacittaṃ tadā jñātvā devadeva umāpatiḥ |
vyāsasya darśanaṃ datvā kṛtvā veṣaṃ tu mānuṣam ||
[“ehy ehi bhagavan sādho bhikṣāṃ grāhaya sattama |
asmadgṛhe kadācit tvaṃ nāgato ‘si mahāmune” ||
etac chrutvā prītamanā bhikṣāṃ grahītum udyataḥ |]
bhikṣāṃ datvā tu vyāsāya ṣaḍrasām amṛtopamām ||
anāsvāditapūrvā sā bhakṣitā muninā tataḥ |
bhikṣāṃ vyāsas tato bhuktvā cintayan hṛṣṭamānasaḥ ||
vyāsaḥ kamalapatrākṣi idaṃ vacanam abravīt |
devo devī nadī gaṅgā miṣṭam annaṃ śubhā matiḥ ||
vārāṇasyāṃ viśālākṣa vāsaḥ kasya na rocate |
evam uktvā tato vyāso nagarīm avalokayan ||
cintayaṃś cāpi tāṃ bhikṣāṃ hṛdayānandakāriṇīm |
apaśyat purato devaṃ devīṃ ca girijāṃ tadā ||
gṛhāṅgaṇasthitaṃ vyāsaṃ devadevo ‘bravīd idam |
“iha kṣetre na vastavyaṃ krodhanas tvaṃ mahāmune ||
evaṃ samayam āpanno devaṃ vyāso ‘bravīd vacaḥ |
vyāsa uvāca |
caturdaśyāṃ tathāṣṭamyāṃ praveśaṃ dātum arhasi |
[29]        evam astv ity anujñāya tatraivāntaradhīyata ||
na tad gṛhaṃ na sā devī na devo jñāyate kvacit |
evaṃ trailokyavikhyātaḥ purā vyāso mahātapāḥ ||
jñātvā kṣetraguṇān sarvān sthitas tasyaiva pārśvataḥ |
evaṃ vyāsaṃ sthitaṃ jñātvā kṣetraṃ śaṃsanti paṇḍitāḥ ||
tathā |
tapāṃsi yāni paṭhyante vratāni niyamāś ca ye |
sarvatīrthābhiṣekaṃ tu sarvadānaphalāni ca ||
sarvayajñeṣu yat puṇyam avimukte tad āpnuyāt |
atītaṃ vartamānaṃ ca jñānād ajñānato ‘pi vā ||
sarvaṃ tasya ca yat pāpaṃ kṣetraṃ dṛṣṭvā vinaśyati |
śāntair dāntais tapas taptaṃ yat kiṃcid dharmasaṃjñitam ||
sarvaṃ caitad avāpnoti avimukte jitendriyaḥ |
avimuktaṃ samāsādya liṅgam arcayate naraḥ ||
[kalpakoṭiśataiś cāpi nāsti tasya punarbhavaḥ |
amarā hy akriyāś caiva krīḍanti bhavasaṃnidhau ||
kṣetraṃ tīrthopaniṣadam avimuktaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
“tīrthopaniṣadam” tīrthānāṃ rahasyam etad ity arthaḥ |
avimukte mahādevam arcayanti stuvanti vai ||
sarvapāpavimuktās tu te tiṣṭhanty ajarāmarāḥ |
sarvakāmasya ye yajñāḥ punarāvartakāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
avimukte mṛtā ye ca sarve te hy anivartakāḥ |
grahanakṣatratārāṇāṃ kālena patanād bhayam ||
avimukte mṛtānāṃ tu patanaṃ naiva vidyae | ]
[30]        kalpakoṭisahasrais tu kalpakoṭiśatair api ||
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttir ye mṛtāḥ kṣetra uttame |
saṃsārasāgare ghore bhramantaḥ kālaparyayāt ||
avimuktaṃ samāsādya gacchanti paramāṃ gatim |
jñātvā kaliyuge ghoraṃ hāhābhūtam acetanam ||
avimuktaṃ na muñcanti kṛtārthās te narā bhuvi |
avimuktaṃ praviṣṭas tu yadi gacchet tataḥ punaḥ ||
tadā hasanti bhūtāni anyonyakaratāḍanaiḥ |
kāmakrodhena lobhena grastā ye bhuvi mānavāḥ ||
niṣkramante narā devi daṇḍanāyakamohitāḥ |
tathā |
uddeśamātraṃ kathitā avimuktaguṇās tathā |
samudrasyeva ratnānām avimuktasya vistaraḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
brahmovāca |
avimuktaṃ ca bhavataḥ sthānaṃ yad vai purātanam |
kapālamocanaṃ tīrthaṃ tatra tatra bhaviṣyati ||
ahaṃ ca tvaṃ sthitas tatra viṣṇuś cāpi gamiṣyati |
darśaṇād bhavatas tatra mahāpātakinaś ca ye ||
te ‘pi bhogaṃ samaśnanti viśuddhā bhavane mama |
varaṇā cāpy asiś caiva dve nadyau suravallabhe ||
antarāle tayoḥ kṣetraṃ dharaṇyāṃ na viśet kvacit |
[31]        tīrthānāṃ prathamaṃ tīrthaṃ kṣetrāṇāṃ prathamaṃ tava |
ā dehapatanād ye tu kṣetraṃ sevanti mānavāḥ ||
te mṛtā haṃsayānena divyaṃ yānty akutobhayāḥ |
pañcakrośapramāṇaṃ tu kṣetraṃ dattaṃ mayā tava ||
kṣetramadhye yadā gaṅgā gamiṣyati saritpatim |
tena sā mahatī puṇyā purī rudra bhaviṣyati ||
puṇyā codaṅmukhī gaṅgā prācī caiva sarasvatī |
udaṅmukhī yojane dve gacchate jāhnavī nadī ||
tatra te vibudhāḥ sarve mayā saha sabāndhavāḥ |
tatraiva vastum eṣyanti kapālaṃ tatra mocaya ||
tasmimś tīrthe tu me gatvā piṇḍadānena vai pitṝn |
śrāddhais tu prīṇayiṣyanti teṣāṃ loko ‘kṣayo divi ||
vārāṇasyāṃ mahātīrthe naraḥ snātvā vimucyate |
[saptajanmakṛtāt pāpād gamanād eva mucyate ||]
tat tīrthaṃ sarvatīrthānām uttamaṃ parikīrtitam |
tyajanti tatra ye prāṇān prāṇinaḥ saṃyatās tathā ||
rudravat te samāsādya modante bhavatā saha |
tatra cāṇv api yad dattaṃ dānaṃ rudraratātmanām ||
mahāphalaṃ ca tat teṣāṃ kurute bhāvitātmanām |
khaṇḍasphuṭitasaṃskāraṃ tatra kurvanti ye narāḥ ||
te rudralokam āsādya modante sukhinaḥ sadā |
tatra pūjā japo homaḥ kṛto bhavati dehinām ||
anantaphaladāḥ sarve rudrabhaktisamanvitaiḥ |
tatra dīpapradānena jñānacakṣuratīndriyam ||
[prāpnoti dhūpadānena sthānaṃ rudraniṣevitam |
suśvetaṃ taruṇaṃ saumyaṃ rūpavat surabhīyutam ||
[32]        paśvāṃkicā (?) mocayati sa yāti paramāṃ gatim |]
pitṛbhiḥ sahito mokṣaṃ gacchaty atra na saṃśayaḥ ||
atha kiṃ bahunoktena yad dānaṃ kriyate naraiḥ |
dharmakāmārtham uddiśya tad anantaphalaṃ bhavet ||
svargāpavargayor hetus tad dhi tīrthatamaṃ bhuvi |
snānaṃ japyaṃ tathā homaṃ hy anantaphalasādhanam ||
gatvā vārāṇasītīrthe yas tu cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret |
yas tatra pañcatāṃ yāti mokṣaṃ yāti na saṃśayaḥ ||
vasavaḥ pitaro jñeyā rudrāś caiva pitāmahāḥ |
ādityās tu tathā teṣāṃ vihitāḥ prapitāmahāḥ ||
trividhapiṇḍadānāya vidhir ukto mayānagha |
manuṣyaiḥ piṇḍadānādi kāryam atrāgataiḥ sadā ||
piṇḍadānaṃ ca tatraiva suputraiḥ kāryam ādarāt |
suputrāś ca pitṝṇāṃ tu bhavanti sukhadāyakāḥ ||
āditīrthaṃ bhava mayā darśanāt tava muktidam |
snātas tu salile tatra mucyate bhavabandhanāt ||
vimukto brahmahatyāyās tiṣṭha rudra yathāsukham |
avimukte mayā datte tiṣtha tvaṃ bhāryayā saha ||
liṅgapurāṇe |
īśvara uvāca |
anyac ca te pravakṣyāmi upāyajñānasādhanam |
yāni tīrthāni coktāni vyomatantre purā mayā ||
teṣām adhyadhikaṃ tīrtham avimuktaṃ mahāmune |
[33]        sarvatīrthāni ca mayā tasmin sthāne pratiṣṭhitāḥ ||
na kadācin mayā muktaṃ sthānaṃ ca satataṃ mune |
sarvatīrthamayaṃ puṇyaṃ guhyād guhyataraṃ mahat ||
sthānānāṃ caiva sarveṣām ādibhūtaṃ maheśvaram |
yatra siddhiṃ parāṃ prāptā munayo munisattama ||
anenaiva śarīreṇa prāptā nirvāṇam uttamam |
tatra caiva tu saṃbhūto jñānaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
tathā |
gaccha vārāṇasīṃ śīghraṃ yatra devaḥ sanātanaḥ |
devatābhiḥ samastābhis tatra devaḥ pinākadhṛk ||
stūyate varado devair brahmādibhir abhīkṣṇaśaḥ |
tatrāsir varaṇā caiva nimnage siddhasevite ||
bahujanmāptapāpānāṃ duṣṭānāṃ dehināṃ bhuvi |
kṣālanaṃ kurute devi sā nadī yatra jāhnavī ||
[yā dṛśā sarvathā svarge sā nadīnāṃ saridvarā |
yā mātā sarvabhūtānāṃ sā gaṅgā yatra nimnagā || ]
“dṛśā” dṛṣṭiḥ |
avimuktaṃ paraṃ kṣetraṃ śaṅkarasya sadaiva hi |
tatra sthānaṃ prasiddhaṃ ca trailokye śūlapāṇinaḥ ||
nimnagābhyāṃ purī sā ca nāmnā vārāṇasī mune |
kṛtasnānena devena oṃkāre saṃsthitena vā ||
[34]        tasmin kāle varo datto devadevena śambhunā |
devadeva uvāca |
ye smariṣyanti tat sthānam avimuktaṃ sadā narāḥ |
nirdhūtasarvapāpās te bhaviṣyanti gaṇopamāḥ ||
āgamiṣyanti ye draṣṭuṃ ye janā yajanena tu |
te brahmahatyāṃ mokṣyanti bhaviṣyanti mamānugāḥ ||
viditvā bhaṅguraṃ lokaṃ ye ‘smin vatsyanti me pure |
antakāle ‘pi vatsyanti teṣāṃ bhavati mokṣadam ||
mokṣaḥ sudurlabho yasmāt saṃsāraś cātibhīṣaṇaḥ |
aśmanā caraṇau bhittvā vārāṇasyāṃ vasen naraḥ ||
sarvāvastho ‘pi yo martyo vārāṇasyāṃ vaset sadā |
sa yāṃ gatim avāpnoti puṇyadānair na sā gatiḥ ||
durlabhā tapasā sā ca martyānāṃ munisattama |
tatra vipra vraja śīghraṃ manaḥsthairyaṃ yadīcchasi ||
manasaḥ sthairyahetutvaṃ śṛṇuṣva gadato mama |
dakṣiṇaṃ cottaraṃ caiva tasmin sthāne sthitaṃ sadā ||
viṣuvaṃ caiva madhyasthaṃ devānāṃ api durlabham |
kalau yuge tu martyānāṃ sthānaṃ mokṣāvahetukam ||
bhaktim ārādhanenaiva snānapūjanatarpaṇaiḥ |
cāturvarṇyavibhāgasya śarīraṃ vaiśvaraṃ padam ||
piṅgalā nāma yā nāḍī āgreyī sā prakīrtitā |
śuṣkā saric ca sā jñeyā lolārko yatra tiṣṭhati ||
iḍānāmnī ca yā nāḍī sā saumyā saṃprakīrtitā |
varaṇā nāma sā jñeyā keśavo yatra saṃsthitaḥ ||
[35]        ābhyāṃ madhye tu yā nāḍī suṣumnā ca prakīrtitā |
matsyodarī ca sā jñeyā viṣuvaṃ tat prakīrtitam ||
tathā |
śrutvā kaliyugaṃ ghoram alpāyuṣam adhārmikam |
siddhakṣetraṃ na sevante jāyante ca mriyanti ca ||
liṅgarūpadharās tīrthe dṛgicaṇḍeśvarādayaḥ |
avimukte sthitāḥ sarve śudhyante pāpakarmiṇaḥ ||
tathā |
avimuktaṃ paraṃ kṣetram avimukte parā gatiḥ |
avimukte parā siddhir avimukte paraṃ padam ||
antakāle manuṣyāṇāṃ bhidyamāneṣu marmasu |
vāyunā preryamāṇānāṃ smṛtir naivopajāyate ||
ye ‘vimukte sthitā rudrā bhaktānāṃ prītidāyakāḥ |
karṇajāpaṃ prayacchanti dṛgicaṇḍeśvarādayaḥ ||
tathā |
avimuktaṃ mahat kṣetraṃ puṇyakṛdbhir niṣevitam |
sarvapāpakṣayakaraṃ sākṣāc chivapuraṃ mahat ||
śmaśānaṃ paraṃ viddhi kṣetrāṇāṃ paramaṃ tathā |
pāpmānam utsṛjaty āśu praviṣṭas tatra vai pumān ||
[36] tathā |
vārāṇasyāṃ tu yaḥ kaścit praviṣṭo brahmaghātakaḥ |
tiṣṭhate kṣetrabāhye tu nirgate gṛhyate punaḥ ||
“tiṣṭhate” brahmahatyeti śeṣaḥ |
tathā |
liṅgarūpadharā mūrtā saptakoṭyas tu sarvataḥ |
avimukte sthitā rudrā bhaktānāṃ siddhidāyakāḥ ||
skandapurāṇe |
tām uvāca suraśreṣṭhas tadā devīṃ girīndrajām |
madīyaṃ vratam āsthāya bhaktimadbhir dvijottamaiḥ ||
yair yair yoga ihābhyastas teṣām ekena janmanā |
kṣetrasyāsya prabhāveṇa bhaktyā ca mama bhāvataḥ ||
anugraho mayā hy evaṃ kriyate muktidaḥ sadā |
tasmād idaṃ mahat kṣetraṃ brahmādyaiḥ sevyate mama ||
śrutimadbhiś ca viprendraiḥ saṃsiddhaiś ca tapasvibhiḥ |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ pakṣayor ubhayor api ||
śaśibhānūparāgeṣu kārtikyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
sarvaparvasu puṇyeṣu viṣuveṣv ayaneṣ ca ||
pṛthivyāḥ sarvatīrthāni vārāṇasyāṃ tu jāhnavī |
uttarapravahāṃ puṇyāṃ mama maulivinirgatām ||
pitus te girirājasya śubhāṃ himavataḥ sutām |
bhajante sarvato ‘bhyetya tāṃ śṛṇuṣva varānane ||
saṃnihatya kurukṣetraṃ sārdhaṃ tīrthaśatais tathā |
[37]        naimiśaṃ puṣkaraṃ caiva prayāgaṃ sapṛthūdakam ||
saṃdhyā saptaṛxaṃ caiva sarvā nadyaḥ sarāṃsi ca |
samudrāḥ sapta caivātra devatīrthāni kṛtsnaśaḥ ||
bhāgīrathīṃ sameṣyanti sarvaparvasu kāśigām |
avimukteśvaraṃ māṃ ca kāśistham acalātmaje ||
pṛthivyāṃ yāti tīrthāni puṇyāny āyatanāni ca |
praviśanti sadābhyetya puṇye ‘smin sarvavarvasu ||
kedāre caiva yal liṅgaṃ yac ca liṅgaṃ mahālaye |
madhyameśvarasaṃjñaṃ ca tathā paśupatiśvaram ||
śaṅkukarṇeśvaraṃ caiva gokarṇau ca tathā ubhau |
dṛgicaṇḍeśvaraṃ caiva bhadreśvaram athaiva ca ||
sthāneśvaram athaikāmraṃ kāmeśvaram ajeśvaram |
bhairaveśvaram īśānaṃ tathā kāyāvarohaṇe ||
yāni cānyāni puṇyāni sthānāni mama bhūtale |
tāni sarvāṇy anekāni kāśīpuryāṃ viśanti mām ||
sarvaparvasu puṇyeṣu guhyaṃ caitad udāhṛtam |
teneha labhyate jantor vipannasyāmṛtaṃ padam ||
snātasya caiva gaṅgāyāṃ dṛṣṭena ca mayā śubhe |
sarvayajñaphalais tulyam iṣṭaiḥ śatasahasraśaḥ ||
sadya evam avāpnoti kiṃ tataḥ param uttamam |
sarvāyatanamukhyānāṃ divi bhūmau giriṣv api ||
nātaḥ parataraṃ devi buddhyasvāstīti kṛtsnaśaḥ |
[38]        brahmārkavaiśvānaracakracandrair
jalendravittādhipavāyubhiś ca |
gandharvayakṣoragasiddhasaṅghaiḥ
sārdhaṃ sadā sevitam etad agryam ||
snānaṃ mamedaṃ himaśailaputri
guhyaṃ sadā kṣetram idaṃ supuṇyam |
vimokṣasiddhiṃ ca phalaṃ hi tattvaṃ
siddhāḥ prabuddhā munayo vadanti ||
kṣetre ‘smin nivasanti ye sukṛtino bhaktāḥ sadā mānavāḥ
paśyanto dvayam ādareṇa śucayaḥ santaḥ sadā matparāḥ |
te martyā bhayaduḥkhapāparahitāḥ saṃśuddhakarmakriyā
bhittvā saṃbhavabandhajālagahanaṃ vindanti mokṣaṃ param ||
evam etat suvistīrṇaṃ nānāvanalatākulam |
jāhnavyālaṃkṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ kṣetraṃ priyatamaṃ mama ||
bhāgīrathīm ihāsādya vārāṇasyāṃ mamāspade |
aśvamedhaśataṃ prāpya brahmalokaṃ ca vindati ||
nātaḥ puṇyataraṃ devi jāto guhyatamaṃ kvacit |
nātaḥ śubhataraṃ kiṃcin nātaḥ parataraṃ mama ||
kṣetraṃ mamedaṃ surasiddhajuṣṭaṃ
saṃprāpya martyaḥ sukṛtaprabhāvāt |
khyāto bhavet sarvasurāsurāṇāṃ
mṛtaś ca yāyāt paramaṃ padaṃ ca ||
[39] tathā |
brahmaghnagoghnagurutalpagabhinnavṛtti-
nyāsāpahārikuhakādiniṣiddhavṛttiḥ |
saṃsārabhūtadṛḍhapāśavimuktadeho
vārāṇasīṃ bhavapurīṃ samupaiti lokaḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
varaṇā vāpy asiś caiva dve nadyau suravallabhe |
antarāle tayoḥ kṣetraṃ dharaṇyāṃ na viśet kvacit ||
matsyapurāṇe |
dviyojanam athārdhaṃ ca tat kṣetraṃ paścime smṛtam |
ardhayojanavistīrṇaṃ dakṣiṇe ‘ntarataḥ smṛtam ||
varaṇā ca nadī yāvad yāvac chuṣkanadī bhavet |
eṣa kṣetrasya vistāraḥ prokto devena dhīmatā ||
tathā |
dviyojanaṃ tu tat kṣetraṃ pūrvapaścimataḥ smṛtam |
ardhayojanavistīrṇaṃ dakṣiṇe ‘ntarataḥ sthitam ||
varaṇā ca nadī yāvad yāvac chuṣkanadī tathā |
bhīṣmacaṇḍīkam ārabhya parvateśvaram antike ||
brahmapurāṇe |
pañcakrośapramāṇaṃ tu kṣetraṃ dattaṃ mayā tava |
[40]        yatra codaṅmukhī gaṅgā prācī vāpi sarasvatī ||
udaṅmukhī yojane dve gacchate jāhnavī nadī |
skandapurāṇe |
catuḥkrośaṃ caturdikṣu kṣetram etat prakīrtitam |
yojanaṃ viddhi cārvaṅgi mṛtyukāle ‘mṛtapradam ||
liṅgapurāṇe |
kṛttivāsasam ārabhya krośaṃ krośaṃ caturdiśam |
yojanaṃ tatra tat kṣetraṃ gaṇai rudraiś ca saṃvṛtam ||
tasya madhye yadā liṅgaṃ bhūmiṃ bhittvā samutthitam |
madhyameśvaranāmākhyaṃ khyātaṃ sarvasurāsuraiḥ ||
asmād ārabhya liṅgāt tu krośaṃ krośaṃ caturṣv api |
yojanaṃ viddhi tat kṣetraṃ mṛtyukāle ‘mṛtapradam ||
evaṃ kṣetrasya saṃnyāsaḥ purāṇe prikīrtitaḥ |
asmāt tu parato devi vihāro naiva vidyate ||
tathā liṅgapurāṇe |
devi uvāca |
vārāṇasyāṃ tu kiṃ guhyaṃ sthānaṃ hi ca tava priye |
kiṃ rahasyaṃ ca liṅgānāṃ ke krādas tatra viśrutāḥ ||
ke kūpāḥ kāni kuṇḍāni liṅgānāṃ sthāpakāś ca ke |
kasmin sthāne kṛtaṃ karma jñānaniṣṭhaṃ prajāyate ||
[41]        etad ācakṣva me sarvaṃ yady anugrahabhāgaham |
devadeva uvāca |
ruciraṃ sthānam āsādya avimuktaṃ tu me gṛham |
na kadācin mayā muktam avivuktaṃ tataḥ smṛtam ||
anenaiva prakāreṇa avimuktaṃ tu kathyate |
aviśattena pāpaṃ tu kathyate vedavādibhiḥ ||
tena pāpena tat kṣetraṃ varjitaṃ varavarṇini |
siddhāḥ pāśupatāḥ śreṣṭhās tanniṣṭhās tatparāyaṇāḥ ||
upāsate ca māṃ nityaṃ tasmin sthāne sthitāḥ sadā |
pūrvottare digvibhāge tasmin kṣetre tu sundari ||
surāsuraiḥ stutaś cāhaṃ tatra sthāne yaśasvini |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu stuto ‘haṃ vividhaiḥ stavaiḥ ||
utpannaṃ mama liṅgaṃ tu bhittvā bhūmiṃ yaśasvini |
teṣām anugrahārthāya lokānāṃ bhaktibhāvataḥ ||
vārāṇasyāṃ mahādevi tatra sthāne sthito hy aham |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā manujo devi paśupāśair vimucyate ||
kūpas tatraiva saṃllagno mahādevasya caiva hi |
tatropasparśanād devi labhed vāgīśvarīṃ gatim ||
tatra vārāṇasī devī sthitā vigraharūpiṇī |
[42]        mānavānāṃ hitārthāya sthitā kūpasya paścime ||
vārāṇasīṃ tu yo dṛṣṭvā bhaktyā caiva namasyati |
tasya tuṣṭā ca sā devī vasatiṃ ca prayacchati ||
mahādevasya pūrveṇa goprekṣam iti viśrutam |
tena dṛṣṭena suśroṇi pūrvoktaṃ phalam āpnuyāt ||
“paśupāśaiḥ” saṃsārabandhaiḥ | “upasparśanāt” snānāt | “vāgīśvarīgatiḥ” sārasvatalokaprāptiḥ | “pūrvoktaṃ phalam” paśupāśachedalakṣaṇam |
skandapurāṇe |
goprekṣeśvaramāhātmyaṃ dṛṣṭvābhyarcya ca mānavaḥ |
na durgatim avāpnoti kalmaṣaiś ca vimucyate ||
liṅgapurāṇe |
īśvara uvāca |
goprekṣasyottareṇātha anasūyākhyaliṅgakam |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi gatiṃ ca labhate parām ||
paścānmukhaṃ ca tal liṅgam anasūyāpratiṣṭhitam |
anasūyeśvarasyāgre gaṇeśvaram iti smṛtam ||
tena dṛṣṭena labhate gaṇeśasya salokatām |
[43]        gaṇeśvarāt paścimena hiraṇyakaśipuḥ purā ||
sthāpayāmāsa me liṅgaṃ kūpasyaiva samīpataḥ |
tasyaiva paścime devi liṅgaṃ siddheśvaraṃ smṛtam ||
darśanād eva me liṅgaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
anyad āyatanaṃ bhadre śṛṇuṣva gadato mama ||
vṛṣabheśvaranāmānaṃ liṅgaṃ tatraiva tiṣṭhati |
pūrvāmukhaṃ maheśāni goprekṣasya tu nairṛte ||
tena dṛṣṭena suśroṇi abhīṣṭaṃ phalam āpnuyāt |
na dānair na tapobhiś ca na yajñair nāpi vidyayā |
prāpyate gatir iṣṭā hi yāvimukte tu labhyate ||
tathā |
goprekṣasya dakṣiṇataḥ sthāpitaṃ liṅgam uttamam |
dadhīceśvaranāmānaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam ||
dadhīceśvarasāmīpye dakṣiṇe varavarṇini |
atriṇā sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ daivam ārtiharaṃ śūbham ||
atrīśvarād dakṣiṇataḥ sūryakhaṇḍamukhe ‘pi ca |
madhukaiṭabhābhyāṃ śuśroṇi liṅgasaṃsthāpanaṃ kṛtam ||
tatra paścānmukho devi visamanthāḥ (?) prapaṭhyate |
pūrvāmukhaṃ kaiṭabhasya liṅgaṃ trailokyaviśrutam ||
goprekṣakasya pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ vai bālakeśvaram |
bālakeśvarasāmīpye vijvareśvarasaṃjñitam ||
[44]        tena dṛṣṭena suśroṇi jvaro naśyati tatkṣaṇāt |
vijvareśvarapūrveṇa deveśvaram iti śrutam ||
īśānābhimukhaṃ liṅgaṃ koṇe tasya mukhāni vai |
tena dṛṣṭena suśroṇi caturvedo bhaved dvijaḥ ||
vedeśvarasyottarataḥ svayaṃ tiṣṭhati keśavaḥ |
kṣetrasya kāraṇaṃ cāsya kṣetrajña iti cocyate ||
tena dṛṣṭena suśroṇi sarvaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ carācaram |
tatsamīpe tu suśroṇi liṅgaṃ me saṃgameśvaram ||
tena dṛṣṭena suśroṇi śiṣṭaiḥ saha samāgamaḥ |
skandapurāṇe |
nadī vārāṇasī ceyaṃ puṇyā pāpapramocanī |
kṣetram etad alaṃkṛtya jāhnavyā saha saṃgatā ||
sthāpitaṃ saṃgame cāsmin brahmaṇā liṅgam uttamam |
saṃgameśvaranāmānaṃ khyātaṃ jayati dṛśyatām ||
saṃgame devanadyāś ca yaḥ snātvā manujaḥ śuciḥ |
arcayet saṃgameśaṃ tu tasya janmabhayaṃ kutaḥ ||
liṅgapurāṇe |
[45]        saṃgameśasya pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ caiva caturmukham |
brahmaṇā sthāpitaṃ bhadre prayāgam iti kīrtyate ||
tena dṛṣṭena labhate brahmaṇaḥ padam uttamam |
tatra sā sāṅkarī devī brahmavṛkṣe ‘vatiṣṭhate ||
śāntiṃ karoti sarveṣāṃ ye ca tīrthanivāsinaḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ tu saṃvedyaṃ gaṅgāvaraṇasaṃgamam ||
śravaṇadvādaśīyogo budhavāre yadā bhavet |
tadā tasmin naraḥ snātvā sannihatyāphalaṃ labhet ||
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā tu yas tatra tasmin kāle yaśasvini |
tārayitvā pitṝn sarvān viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati ||
matsyapurāṇe |
vārāṇasījāhnavībhyāṃ saṃgame lokaviśrute |
datvānnaṃ ca vidhānena sa bhūyo nābhijāyate ||
liṅgapurāṇe |
varaṇāyās taṭe pūrve kumbhīśvaram iti smṛtam |
kumbhīśvarāt tu pūrveṇa kāleśvaram iti smṛtam ||
kāleśvarasyottarato mahātīrthaṃ varānane |
kapilāhradanāmānaṃ khyātaṃ sarvasurāsuraiḥ ||
tasmin hrade tu yaḥ snānaṃ kuryād bhaktiparāyaṇaḥ |
vṛṣadhvajaṃ ca vai dṛṣṭvā rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet |
narakasthās tato devi pitaraḥ sapitāmahāḥ |
[46]        pitṛlokaṃ prāpnuvanti tasmin śrāddhe kṛte tu vai ||
gayāyāṃ cāṣṭaguṇitaṃ puṇyaṃ proktaṃ maharṣibhiḥ |
tasmin śrāddhe kṛte bhadre pitṝṇām anṛṇo bhavet ||
skandapurāṇe |
kapilāhradatīrthe ‘smin snātvā saṃyatamānasah |
vṛṣadhvajam imaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet ||
liṅgapurāṇe |
paścime tu diśābhāge mahādevasya bhāmini |
skandena sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ mama bhaktyā sureśvari ||
tena dṛṣṭena gacchanti skandasyaiva salokatām |
tatra śākhair viśākhaiś ca naigamīyaiś ca sundari ||
sthāpitāni ca liṅgāni gaṇaiḥ sarvair bahūni ca |
“śākhāviśākhānaigamīyāḥ” gaṇaviśeṣāḥ |
skandeśvarasyottarato balabhadrapratiṣṭhitam |
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi anantaphalam āpnuyāt ||
skandeśvarād dakṣiṇato mahāliṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhitam |
paścimābhimukhaṃ liṅgaṃ sthāpitaṃ nandinā purā ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā manujo devi nandilokam avāpnuyāt |
nandīśvarāt paścimato liṅgaṃ paścān mukhaṃ sthitam ||
svarlīnasadṛśaṃ bhadre nandipitrā pratiṣṭhitam (?) |
śilākṣeśvaranāmānaṃ surasaṅghaiḥ prapūjitam ||
[47]        anyat tatra tu vikhyātaṃ hiraṇyākṣeśvaraṃ vibhum |
hiraṇyākṣeṇa daityena sthāpitaṃ mama bhaktitaḥ ||
hiraṇyākhyasya sāmīpye anyair devaiḥ sahasraśaḥ |
sthāpitāni ca liṅgāni bhaktyā caiva phalārthibhiḥ ||
anyad vai devadevasya sthitaṃ paścānmukhaṃ smṛtam |
tatra sthāne varārohe hiraṇyākṣasya dakṣiṇe ||
teṣāṃ paścimadigbhāge aṭṭahāsaṃ sthitaṃ śubham |
mukhaṃ liṅgaṃ tu tad devi paścimābhimukhaṃ sthitam ||
prasannavadane devi sarvapātakanāśakam |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi aiśānaṃ lokam āpnuyāt ||
aṭṭahāsasamīpena paścimena yaśasvini |
mitrāvaruṇanāmānau pūrvadvāre vyavasthitau ||
mitrāvaruṇalokas tu tayoḥ saṃdarśanād bhavet |
anyat tatraiva vikhyātaṃ vasiṣṭheśam iti sthitam ||
sthāpitaṃ tatra talliṅgaṃ yājñavlkyena vai purā |
caturmukhaṃ ca talliṅgaṃ sarvapāpakṣayakaram |
anyat tatraiva saṃlagnaṃ maitreyyā sthāpitaṃ śubham |
[48]        tena dṛṣṭena labhate paraṃ jñānaṃ sudurlabham |
yājñavalkyeśvarasyāpi paścime paścimānanam ||
prahrādeśvaranāmānam advaitaphaladāyakam |
prahrādeśvarāt purataḥ svayaṃlīnaṃ tu tiṣṭhati ||
svarlīneśvaranāmānaṃ sumahāphaladāyakam |
jñānavijñānaniṣṭhānāṃ paramānandam icchatām ||
yā gatir vihitā teṣāṃ svarlīne tu mṛtasya ca |
svarlīnāt putato liṅgaṃ sthitaṃ pūrvamukhaṃ śubham ||
vairocaneśvaraṃ nāma sthāpitaṃ daityasūnunā |
tasya caivottare devi liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ smṛtam ||
balinā sthāpitaṃ tat tu śivālokaparāyaṇam |
anyac caitat sthiraṃ liṅgaṃ bāṇeśvara iti sthitam ||
rākṣasī tu mahābhīmā nāmnā śālakaṭaṃkatā |
tayā ca sthāpitaṃ bhadre tasya cottarataḥ śubham ||
anyad āyatanaṃ puṇyaṃ tasmin sthāne yaśasvini |
hiraṇyagarbhaṃ vikhyātaṃ puṇyaṃ tasyāpi darśanam ||
mokṣeśvaraṃ tu tatraiva svargeśvaram ataḥ param |
etau dṛṣṭvā sureśāni svargaṃ mokṣaṃ ca vindati ||
vāsukīśvaranāmānaṃ tayoś cottarataḥ śubham |
caturmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam ||
tasyaiva pūrvakhaṇḍe tu vāsukes tīrtham uttamam |
tatra snāto varārohe rogair naivābhibhūyate ||
[49]        tasyaiva ca samīpe tu candreṇa sthāpitaṃ śubham |
candreśvarasya pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ vidyeśvaraṃ śubham ||
labhed vaidyādharaṃ lokaṃ tasya liṅgasya darśanam |
iti liṅgapurāṇe tṛtīyo ‘dyāyaḥ |
[tathā] |
devy uvāca |
kathaṃ vīreśvaro deva etad icchāmi veditum |
kathayasva prasādena devadeva maheśvara ||
īśvara uvāca |
iha āsīt purā rājā niyuktir nāma viśrutaḥ |
tasya bhāryā mahādevi arajā nāma viśrutā ||
ekaḥ putras tayā jātaḥ kālena bahunā tadā |
pāde dvitīye saṃbhūte mūlanakṣatrasaṃjñake ||
mantribhiś ca tadā devi uktā tatreśabhāminī |
jāto ‘yaṃ dārako devi pāpanakṣatrasaṃbhavaḥ ||
tasmāt tyājyas tu bālo ‘yaṃ rājñā caiva hitārthinā |
evam uktā tu sā devi mantribhir hitakāmyayā ||
dhyātvā cādhomukhī dīnā pratipede maheśvarīm |
provācedaṃ tadā dhātrīṃ bālaṃ gṛhṇīṣva mā ciram ||
svarlīnasyottare pārśve mātṛbhyaś ca samarpitam |
“rakṣatām iti bālo ‘yaṃ mama putra” iti bravīt ||
rājñyās tu vacanaṃ sarvaṃ kṛtaṃ dhātṛkayā tadā |
mātṝṇāṃ hi tadā bālaṃ nikṣeptum upacakrame ||
kadācitkālaparyāye mātṛbhiḥ paricintitam |
asmākaṃ putratāṃ prāpta eṣa bālo na saṃśayaḥ ||
[50]        asmābhir gantum ārabdhaṃ khecarīcakram uttamam |
brahmāṇī cābravīd devi yogapīṭhaṃ tu nīyatām ||
yogapīṭhena dṛṣṭena bālo rājyakṣamo bhavet |
sarvābhir mātṛbhiś cātha tadvākyam abhinanditam ||
nīto vidyādharaṃ lokaṃ yogapīṭhaṃ ca darśitam |
āśvāsito mātṛgaṇaiḥ spṛṣṭaḥ tatra sa bālakaḥ ||
kathyatāṃ pūrvavṛttāntaḥ putra bālakumāraka |
kasya tvaṃ pūrṇacandrābha kathaṃ prāpto ‘si no gṛham ||
evam uktas tadā bālo na kiṃcid pratyabhāṣata |
pañcamudrovāca |
yathā rājyakṣamo bālas tathā tvaṃ kartum arhasi |
evaṃ śrutvā tu tat sarvā mātaro ‘bhimukhābhavan ||
evaṃ bhaviṣyatīty uktvā tuṣṭo vai khecarīgaṇaḥ |
gaccha putra svayaṃ rājyaṃ pālayasva yathāsukham ||
bālena prārthitāḥ sarvāḥ prajākāmena sundari |
yadāhaṃ bhavitā corvyāṃ sarvalokeṣu pārthivaḥ ||
avatāras tadā karyo madbhaktyā parayā tadā |
evaṃ vai prārthitāḥ sarvā mātaro lokamātaraḥ ||
avaterur yathāyogaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm |
pañcamudrā tu bālaṃ tam anayan nagaraṃ punaḥ ||
āgatya ca yathāyogam ardharātre vyavasthitam |
avaterus tadā hṛṣṭāḥ pañcamudrā vimātaraḥ ||
bālena pūjitāḥ sarvāḥ pratiṣṭhāpya yathāvidhi |
pūjāṃ gṛhītvā bālasya ākāśaṃ tu punar gatāḥ ||
[51]        adyāpi dṛśyate vyomni mātṝṇāṃ gaṇamaṇḍalam |
nirīkṣyate puṇyakarmā uttarābhimukhaṃ sthitam ||
yad etad dṛśyate vyomni mātṝṇāṃ tu samīpataḥ |
ākāśaliṅgam ity uktam ayaṃ svarlīna ucyate ||
yathākāśe tathā bhūmau evaṃ sarvatra dṛśyate |
evam ālokya taṃ sarvaṃ gamane mātṛmaṇḍalam ||
mātṝṇāṃ tu prabhāveṇa naro bhavati siddhibhāk |
tataḥ prabhṛti deveśi asmin kṣetre vyavasthitā ||
vipadbhiyāgatā yasmād vikaṭā procyate budhaiḥ |
bālo vīratvam āpanno matprasādād yaśasvini ||
bālena cāpy ahaṃ devi asmin deśe sukhoṣitaḥ |
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe guhyāyatanavarṇane caturtho ‘dhyāyaḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
vāyavye tu diśābhāge tasya pīṭhasya sundari |
sagareṇa purā devi tasmin deśe pratiṣṭhitam ||
caturmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
tasyaivottarapūrveṇa nāmnā bālīśvaraṃ śubham ||
bālinā sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ kapinā sumahātmanā |
taṃ ḍṛṣṭvā mānavo devi tiryagyoniṃ na gacchati ||
tasya cottaradigbhāge sugrīvasya mahātmanaḥ |
liṅgaṃ tasya śubhaṃ bhadre sarvakilbiṣanāśanam ||
tathā hanumatātraiva sthāpitaṃ liṅgam uttamam |
[52]        sagarāt paścimenaiva liṅgaṃ tatra pratiṣṭhitam ||
mama bhaktyā ca suśroṇi aśvibhyāṃ parameśvari |
tasyaivottarapārśve tu bhadradoham iti smṛtam ||
gavāṃ kṣīreṇa saṃjātaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam |
kapilānāṃ sahasrasya samyag dattasya yat phalam ||
tat phalaṃ labhate martyaḥ snātas tatra na saṃśayaḥ |
pūrvabhādrapadāyuktā paurṇamāsī yadā bhavet ||
tadā puṇyatamaḥ kālo hy aśvamedhaphalapradaḥ |
hradasya paścime tīre bhadreśvaram iti sthitam ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo bhadre golokaṃ labhate dhruvam |
bhadreśvarasya digbhāge nirṛte tu yaśasvini ||
upaśāntaśivaṃ nāma khyātaṃ sarvasurāsuraiḥ |
upaśāntasya devasya uttare varavarṇini ||
cakreśvaram iti khyātaṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtam |
paścimābhimukhaṃ devi hradas tasyaiva cāgrataḥ ||
tasmin hrade naraḥ snātvā [pūjayitvā] maheśvaram |
śivalokam avāpnoti bhāvitenāntarātmanā ||
tasya paścimadigbhāge śūleśvaram iti sthitam |
śūlayantraṃ purā nyastaṃ snānārthaṃ varavarṇini ||
hradas tatra samutpanno devadevasya cāgrataḥ |
snānaṃ kṛtvā hrade tasmin dṛṣṭvā śūleśvaraṃ prabhum ||
rudralokam avāpnoti tyaktvā saṃsārasāgaram |
śūleśvarasya pūrveṇa anyad āyatanaṃ śubham ||
taptaṃ tatra tapas tīvraṃ nāradena surarṣiṇā ||
[53]        sthāpitaṃ mama liṅgaṃ tu kuṇḍasya purataḥ śubham |
tasmin kuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā vai nāradeśvaram ||
saṃsāramāyā yā ghorā tāṃ taren nātra saṃśayaḥ |
nāradeśasya pūrveṇa nāmnā dharmeśvaraṃ śubham ||
sthāpitaṃ mama liṅgaṃ tu kuṇḍasya purataḥ śubhe |
vāyavye tu diśābhāge tasya devasya sundari ||
vināyakam iti khyātaṃ kuṇḍaṃ tatra subhodakam |
tasmin kuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā caiva vināyakam ||
sarvavighnavinirmukto hy asmin kṣetre vasec ciram |
vināyakasya saṃlagna uttareṇa yaśasvini ||
hradas tatra suvikhyāto ‘marako nāma nāmataḥ |
dakṣiṇena tu kuṇḍasya mukhaliṅgaṃ tu tiṣṭhati ||
tasmin kuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā cāmarakeśvaram |
ajñānāc caiva yat kiṃcid iha kṣetre tu yat kṛtam ||
vilayaṃ yāti tat sarvaṃ dṛṣṭvā tal liṅgam uttamam |
tasya cottaradigbhāge nātidūre yaśasvini ||
varaṇāyās taṭe śuddhe liṅgaṃ tatraiva saṃsthitam |
varaṇeśvaraṃ tu vikhyātaṃ paścimābhimukhaṃ sthitam ||
tasmin pāśupataḥ siddha aśvapādo yaśasvini |
anenaiva śarīreṇa śāśvatīṃ siddhim āgataḥ ||
[54]        mamāpi tatra sāṃnidhyaṃ tasmiṃl liṅte yaśasvini |
tena dṛṣṭena suśroṇi gandharvatvaṃ ca vindati ||
tasya paścimadigbhāge nāmnā śaileśvaraṃ śubham |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi pūrvoktaṃ labhate phalam ||
dakṣiṇe cāpi tasyaiva koṭīśvaram iti sthitam |
yatra sā dṛśyate devi viśrutā bhīṣmacaṇḍikā ||
koṭitīrtheṣu yaḥ snātvā koṭīśvaram athārcayet |
gavāṃ koṭipradānena yat phalaṃ labhate naraḥ ||
tat phalaṃ sakalaṃ tasya snānenaikena sundari |
koṭīśvarasya pūrveṇa ṛṣisaṅghaiḥ pratiṣṭhitam ||
tena liṅgena dṛṣṭena dṛṣṭaṃ syāt sacarācaram |
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe guhyāyatanavarṇane pañcamo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
koṭīśvarasya devasya āgneyyāṃ diśi saṃsthitaḥ |
śmaśānastambhasaṃjñeti vikhyātaḥ supratiṣṭhitaḥ ||
mānavās tatra pātyante iha yai duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam |
yatra stambhe sadā devi ahaṃ tiṣṭhāmi bhāmini ||
tatra gatvā tu yaḥ pūjāṃ mama devi kariṣyati |
sarvapāpavinirmukto gacchec ca paramāṃ gatim ||
[55]        anyac ca te pravakṣyāmi mahātīrthaṃ yaśasvini |
kapālamocanaṃ nāma triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam ||
kapālaṃ patitaṃ tatra snātasya mama sundari |
tasmin snāto varārohe brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati ||
kapāleśvaranāmānaṃ tasmiṃs tīrthe vyavasthitam |
aśvamedham avāpnoti darśanāt tasya sundari ||
tasyaiva cottare pārśve tīrthaṃ trailokyaviśrutam |
tatra snātvā varārohe ṛṇair mukto bhaven naraḥ ||
ṛṇamocanakaṃ nāmnā vikhyātaṃ bhuvi sundari |
trīṇi liṅgāni tiṣṭhanti tatraiva mama sundari ||
tāni dṛṣṭvā tu suśroṇi naśyati trividham ṛṇam |
dakṣiṇe tu diśābhāge tasya tīrthasya sundari ||
aṅgāreśvaranāmānaṃ mukhaliṅgaṃ vyavasthitam |
paścimābhimukhaṃ devi kuṇḍasya purataḥ sthitam ||
aṅgāreṇa yadā yogaś caturthyām aṣṭamīṣu vā |
tīrthe tasmin naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā vai maṅgaleśvaram ||
vyādhibhiś ca vinirmukto yatra tatrābhijāyate |
tasyaiva ca samīpastham uttareṇa yaśasvini ||
liṅgaṃ tu sumahat puṇyaṃ viśvakarmapratiṣṭhitam |
paścimābhimukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvajñatvam avāpnuyām ||
budheśvaraṃ tu tatraiva dṛṣṭvā bhaktyā dṛḍhavrataḥ |
sarvān kāmān avāpnoti dṛṣṭvā devaṃ budheśvaram ||
[56]        budheśvarād dakṣiṇato liṅgaṃ caiva caturmukham |
mahāmuṇḍeśvaraṃ nāma sarvasiddhipradāyakam ||
tasya devasya purataḥ kūpas tiṣṭhati vai śubhaḥ |
tasya kūpasya sā devī upariṣṭāt sthitā śubhā ||
snānārthaṃ tatra sā kṣiptā mālā muṇḍamayī mayā |
tena saṃprocyate devi mahāmuṇḍeti mānavaiḥ ||
khaṭvāṅgaṃ tatra vai kṣipraṃ snānārthaṃ varavarṇini |
khaṭvāṅgeśvaranāmnā tu sthitaṃ tatraiva suvrate ||
bhuvaneśvaranāmnā tu liṅgaṃ devi phalapradam |
uttarābhimukhaṃ liṅgaṃ kuṇḍād vai dakṣiṇe taṭe ||
tasmin kuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā vai bhuvaneśvaram |
na durgatim avāpnoti kalmaṣaiś ca vimucyate ||
dakṣiṇe bhuvaneśasya kuṇḍam anyac ca tiṣṭhati |
nāmnā vimalam īśaṃ ca liṅgaṃ tasyaiva pūrvataḥ ||
vaimalyaṃ tu narā yānti tasya liṅgasya darśanāt |
tatra snātvā varārohe modate divi daivataiḥ ||
tasmin pāśupataḥ siddhas tryambako nāma vai muniḥ |
anenaiva śarīreṇa rudralokam avāpnuyāt ||
tasyāṅgārakakuṇḍasya paścimena yaśasvini |
mahad āyatanaṃ puṇyaṃ bhṛguṇā sthāpitaṃ purā ||
[57]         yas tad āyatanaṃ dṛṣṭvā arcitaṃ stutipūrvakam |
śivalokāc ca te puṇyān na cyavanti kadācana ||
dakṣiṇena tu tasyaiva anyad āyatanaṃ śubham |
nandīśeśvaranāmānaṃ devānām api durlabham ||
tasya darśanamātreṇa vrataṃ pāśupataṃ labhet |
tatra siddho mahātmā vai kapilarṣir mahātapāḥ ||
trikālam ārcayad devaṃ guhāśāyī yatātmavān |
evaṃ varṣasahasreṇa tasya tuṣṭo ‘smy ahaṃ priye ||
mama devi prasādena sāṅkhyavettā mahāyaśāḥ |
kapileśvarasyādhastād guhā tatraiva saṃsthitā ||
tāṃ guhāṃ vīkṣate yo vai na sa pāpena lipyate |
devy uvāca |
kapiśeśvaraṃ kathaṃ devam oṃkāreśvarasaṃjñitam |
kathayasva prasādena devadevaṃ maheśvaram ||
īśvara uvāca |
trīṇi liṅgāni guhyāni vārāṇasyāṃ mama priye |
yeṣāṃ caiva tu sāṃnidhyaṃ mama caiva sureśvari ||
evaṃ cānyaprakāreṇa brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ |
kramān mātrā samuddiṣṭā nandīśasya tu sundari ||
akāre ca sthito viṣṇuḥ pañcāyatanasaṃsthitaḥ |
ukāro brahmaṇo rūpaṃ tasya dakṣiṇataḥ priye ||
nandīśreśvaranāmāham uttareṇa vyavasthitaḥ |
taṃ ca devi tad oṃkāraṃ mama rūpaṃ sureśvari ||
[58]        mānavānāṃ hitārthāya tatra sthāne sthito hy aham |
matsyodaryās tu kūle ‘ham uttare cottare priye ||
nandīśeśvaranāmāham uttareṇa vyavasthitaḥ |
nandīśaṃ paramaṃ brahma nadīśaṃ paramā gatiḥ ||
nandīśaṃ paramaṃ sthānaṃ duḥkhasaṃsāramocanam |
aprakāśyam idaṃ kānte tava snehāt prakāśitam ||
anyathā gopanīyaṃ tu mama bhaktivivarjite |
yuge saptadaśe devi kṛtvā caikāṃ vasundharām ||
saṃhāraṃ tu tapaḥ kṛtvā asmin deśe samāgataḥ |
oṃkāramūrtim āsthāya tribhedena sthito hy aham ||
sarveṣām eva siddhānāṃ tat sthānaṃ parikīrtitam |
tasmiṃl liṅgaṃ śivaḥ sākṣāt svayam eva vyavasthitaḥ ||
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu taṃ devaṃ siddhasaṅghaiḥ prapūjitam |
oṃkāreśvaranāmānaṃ devānām api durlabham ||
vāmadevas tu sāvarṇir aghoraḥ kapilas tathā |
tatra siddhiṃ parāṃ prāptā yoge pāśupate sthitāḥ ||
anye ca ṛṣayo devā yakṣagandharvaguhyakāḥ |
yuge yuge gamiṣyanti tasmin sthāne sthitaḥ sadā ||
divyā hi sā parā mūrtiḥ kapileśvarasaṃjñitā |
kadācid asya devasya darśane jāhnavī priye ||
matsyodarīṃ samāyāti tatra snānaṃ tu mokṣadam |
ārādhya kapileśaṃ tu trailokyapālanakṣamāḥ ||
bhavanti puruṣā devi mama nityaṃ ca vallabhāḥ |
oṃkāraṃ tat paraṃ brahma sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ sthitam ||
rudralokasya tad dvāraṃ rahasyaṃ parikīrtitam |
kapileśvarasyādhastād dakṣiṇe varavarṇini ||
[59]        matsyodarīṃ sameṣyanti tīrthāni saha sāgaraiḥ |
ṣaṣṭikoṭisahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭiśatāni ca ||
pakṣe pakṣe sameṣyanti caturdaśy aṣṭamīṣu ca |
matsyodarīṃ yadā gaṅgā paścime kapileśvare ||
samāyāti mahādevi sa ca yogaḥ sudurlabhaḥ |
tasmin snānaṃ mahābhāge aśvamedhasahasradam ||
tasya liṅgasya māhātmyaṃ kapileśasya kīrtitam |
na kasyacid adeyaṃ (?) gopanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ ||
tatraiva akṣaraṃ brahma nādeyaṃ parikīrtitam |
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe kapileśvaramāhātmye oṃkāranirṇayo nāma ṣaṣṭho ‘dhyāyaḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
tatra sthāne tu ye siddhās tān pravakṣyāmy ahaṃ punaḥ |
mahāpāśupatā śreṣṭhā mama putrā mahaujasaḥ ||
ananyamanasaḥ śuddhāḥ sevito ‘haṃ purā sadā |
śītātapavinirmuktaṃ prāsādair upaśobhitam ||
kailāsapṛṣṭhe devasya yādṛg devi gṛhaṃ śūbham |
tad abhyadhikarūpaṃ tu kṛtvā devasya mandiram ||
sevyate siddhatulyais tu sarvasiddhānukampibhiḥ |
tadā siddhir anuprāptā nirvāṇāyā gatiḥ purā ||
kapileśvarasya caivāgre liṅgaṃ paścān mukhaṃ smṛtam |
uddālaka ṛṣis tatra siddhiṃ paramikāṃ gataḥ ||
anyat paścānmukhaṃ liṅgaṃ sthitaṃ tatra tathottare |
tasmiṃl liṅge tu saṃsiddhaḥ pārāśaryo mahāmuniḥ ||
[60]        anyat tatraiva saṃlagne sthitaṃ paścānmukhaṃ śubham |
tasminn āyatane siddho mahājñānī hi bāṣkaliḥ ||
tasyaiva tu samīpasthaṃ sthitaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ priye |
tatra pāśupataḥ siddho bhāvavṛttas tu vai muniḥ ||
tasyaiva paścime devi mukhaliṅgaṃ tu tiṣṭhati |
tatra siddhiṃ prāṃ prāpta āruṇir nāma nāmataḥ ||
paścime aruṇīśasya anyal liṅgaṃ tu tiṣṭhati |
asmin pāśupatācāryo yogasiddho mahāmuniḥ ||
anyat tatraiva saṃlagnaṃ dakṣiṇe liṅgam uttamam |
tatra siddhiṃ gato devi kaustubho nāma vai ṛṣiḥ ||
tasya dakṣiṇapārśve tu liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam |
mahāpāśupataḥ siddhaḥ sāvarṇis tatra vai muniḥ ||
tasyāgre tu mahal liṅgaṃ sthitaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ śubham |
asmiṃl liṅge śivaḥ sākṣāt svayam eva vyavasthitaḥ ||
oṃkāramūrtim āsthāya sthito ‘haṃ tatra suvrate |
catvāro munayaḥ siddhās tasmiṃl liṅge yaśasvini ||
vāmadevas tu sāvarṇir aghoraḥ kapilas tathā |
tasmiṃl liṅge tu saṃsiddhā nandiśasya prabhāvataḥ ||
tasy devasya cādhastād guhā siddhais tu vanditā |
śrīmukhī nāma sā jñeyā yogasiddhais tu sevitā ||
tatra pāśupatāḥ śreṣṭhā mama liṅgārcane ratāḥ |
teṣāṃ caiva nivāsārthaṃ sā guhā nirmitā mayā ||
tasya dvāre tu suśroṇi siddha aghoro mahāmuniḥ |
anenaiva śarīreṇa rudratvaṃ gatavān muniḥ ||
[61]        tatra gatvā trirātraṃ tu kṣapayed ekamānasaḥ |
naro vā yadi vā nārī saṃsāraṃ na viśet punaḥ ||
aghoreśvaradevasya cottare kūpam uttamam |
tasyopasparśanād devi vājapeyaṃ ca vindati ||
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe guhyāyatanavarṇanaṃ nāma saptamo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
anyad āyatanaṃ vakṣye vārāṇasyāṃ sureśvari |
yatra sākṣāt svayaṃ bhadre ramamāṇaṃ tu sarvadā ||
matsyodarītaṭe ramye surasiddhanamaskṛte |
rocate me sadā vāsas tasminn āyatane śubhe ||
sthānānām eva sarveṣām atiramyaṃ mama priyam |
yatra pāśupatā devi mama liṅgārcane ratāḥ ||
mama putrās tu te sarve brahmacaryeṇa saṃyutāḥ |
śāntā dāntā jitakrodhā siddhās tatra na saṃśayaḥ ||
lobhādiviṣayāsakto narakāc ca nivartate |
mama liṅgāni puṇyāni pūjayanti sadātra ye ||
teṣāṃ madhye tu tatraiva liṅgaṃ vai paścimāmukham |
śrīkaṇṭhanāmavikhyātaṃ kapileśvaradakṣiṇe ||
tasmin pāśupataḥ siddhaḥ kratudhvaja iti smṛtaḥ |
mama caiva prasādena yogaiśvaryam avāpnuyāt ||
tasyaiva cāgrato bhadre liṅgaṃ pūrvamukhaṃ sthitam |
tasmiṃl liṅge tu jābālaḥ siddhiṃ paramikāṃ gataḥ ||
aparaṃ caiva liṅgaṃ tu tasya dakṣiṇataḥ sthitam |
oṃkāreśvaranāmānaṃ devānāṃ api durlabham ||
[62]        tatra siddhiṃ parāṃ prāpto muniḥ kālikavṛkṣiyaḥ |
sarveṣām eva siddhānām uttamottamasaṃsthitaḥ ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe bhadre liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
tasmiṃl liṅge tu saṃsiddho gārgyaś ca sumahātapāḥ ||
paścāyatanam etaṃ te mayā ca kathitaṃ śubhe |
na kasyacin mayākhyātaṃ rahasyaṃ paramādbhutam ||
pañcabrahmeti vikhyātam etad adyāpi sundari |
etasmāt kāraṇād devi pañcāyatanam ucyate ||
caturāśramiṇāṃ puṇyaṃ yat phalaṃ pratipaṭhyate |
tat phalaṃ saphalaṃ proktaṃ pañcāyatanadarśanāt ||
idaṃ pāśupataṃ śreṣṭhaṃ madīyavratacāriṇām |
yogināṃ mokṣalipsūnāṃ saṃsārabhayanāśanam ||
narāṇām alpabuddhīnāṃ pāpopahatacetasām |
bheṣajaṃ paramaṃ proktaṃ pañcāyatanam uttamam ||
tasmād yatnaṃ sadā kuryāt pañcāyatanadarśane |
pañcāyatanasāmīpye kūpas tiṣṭhati sundari ||
tasmin kūpa upaspṛśya dīkṣāphalam avāpnuyāt |
tasmin dakṣiṇadigbhāge rudravāsaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
rudrasyottarapārśve tu pañcāyatanadakṣiṇe |
tatra kuṇḍaṃ mahat proktaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam ||
tasmin kuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā abhīṣṭaṃ phalam āpnuyāt |
caturdaśyāṃ yadā yoga ārdrānakṣatrasaṃyutaḥ ||
tadā puṇyatamaḥ kālas tasmin snāne mahāphalam |
tasmiṃs tīrthe naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā rudraṃ ca bhāmini ||
yatra tatra mṛto devi rudralokaṃ tu gacchati |
pūrvāmukhasthitas cāhaṃ tasmiṃl liṅge maheśvari ||
[63]        rudrāṇāṃ koṭijapyena yat phalaṃ pratipadyate |
tat phalaṃ labhate bhadre tasya liṅgasya darśanāt ||
rudrasya ca samīpe tu ṛṣibhiḥ sthāpitāni ca |
liṅgāni mama susroṇi sarvakāmaphalāni ca ||
rudrasya nairṛte bhāge mahālayam iti smṛtam |
darśāc ca padaṃ tasya mahābhāgyasya sundari ||
tatra sthāne śubhe ramye svayaṃ tiṣṭhati pārvatī |
tasyaiva cāgrato devi kūpas tiṣṭhati nirmalam ||
pitaras tatra tiṣṭhanti ye divyā ye ca mānuṣāḥ |
tasmin kūpa upaspṛśya jalaṃ saṃgṛhya bhāmini ||
piṇḍas tatra pradātavyo mama devi padaspṛhaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ tatra prakurvīta annādyenodakena ca ||
piṇḍaḥ kūpe tu tatraiva prakṣeptavyaḥ subhānane |
evaṃ kṛtvā tu yas tasmiṃs tīrtha rudramahālaye ||
ekaviṃśakulopeto rudralokaṃ sa gacchati |
tatra vaitaraṇī nām dīrghikā paścimāmukhī ||
tasyāṃ snātvā varārohe narakaṃ na ca paśyati |
khaṇḍasphuṭitasaṃskāraṃ yas tatra kurute śūbhe ||
rudrloko ‘kṣayas tasya sarvakālaṃ yaśasvini |
mahālayasyottareṇa liṅgāni sumahānti ca ||
devaiḥ sarvair mahābhāgaiḥ sthāpitāni śubhārthibhiḥ |
paścime tu diśābhāge rudrakuṇḍasya bhāmini ||
liṅgaṃ tatra sthitaṃ śubhraṃ devārcāya pratiṣṭhitam |
bṛhaspatīśvaraṃ nāma sarvaduḥkhavināśanam ||
[64]        pitṛbhiḥ sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ taṭe kūpasya dakṣiṇe |
tena pūjitamātreṇa pitaras tṛptim āpnuyuḥ ||
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe guhyāyatanavarṇane ‘ṣṭamo ‘dhyāhaḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
anyad āyatanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kālipuryāṃ sureśvari |
dakṣiṇena sthitaṃ devaṃ rudravāsasya sundari ||
kāmeśvaram iti khyātaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam |
taptaṃ tatra tapas tīvraṃ kāmadevena vai purā ||
kuṇdaṃ tad ubhayaṃ devi padmotpalasamanvitam |
kuṇḍasyaiva taṭe ramye uttame varavarṇini ||
liṅgaṃ tatra sthitaṃ divyaṃ paścimābhimukhaṃ priye |
gandhadhūpanamaskārair mukhavādyaiś ca sarvaśaḥ ||
yo mām arcayate tatra tasya tuṣyāmy ahaṃ [sadā] |
tuṣṭe tu mayi. deveśi sarvān kāmāṃl labheta saḥ ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vai tasminn anye ‘pi surapuṅgavāḥ |
ārādhayanto māṃ tasmiṃs tīrthaṃ vaktuṃ mahātapāḥ ||
yasya yasya yadā kāmas tatra taṃ taṃ dadāmy aham |
dadāmi sarvakāmāṃś ca darmaṃ mokṣaṃ tathaiva ca ||
tasmād anye ‘pi ye kecit tīrthe tasmin janāḥ sthitāḥ |
ārādhayanto deveśaṃ kāmeśaṃ caiva sarvadā ||
so yasy manasaḥ kāmaḥ taṃ tam āpnoti niścitam |
kāmeśvarasamīpe tu dakṣiṇe varavarṇini ||
tatra snātvā varārohe rudrasyānucaro bhavet |
caitre māsi site pakṣe trayodaśyāṃ tu mānavaḥ ||
[65]        snānaṃ ye ca prakurvanti te kāmasadṛśā narāḥ |
kāmeśvaraṃ sadā liṅgaṃ yo ‘rcayatīha mānavaḥ ||
labhed vidyādharaṃ lokam evam eva na saṃśayaḥ |
kāmeśvarasya pūrveṇa nāmnā pañcālakeśvaram ||
dhanadasya tu putreṇa pūjito ‘haṃ sureśvari |
kṣetraṃ mama priyaṃ jñātvā tasmin deśe vyavsthitaḥ ||
ārādhayati māṃ nityaṃ mama pūjārataḥ sadā |
pañcāleśvaranāmāhaṃ tasmin deśe vyavasthitaḥ ||
narāṇāṃ dhanadānaṃ tu kariṣyāmi yaśasvini |
tatra pūrvamukhaṃ devi mukhaliṅgaṃ tu tiṣṭhati ||
pañcakeśvaranāmāhaṃ tatra devi pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
kūpas tasyaiva cāgre tu pāvanaḥ sarvadehinām ||
tasmin sthāne sthitā devi aghoreśeti nāmataḥ |
mānavānāṃ hitārthāya svayaṃ tatra vyavasthitā ||
nava liṅgāni guhyāni sthāpitāni tu kinnaraiḥ |
pañcakeśvarapūrveṇa divākaraniśākarau ||
liṅgāni tāni puṇyāni sarvapāpaharāṇi ca |
dakṣiṇena tu tasyaiva andhakeśeti nāmataḥ ||
tatra liṅgaṃ mahat puṇyam andhakena pratiṣṭhitam |
mama caiva prasādena gato ‘sau paramāṃ gatim ||
paścime tu diśābhāge tasya devasya sundari |
nāmnā deveśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ kāmakuṇḍasya dakṣiṇe ||
[66]        aham eva sadā bhadre tasmin sthāne vyavasthitaḥ |
bhīṣmeśvaraṃ tu tatraiva siddheśvaram ataḥ param ||
gaṅgeśvaraṃ tu tatraiva yamuneśvaram eva ca |
manḍaleśvaraṃ tu tatraiva ūrvaśīliṅgam uttaram ||
anyāni tatra liṅgāni sthāpitāni mahātmabhiḥ |
tāni drṣṭvā tu manujaḥ sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet ||
maṇḍaleśvarasāmīpye mukhaliṅgaṃ ca tiṣṭhati |
śāntena sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham ||
vāyavye tu diśābhāge droṇeśvarsamīpataḥ |
vālakhilyeśvaraṃ nāma sukhadaṃ sarvadehinām ||
tac ca paścānmukhaṃ liṅgaṃ kāmakuṇḍasya paścime |
vālakhilyeśvaraṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet ||
tasyaiva cāgrato bhadre mukhaliṅgaṃ ca tiṣṭhati |
vālmīkeśvaranāmānaṃ taṃ ca dṛṣṭvā na śocati ||
tasyaiva kāmakuṇḍasya purā saṃsthāpitaṃ taṭe |
liṅgaṃ tatra mahāpuṇyaṃ cyavanena pratiṣṭhitam ||
tasya darśanamātreṇa jñānavān jāyate naraḥ |
vālakhilyeśvarasyaiva dakṣiṇe varavarṇini ||
nāmnā vāteśvaraṃ devaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi vāyulokaṃ ca gacchati ||
agnīśvaraṃ tu tatraiva bharateśaṃ tathaiva ca |
varuṇeśaṃ tathā caiva sarvasiddhipradāyakam ||
etān dṛṣṭvā mahādevi yatheṣṭāṃ gatim āpnuyāt |
anyad āyatanaṃ puṇyaṃ sanakena pratiṣṭhitam ||
[67]        sanakeśvaranāmānaṃ sarvasiddhāmarārcitam |
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi rajasūyaphalaṃ labhet ||
dharmeśvaraṃ tu tatraiva dakṣiṇe varavarṇini |
nāmnā dharmeśvaraṃ devaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam ||
anyat tatraiva liṅgaṃ tu ṛṣibhiḥ sthāpitaṃ purā |
sanakeśvarasyottarato nāmnā guruḍakeśvaram ||
siddhikāmena suśroṇi sthāpitaṃ garuḍena tu |
garudeśvarasya purataḥ sthāpitaṃ brahmasūnunā ||
bhaktyā sanatkumāreṇa sthāpito ‘haṃ varānane |
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi jñānavān jāyate naraḥ ||
tasyaiva cottare pārśve sanandena pratiṣṭhitam |
tasya darśanamātreṇa prāpyate siddhir uttamā ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe pārśve sthāpitaṃ hy āsurīśvaram |
tathaiva pañcaśikhinā sthāpitaṃ ca māhātmanā ||
tasya dakṣiṇāpārśve tu nātidūre vyavasthitam |
śanaiścareṇa tatraiva mukhaliṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||
śanaiśvareśvaraṃ nāma sarvalokanamaskṛtam |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi rogair naivābhibhūyate ||
anyac caiva mahāpuṇyaṃ kāśīpuryāṃ mahāśaye |
mārkaṇḍeyas tu vikhyāto mama caiva sadā priyaḥ ||
tasya liṅgasya cāgre tu paścimena yaśasvini |
mārkaṇdeyahrado nāma triṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ ||
[68]        mārkaṇḍeyahrade snātvā kiṃ bhūyaḥ pariśocati |
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ śrāddhaṃ ca pitṛtarpaṇam ||
tat sarvam akṣayaṃ tatra bhavatīti na saṃśayaḥ |
tasmin kuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā caiva caturmukham ||
rudralokaḥ sadā tasya punarāvṛttidurlabhaḥ |
mārkaṇḍeśvarasāmīpye uttareṇa yaśasvini ||
kūpo vāi tiṣṭhate tatra sarvatīrthavaro ‘naghe |
kūpasya cottareṇaiva kuṇḍamadhye yaśasvini ||
kuṇḍeśvaram iti khyātaṃ sarvasiddhais tu vanditam |
dīkṣāṃ pāśupatīṃ tīrtvā dvādaśākṣareṇa yat phalam ||
tat phalaṃ labhate devi brahmaṇas tu na saṃśayaḥ |
kuṇḍasya paścime tīre liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
skandena sthāpitaṃ devi brahmalokagatipradam |
mārkaṇḍeyasya pūrveṇa nātidūre vyavasthitam ||
śāṇḍilyeśvaranāmānaṃ sthitaṃ tatraiva sundari |
mukhaliṅgaṃ tu taṃ bhadre paścimābhimukhaṃ sthitam ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi paśupāśaiḥ pramucyate |
asyaiva dakṣiṇe pārśve nāmnā bhadreśvaraṃ smṛtam ||
tatra paścān mukhaṃ liṅgaṃ sthāpitaṃ ca brahmarṣibhiḥ |
tena dṛṣṭena suśroṇi brāhmaṇyaṃ labhate naraḥ ||
anyac caiva mahādevi pravakṣymy anupūrvaśaḥ |
yo vai pūrvaṃ mayā tubhyaṃ kapālīśaḥ pravartitaḥ ||
[69]        tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge liṅgāni kathayāmy aham |
tatra devī svayaṃ devī śrīr vai tiṣṭhati sarvadā ||
śrīkuṇḍam iti vikhyātaṃ tatra kuṇḍe varānane |
tasmin kuṇḍeśvarī devī varadā sarvadehinām ||
tatra kuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā devīṃ mahāśriyam |
śriyā na rahitaḥ so vai yatra tatrābhijāyate ||
śriyaś cottarapārśve tu kapālīśasya dakṣiṇe |
tatra liṅgaṃ mahābhāge mahālakṣmyā pratiṣṭhitam ||
pūrvābhimukho ‘haṃ tasmin kuṇḍasyaiva tu dakṣiṇe |
snātvā kuṇḍe tu vai devi tal liṅgaṃ hy arcayiṣyati ||
naro vā yadi vā nārī tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu |
cāmarāsaktahastābhiḥ strībhiḥ parivṛtaḥ sadā ||
tiṣṭhate suvimānastho yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam |
iha loke yadā yāti lakṣmīvān rūpasaṃyutaḥ ||
dhanadānyasamāyuktaḥ kule mahati jāyate |
svargalokasya tad dvāraṃ rahasyaṃ devanirmitam ||
yadā matsyodarīṃ yānti devalokād divaukasaḥ |
tadā tenaiva mārgeṇa strībhiḥ parivṛtaḥ sukham ||
tena sā procyate devi mahāśrīr varavarṇini |
etat tubhyaṃ mayā devi rahasyaṃ parikīrtitam ||
tasya viṣṇudhruvasyaiva paścimāyā diśaḥ sthitam |
sthāpitaṃ mama liṅgaṃ tu dadhīcena maharṣiṇā ||
[70]        dadhīceśvaranāmānaṃ khyātaṃ sarvasurāsuraiḥ |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā manujo devi aiśvaraṃ lokam āpnuyāt ||
dakṣiṇe tu tadā tatra gāyatryā sthāpitaṃ purā |
gāyatryā dakṣiṇe caiva sāvitryā sthāpitaṃ punaḥ ||
etau paścānmukhau liṅgau mama devi priyau sadā |
asya caiva tu pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam ||
matsyodarītaṭe ramye sthitaṃ satpatayeśvaram |
tena dṛṣṭena suśroṇi uttamāṃ siddhiṃ āpnuyāt ||
lakṣmīliṅgasya devena liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
ugreśvare mahat puṇyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam ||
tena dṛṣṭena suśroṇi bhavej jātismaro naraḥ |
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe devi mahat kuṇḍaṃ vyavasthitam ||
snātvā kanakhale yadvat puṇyam uktaṃ yaśasvini |
tasmin kuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā phalam āpnoti tatsamam ||
dadhīceśāt paścimato nāmnā tu dhanadeśvaram |
yatra devi tapas taptaṃ dhanadena mahātmanā ||
tatra kuṇḍaṃ mahādevi dhanadeśasya dhīmataḥ |
tatra snātvā naro devi dhanadeśaṃ ca paśyati ||
tasya tuṣṭaḥ kuberas tu devatvaṃ saṃprayacchati |
anyāni tatra liṅgāni sthāpitāni surāsuraiḥ ||
tāni dṛṣṭvātipuṇyāni svargalokaṃ vrajen naraḥ |
dhanadeśāt paścimato nāmnā tu karavīrakam ||
[71]        tena dṛṣṭena deveśi siddhiṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ |
puṇyāni tatra liṅgāni sthitāni parameśvari ||
tasya vāyavyakoṇe tu liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
mārīceśvaranāmānaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam ||
tasya caivāgrato devi sthāpitaṃ kuṇḍam uttamam |
tatra snātvā naro bhaktyā bhrājate bhāskaro yathā ||
mārīceśāt paścimato liṅgam indreśvaraṃ mahat |
paścimābhimukhaṃ devi kuṇḍasya taṭasaṃsthitam ||
indreśvarād dakṣiṇato vāpī karkoṭakasya ca |
tatra vīrajale snātvā dṛṣṭvā karkoṭakeśvaram ||
nāgānāṃ cādhipatyaṃ tu jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
karkoṭakād dakṣiṇato nātidūre vyavasthitam ||
dṛmicaṇḍeśvaraṃ nām brahmahatāpahārakam |
tatra pāśupataḥ siddhaḥ kauthumir nāma nāmataḥ ||
jñānaṃ pāśupataṃ prāpya rudralokam ito gataḥ |
paścimābhimukhaṃ liṅgaṃ kuṇḍasyottarataḥ sthitam ||
tatra kuṇḍe naraḥ sthitvā dṛmicaṇḍeśvarasya tu |
rudralokam avāpnoti tyaktvā saṃsārasāgaram ||
tasya pūrveṇa deveśi dīrghikāyās taṭe śubhe |
agnīśvaraṃ tu nāmānaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi agnilokaṃ tu gacchati |
tasyaiva pūrvadigbhāge nāmnā hy āmnātakeśvaram ||
[72]        taṃ dṛṣṭvā manujo bhadre rudrasyānucaro bhavet |
ekaliṅgaṃ tu tad vidyāt sūkṣmaṃ ca varavarṇini ||
tasyaivāmnātakeśasya dakṣiṇe nātidūrataḥ |
kuṇḍaṃ tadudbhavaṃ divyaṃ suralokapradāyakam ||
urvaśīśvaranāmānaṃ sthitaṃ paścānmukhaṃ bhuvi |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā manujo devi gaṇatvaṃ labhate dhruvam ||
kuṇḍasya nairṛte bhāge nātidūre kathaṃcana |
urvaśīśamamīpe tu tālakarṇeśvaraṃ smṛtam ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi caṇḍasyaiti salokatām |
tasyaiva tu samīpe tu liṅgāni sthāpitāni ca ||
gaṇais tu mama dharmajñaiḥ śreṣṭhāni sumahānti ca |
tasya pūrveṇa kūpas tu tiṣṭhate sumahān priye ||
tasmin kūpe jalaṃ spṛśya pūto bhavati mānavaḥ |
caṇḍeśvarasya pūrvaṃ tu sthitaṃ citreśvaraṃ śubham ||
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi citrasya samatāṃ vrajet |
citreśvarasamīpe tu sthitaṃ kāleśvaraṃ mahat ||
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi citrasya samatāṃ vrajet |
citreśvarasamīpe tu sthitaṃ kāleśvaraṃ mahat ||
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi kālaṃ vañcati mānavaḥ |
devy uvāca |
kathaṃ kāleśvaro devaḥ kena vā vañcitaḥ prabhuḥ |
kasmin sthāne tu kaḥ siddhas tan me brūhi sureśvara ||
[73]                īśvara uvāca |
tasmin sthāne purā bhadre piṅgākṣo nāma vai muniḥ |
jñānasya vaktā pañcārthe loke pāśupataḥ sthitaḥ ||
tena caiva purā bhadre liṅge ‘smin sa prasāditaḥ |
tato liṅgaprabhāveṇa kālaṃ vañcitavān muniḥ ||
nāntato dṛśyate kāla īśvarāsaktacetasaḥ |
tatra sthitvā tu sumahatkālaṃ yaḥ kālayet prajāḥ ||
na tasya kramituṃ śaktaḥ kālo vai ghorarūpiṇaḥ |
tataḥ prabhṛti ye ‘nye ‘pi tasminn āyatane sthitā ||
teṣāṃ nākramate kālaḥ varṣalakṣāyutair api |
anyac ca te pravakṣyāmi rahasyaṃ varavarṇini ||
tasya devasya cāgre tu kūpas tiṣṭhati vai śrutaḥ |
tatra kālodakaṃ nāma udakaṃ devi tiṣṭhati ||
tasyaiva prāśanād devi pūto bhavati mānavaḥ |
yais tu tatrodakaṃ pītaṃ naraiḥ strībhiś ca karmabhiḥ ||
svayaṃ devena śarveṇa triśūlāṅkena cāṅkitaḥ |
na teṣāṃ parivarto vai kalpakoṭiśatair api ||
yat pītvā bhavabandhotthabhayaṃ muñcanti mānavāḥ |
etad devi rahasyaṃ tu kālodakam udāhṛtam ||
[74]        darśanāt tasya devasya mahāpātakino ‘pi ye |
te ‘pi bhogān samaśnanti na teṣāṃ kramate bhavaḥ ||
tal liṅgaṃ sarvaliṅgānām uttamaṃ parikīrtitam |
darśanāt tasya liṅgasya rudratvaṃ yāti mānavaḥ ||
tatra vāpi hi yad dattaṃ dānaṃ rudraratātmanām |
tad vai mahāphalaṃ teṣāṃ yacchate bhāvitātmanām ||
khaṇḍasphuṭitasaṃskāraṃ tatra kurvanti ye narāḥ |
rudralokaṃ samāsādya modante sukhinaḥ sadā ||
siddhiliṅgāśramaṃ bhagnaṃ dṛṣṭvā rājñe nivedayet |
svato vā parato vāpi ye kurvanti yathā tathā ||
te bhogānāṃ narāḥ pātram ante mokṣasya bhājanāḥ |
mokṣapradāyinaḥ liṅgaṃ yatkāryārthasya lipsayā ||
rājapratigrahāsaktāḥ kṛtakān pūjayanti ye |
te rudraśāpanirdagdhāḥ patanti narake dhruvam ||
ye punaḥ siddhiliṅgānāṃ prāsādānāṃ svaśaktitaḥ |
kurvanti pūjāṃ satkāraṃ te muktā nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
kāleśvare tu yo devi naraḥ kārayate puram |
ekaviṃśakulopeto rudraloke vasec ciram ||
tatra pūjā japo homaḥ kāleśe kriyate hi yat |
tatra dīpapradānena jñānacakṣur bhaven naraḥ ||
prāpnoti dhūpadānena tat sthānaṃ rudrasevitam |
[75]        jāgaraṃ ye prakurvanti kāleśasyaiva cāgrataḥ ||
te mṛtā vṛṣabhārūḍhāḥ śūlahastās trilocanāḥ |
bhūtvā rudrasamā bhadre rudralokaṃ tu te gatāḥ ||
bahunātra kim uktena kāleśe devi yat kṛtam |
tat sarvam akṣayaṃ devi punar janmani janmani ||
etat te sarvam ākhyātaṃ bhūyo vistarato mayā |
na kasyacid ihākhyātaṃ gopanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ ||
kāleśvarasya devasya śivasyāyatanaṃ śubham |
kāleśvarasamīpe tu dakṣiṇe varavarṇini ||
mṛtyunā sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ sarvarogavināśanam |
kūpasya cottare bhāge mahāliṅgāni suvrate ||
ekaṃ dakṣeśvaraṃ nāma dvitīyaṃ kaśyapeśvaram |
paścānmukhaṃ tu yal liṅgaṃ tad dakṣeśvarasaṃjñakam ||
dakṣeśvarasya pūrveṇa mahākālas tu tiṣṭhati |
kuṇḍe snānaṃ naraḥ kṛtvā mahākālaṃ tu yo ‘rcayet ||
arcitaṃ tena suśroṇi jagad etac carācaram |
dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi tathā tasya kuṇḍasya vai taṭe ||
sthāpitaṃ devaliṅgaṃ tu antakena mahātmanā |
mahatphalam avāpnoti tasya liṅgasya darśanāt ||
antakeśvarasāmīpye liṅgaṃ vai dakṣiṇe sthitam |
śakreśvareti nāmānaṃ sthāpitaṃ śakrahastinā ||
[76]        tasyaiva dakṣiṇe bhāge mātalīśvaram uttamam |
saṃsthāpitaṃ mātalinā sarvasaukhyapradāyakam ||
devasya cāgrataḥ kuṇḍe tatra tīrthaṃ varānane |
hastipāleśvarasyāgre kuṇḍe tiṣṭhati bhāmini ||
taptaṃ yatra purā bhadre antakenāntakāriṇā |
hastīśvarasya pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam ||
vijayeśvaranāmānaṃ surasiddhais tu pūjitam |
mahākālasya kuṇḍaṃ tu uttare varavarṇini ||
vālinārādhitaś cāhaṃ tasmin sthāne tu pārvati |
balikuṇḍaṃ tu vikhyātaṃ vārāṇasyāṃ mama priyam ||
tasya kuṇḍasya pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ sthāpitavān baliḥ ||
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe guhyāyatanavarṇanaṃ nāma navamo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
tathā |
vidyāvighneśvarā rudrāḥ śivā ye ca prakīrtitāḥ |
kṛttivāseśvaro yatra tatra sarve vyavasthitāḥ ||
tasmin sthāne mahādaityo hastī bhūtvā mamāntikam |
tasya kṛttiṃ vidāryāśu kariṇaṃ svaṃ janaprabham ||
vāsaṃ tu kṛtavān pūrvaṃ kṛttivāsas tato hy aham |
avimukte sthitaś cāhaṃ tasmin sthāne mahāmune ||
[77]        liṅgaṃ dāruvaṇe guhyam ṛṣisaṅghais tu pūjitam |
paścimābhimukaś cāhaṃ tasminn āyatane sthitaḥ ||
antakeśvaraliṅgaṃ tu mama cāgre sthitaṃ śubham |
uttare mama liṅgaṃ tu sthāpitaṃ śakrahastinā ||
mātalīśvaraliṅgaṃ tu dakṣiṇena sthitaṃ mama |
mama pūrveṇa kūpas tu nānāsiddhisamanvitaḥ ||
aṇimādyās tathāṣṭau ca siddhayas tatra saṃsthitāḥ |
tathā |
ye te pāśupatās tatra madhyameśvarasaṃsthitāḥ |
teṣām anugrahārthaṃ ca kṛttivāsāḥ sthitaḥ purā ||
rudrāṇāṃ tu śarīraṃ tu madhyameśvaram īśvaram |
kṛttivāsāḥ śivaḥ prāhur etad guhyataraṃ mama ||
anye ca bahavaḥ siddhā ṛṣayas tatra saṃsthitāḥ |
upāsanti ca māṃ nityaṃ madbhāvagatamānasāḥ ||
vārāṇasyāṃ pramucyante ye janās tatra saṃsthitāḥ |
kṛmikīṭāḥ pramucyante mahāpātakinaś ca ye ||
smaraṇād vipra liṅgasya pāpaṃ vai bhasmasād bhavet |
tathā |
kṛttivāseśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ ye yajanti śubhānvitāḥ |
te rudrasya śarīre tu praviṣṭā apunarbhavāḥ ||
[78]        anenaiva śarīreṇa prāptā nirvāṇam uttamam |
bahūni tatra tīrthāni saṃkhyā kartuṃ na śakyate ||
daśakoṭisahasrāṇi tīrthāny atraiva vai mune |
kṛttivāseśvaro yatra tatra sarve vyavasthitāḥ ||
tasmiṃl liṅge tu sāṃnidhyaṃ trikālaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
brahmaviṣṇusurendrāṇām aprakāśyaṃ kṛtaṃ mayā ||
yatra tīrthāny anekāni kṛtāni bahubhir dvijaiḥ |
pulastyādyair mahābhāgair lomaśādyair mahātmabhiḥ ||
kṛttivāseśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ na jānanti surāsurāḥ |
bhṛgur uvāca |
kṛte tretādvāpare ca kalau ca parameśvaram |
mahāguhyātiguhyaṃ ca saṃsārārṇavatārakam ||
kena kāryeṇa deveśa tvayedaṃ na prakāśitam |
kṛttivāseśvaraṃ liṅgam avimukte tu saṃsthitam ||
īśvara uvāca |
daśakoṭisahasrāṇi āgacchanti dine dine |
dharmakriyāvinirmuktāḥ satyaśaucavivarjitāḥ ||
devadvijagurūn nityaṃ nindanto bhaktivarjitāḥ |
māyāmohasamāyuktā dambhamohasamanvitāḥ ||
[79]        śūdrānnaniratā viprā vihvalā ratilālasāḥ |
kṛttivāseśvaraṃ prāpya te sarve vigatajvarāḥ ||
saṃsārabhayanirmuktāḥ sarvapāpavivarjitāḥ |
sukhena mokṣam āyānti yathā sukṛtinas tathā ||
divyair vimānair ārūdhāḥ kiṅkiṇīravakānvitaiḥ |
devānāṃ bhuvanaṃ labhyaṃ te yānti paramaṃ padam ||
janmāntarasahasreṇa mokṣo labhyeta vā na vā |
ekena janmanā tatra kṛttivāse tu labhyate ||
pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tasya liṅgasya darśanāt |
tatra siddheśvaraṃ nāma mukhaliṅgaṃ tu saṃsthitam ||
antakeśvaradevasya sthitaṃ caivottareṇa tu |
ālayaṃ sarvasiddhānāṃ tat sthānaṃ paramaṃ mahat ||
avyayaṃ śāśvataṃ divyaṃ virajaṃ brahmaṇālayam |
śaktimūrtisthitaṃ śāntaṃ śivaṃ paramakāraṇam ||
avyaktaṃ śāśvataṃ sūkṣmaṃ sūkṣmāt sūkṣmataraṃ mahat |
tathā |
īśvara uvāca |
etad dāruvanasthānaṃ kalau devasya gīyate |
parasparaṃ tu yaj jñānaṃ mokṣamārgapradāyakam ||
prāpyate dvijaśārdūla kṛttivāse na saṃśayaḥ |
kṛte tu tryambakaṃ proktaṃ tretāyāṃ kṛttivāsasam ||
[80]        māheśvaraṃ tu devasya dvāpare nāma gīyate |
hastipāleśvaraṃ nāma kalau siddhais tu gīyate ||
daṇḍirūpadhareṇaiva devadevena śambhunā |
dvijeṣv anugrahaś cātra tatra sthāne kṛtaḥ purā ||
yuge yuge tu tattvajñā brāhmaṇāḥ śāntacetasaḥ |
upāsate ca māṃ nityaṃ japanti śatarudriyam ||
ā dehapatanād viprās tasmin kṣetra upāsakāḥ |
japanti rudrādhyāyaṃ te sa śivaḥ kṛttivāsasam ||
teṣāṃ devaḥ sadā tuṣṭo divyān lokān prayacchati |
ye te japtā mayā rudrāḥ śaṅkukarṇālaye purā ||
te ‘vimukte tu tiṣṭhanti kṛttivāse na saṃśayaḥ |
dvāraṃ yat sāṃkhyayogānāṃ sā teṣāṃ vasatiḥ smṛtā ||
tathā |
śyāmās tu puruṣā raudrā vaidyutā haripiṅgalāḥ |
aśarīrāḥ śarīrā ye te ca sṛṣṭā mayā purā ||
nīlakaṇṭhāḥ śvetamukhā bimboṣṭhāś ca kapardinaḥ |
haritkeśāḥ śṛṅgiṇaś ca lamboṣṭhās tigmahetayaḥ ||
asaṃkhyāḥ parisaṃkhyātās tathānye ca sahasraśaḥ |
te ‘vimukte tu tiṣṭhanti kṛttivāsasamīpataḥ ||
rudrāṇāṃ tu śivo jñeyaṃ kṛttivāseśvaraṃ param |
tena taiḥ preritā yānti duṣprāpam akṛtātmabhiḥ ||
[81]        aśāśvatam idaṃ jñātvā mānuṣyaṃ bahukilbiṣam |
avimukte tu vastavyaṃ japtavyaṃ śatarudriyam ||
kṛttivāseśvaro devo draṣṭavyaś ca punaḥ punaḥ |
yadīcchet tārakaṃ jñānaṃ śāśvataṃ cāmṛtapradam ||
etat sarvaṃ prakartavyaṃ yadīcchen māmakaṃ padam |
tathā |
gajavaktraḥ svayaṃbhūtas tiṣṭhaty atra vināyakaḥ |
kūṣmāṇḍarājaśambhuś ca jayantaś ca madotkaṭaḥ ||
siṃhavyāghramukhāḥ kecid vikaṭāḥ kubjavāmanāḥ |
yatra nandī mahākālaḥ citraghaṇṭo maheśvaraḥ ||
dṛmicaṇḍeśvaraś caiva ghaṇṭākarṇo mahābalaḥ |
ete cānye ca bahavo gaṇā rudreśvarāya vai ||
rakṣanti satataṃ sarve avimuktaṃ hi me gṛham |
ayanaṃ tūttaraṃ jñeyaṃ dṛmicaṇḍeśvaraṃ mama ||
dakṣiṇaṃ śaṅkukarṇaṃ tu oṃkāraṃ viṣuvaṃ mama |
daśakoṭyas tu tīrthānāṃ saṃviśanty atha parvaṇi ||
rahasyaṃ vipramantrāṇāṃ gopanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
yac ca pāśupataṃ proktaṃ padaṃ samyaṅ niṣevitam ||
pūjanāt tad avāpnoti ṣaṇmāsābhyantareṇa tu |
[82]        mamaiva prītir atulā tasminn āyatane sadā ||
anye ca bahavas tatra siddhaliṅgāś ca suvrate |
sarveṣām eva sthānānāṃ tat sthānaṃ tu mamādhikam ||
jñātvā kaliyugaṃ ghoram aprakāśyaṃ kṛtaṃ mayā |
na sā gatiḥ prāpyate yajñadānais
tīrthābhiṣekair na tapobir ugraiḥ |
anyaiś ca dharmair vividhaiḥ śubhair vā
yā kṛttivāse tu jitendriyaiś ca ||
tathā |
darśanād devadevasya brahmahāpi pramucyate |
sparśane pūjane caiva sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet ||
śraddhayā parayā devaṃ ye ‘rcayanti sanātanam |
phālgunasya caturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe samāhitāḥ ||
puṣpaiḥ phalais tathānyaiś ca bhakṣyair uccāvacais tathā |
kṣīreṇa madhunā caiva toyena saha sarpiṣā ||
tarpayanti paraṃ liṅgam arcayanti devaṃ śubham |
huḍuṅkāranamaskāraiḥ nṛtyagītais tathaiva ca ||
mukhavādyair anekaiś ca stotramantrais tathaiva ca |
upoṣya rajanīm ekāṃ bhaktyā paramayā haram ||
ye yānti paramaṃ sthānaṃ sadāśivam anāmayam |
bhūtā hi caitramāsasya arcayet parameśvaram ||
[83]        sa vitteśapuraṃ prāpya krīḍayed yakṣarāḍ iva |
vaiśākhasya caturdaśyāṃ yo ‘rcayet prayataḥ śivam ||
vaiśākhaṃ lokam āsādya tasyaivānucaro bhavet |
jyeṣṭhe māsi caturdaśyāṃ yo ‘rcayec chraddhayā haram ||
so ‘gnilokam avāpnoti yāvad ācandratārakam |
caturdaśyāṃ śucau māsi yo ‘rcayet tu sureśvaram ||
sūryasya loke susukhī krīdate yāvad īpsitam |
śrāvaṇasya caturdaśyāṃ kāmaliṅgaṃ samarcitam ||
sa yāti vāruṇaṃ lokaṃ krīḍate cāpsaraiḥ saha |
māsi bhādrapade yuktam arcayitvā tu śaṅkaram ||
puṣpaiḥ phalaiś ca vividhai rudrasyaiti salokatām |
pitṛpakṣe caturdaśyāṃ pūjayitvā maheśvaram ||
prāpyate pitṛlokaṃ tu krīdate pūjitas tu taiḥ |
prabodhamāse deveśam arcayitvā maheśvaram ||
sa candralokam āpnoti krīḍate yāvad īpsitam |
bahule mārgaśīrṣasya arcayitvā pinākinam ||
viṣṇulokam avāpnoti [krīḍate] kālam akṣayam |
arcayitvā tathā puṣye sthāṇuṃ tuṣṭena cetasā ||
prāpnoti nairṛtaṃ sthānaṃ tena vai saha modate |
mādhe samarcayitvā vai puṣpamūlaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||
[84]        prāpnoti śivalokaṃ tu tyaktvā saṃsārasāgaram |
kṛttivāseśvaraṃ devam arcayet tu prayatnataḥ ||
avimukte ca vastavyaṃ yadīcchen māmakaṃ padam |
