gāyanti siddhāḥ kila gītakāni
dhanyāvimukte tu narā vasanti |
svargāpavargasya padasya liṅgaṃ
ye kṛttivāsaṃ śaraṇaṃ prapannāḥ ||
tathā |
īśvara uvāca |
anyad āyatanaṃ puṇyaṃ kāśipuryāṃ varānane |
dhavantariḥ purā jātaḥ kāśirājagṛhe śubhe ||
tena bhadre tathā kāle aham ārādhitaḥ śubhe |
bhṛṅgīśeśvaranāmānaṃ liṅgaṃ tatra sthitaṃ mama ||
paścānmukhaḥ sthitaś cāhaṃ kūpas tu mama cāgrataḥ |
tiṣṭhanty oṣadhayas tatra sarvā hy amṛtasaṃbhavaḥ ||
kṣiptās tasmin purā kāle vaidyarājena sundari |
tena tat procyate sthānaṃ vaidyanāthaṃ maheśvari ||
tasmin kūpe tu ye devi pānīyaṃ pibate naraḥ |
vyādhibhiḥ saṃpramucyante vaidyanāthaprabhāvataḥ ||
kūpasya cottare bhāge harikeśvarasaṃjñakam |
rogaiś cāpi pramucyante harikeśvaradarśanāt ||
[85]        tuṅgeśasya samīpe tu dakṣiṇe varavarṇini |
śaivaṃ taḍāgam ākhyātaṃ śivenādhiṣṭhitaṃ śubham ||
paścime tu taṭe ramye sthito ‘haṃ tatra suvrate |
paścimābhimukho bhadre tasmin sthāne vyavasthitaḥ ||
śiveśvara iti khyāto bhaktānāṃ varadāyakaḥ |
śiveśvarād dakṣiṇato nātidūre vyavasthitam ||
paścimābhimukhaṃ liṅgaṃ sthāpitaṃ jamadagninā |
jamadagniliṅgāt paścimato nātidūre vyavasthitam ||
bhairaveśvaravikhyātaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam |
tatra durgā sthitā bhadre mamāpi hi bhayaṃkarā ||
nṛtyamānā tu sā devī liṅgasyaiva samīpataḥ |
bhairaveśaṃ tu taṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃsāre na patet punaḥ ||
tasyaiva bhairaveśasya kūpas tiṣṭhati cottare |
tasyopasparśanaṃ kṛtvā sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet ||
kūpasya paścime bhāge liṅgaṃ tiṣṭhati bhāmini |
śukeśvaram iti khyātaṃ sthāpitaṃ vyāsasūnunā ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi vairāgyam api vindati |
tasyaiva cottare pārśve taḍāgaṃ yatra tiṣṭhati ||
tatra snātvā varārohe kṛtakṛtyo bhaven naraḥ |
nirṛte tu diśābhāge śukeśasya tu sundari ||
sthāpitaṃ mukhaliṅgaṃ tu vyāsenāpi maharṣiṇā |
vyāseśvaraṃ tu vikhyātam ṛṣisaṅghais tu vanditam ||
[86]        vyāsakuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā arcayitvā surān pitṝn |
akṣayāṃl labhate lokān yatra tatrābhikāṅkṣitān ||
vyāsatīrthasamīpe tu paścimena yaśasvini |
ghaṇṭākarṇahradaṃ nāma sarvasaukhyapradāyakam ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā hrade tasmin vyāsasyaiva tu darśanāt |
yatra tatra mṛto vāpi vārāṇasyāṃ mṛto bhavet ||
tatra devi tanuṃ tyaktvā labhed gāṇeśvarīṃ gatim |
ghaṇṭākarṇasamīpe tu uttareṇa yaśasvini ||
puṇyam apsarasāṃ khyātaṃ pañcacūḍāvinirmitam |
pañcacūḍāhrade snātvā dṛṣṭvā caiva tam īśvaram ||
svargalokaṃ naro yāti pañcacūḍāpriyaḥ sadā |
tasya cottaradeśe tu aśokavanasaṃsthitam ||
aśokavanamadhyasthaṃ tatra kuṇḍaṃ śubhodakam |
tasmin kuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā vilokaś caiva jāyate ||
vilokāc cottare bhāge nāmnā mandākinī śubhā |
svargaloke tu sā puṇyā kiṃ punar mānuṣe śubhe ||
yatra vai devadevasya sāṃnidhyaṃ devi sarvadā |
liṅgaṃ tatra svayaṃ bhūtaṃ kṣetramadhye tu sundari ||
tathā |
īśvara uvāca |
mandākinījale snātvā dṛṣṭvā vai madhyameśvaram |
ekaviṃśakulopeto rudraloke vasec ciram ||
[87]        etat kila sadā prāhuḥ pitaraḥ sapitāmahāḥ |
yo ‘pi cāsmāt kule jāto mandākinyā jalodgataḥ ||
bhojayec ca yato viprān yatīn pāśupatān budhaḥ |
snānaṃ dānaṃ tapo homaḥ svādhyāyaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam ||
piṇḍanirvāpaṇaṃ caiva sarvaṃ bhavati cākṣayam |
kṣetrasya cāsya saṃkṣepān mayā te kathitaṃ sphuṭam ||
dakṣiṇaṃ bhūmibhāgaṃ tu madhyameśasya yad bhavet |
tatra pūrvāmukhaṃ liṅgaṃ viśvedevaiḥ pratiṣṭhitam ||
paścānmukhaṃ tu deveśaṃ vīrabhadrapratiṣṭhitam |
paścānmukhena dṛṣṭena vīrabhadrasalokatām ||
tayos tu dakṣiṇe devi bhadrakālīhradaṃ smṛtam |
kuṇḍasya paścime tīre śaunakena pratiṣṭhitam ||
mataṅgeśvaranāmānaṃ liṅgaṃ tatraiva tiṣṭhati |
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam ||
mataṅgeśvarakoṇe tu vāyavye tu yaśasvini |
pratiṣṭhitāni liṅgāni narais tatra mahātmabhiḥ ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe bhāge jayantena pratiṣṭhitam |
[devarājasya putreṇa ātmano jayam icchatā ||
[88]        brahmatāreśvaraṃ caiva tasmin sthāne sureśvari |
pitṛbhiḥ yājñavalkyena tatra liṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhitam || ]
tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge siddhikūṭaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
siddhāḥ pāśupatās tatra mama liṅgārcane ratāḥ ||
teṣāṃ vai tatra kūṭo ‘yaṃ siddhakūṭaḥ sa sidhyate |
tatra dhyānaratāḥ kecij japaṃ kurvanti cāpare ||
svādhyāyam anye kurvanti tapaḥ kurvanti cāpare |
ākāśaśayanaṃ kecit kecid bhāvaṃ samāśritāḥ ||
adhomukhās tathaivānye dhūmapeyās tathāpare |
pradakṣiṇānye kurvanti kāṣṭhamaunaṃ tathāpare ||
kurvanti puṣpāharaṇaṃ gaḍūkānāṃ tathāpare |
taiḥ sarvaiḥ sthāpitaṃ liṅgam arcāpūjanatatparaiḥ ||
teṣāṃ tatra tadā bhaktiṃ jñātvā deve hi suvrate |
sāṃnidhyaṃ kṅṛtavān asmiṃs tadanugrahakāmyayā ||
siddheśvaraṃ tu vikhyātaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham |
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ siddhakūṭe vyavasthitam ||
mānavānāṃ hitārthāya tatra sthāne sthito hy aham |
devasya paścime bhāge vāpī tiṣṭhati sundari ||
tatra vāpījale snātvā dṛṣṭvā siddheśvaram naraḥ |
asmin kṣetre tu nirmālyaṃ pāpaṃ saṃkramate tu yat ||
[89]        tat sarvaṃ vilayaṃ yāti siddheśvarasya darśanāt |
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe vārāṇasīmāhātmye guhyāyatanavarṇanaṃ
nāma daśamo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
siddhakūṭasya pūrveṇa devaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
vyāghreśvareti vikhyātaṃ sarvadevaiḥ stutaṃ śubhe ||
tena dṛṣṭena labhate uttamaṃ padam avyayam |
vyāghreśvarād dakṣiṇe ca svayaṃbhūs tatra tiṣṭhati ||
divyaṃ liṅgaṃ tu tatrasthaṃ devānām api durlabham |
rahasyaṃ sarvadevānāṃ bhūmiṃ bhittvā tu cotthitam ||
tena liṅgena dṛṣṭena pūjitena stutena ca |
kṛtakṛtyo bhaved devi saṃsāre na punar viśet ||
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ jyeṣṭhasthānam idaṃ śubham |
mānavānāṃ hitārthāya tatra sthāne sthito hy aham ||
asyāgre devedeveśi mukhaliṅgaṃ ca tiṣṭhati |
paścānmukhaṃ tu taṃ devi pañcacūḍā śubhekṣaṇā ||
tasya dakṣiṇapārśve tu nāmnā prahasiteśvaram |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā labhate devi ānandaṃ brahmaṇaḥ padam ||
tasyottare tu deveśi puṇyaṃ liṅgaṃ tvayā kṛtam |
nivāseti ca vikhyātaṃ sarveṣām eva yoginām ||
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi yogaṃ vindati śāṅkaram |
catuḥsamudravikhyātaḥ kūpas tiṣṭhati sundari ||
[90]        catuḥsamudrasnānena yat phalaṃ labhate naraḥ |
tat phalaṃ sakalaṃ tasya udakasparśanāc chubhe ||
tatraiva tvaṃ mahādevi ramamāṇā mayā saha |
ye ca tvāṃ pūjayiṣyanti bhaktiyuktāś ca mānavāḥ ||
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttiḥ kalpakoṭiśatair api |
īśvara uvāca |
kūpasya uttare devi vyāghreśasya tu dakṣiṇe |
tiṣṭhate tatra vai liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ ca sundari ||
daṇḍīśvaram iti khyātaṃ varadaṃ sarvadehinām |
tena dṛṣṭena labhyeta aiśvaraṃ padam avyayam ||
tasyottare taḍāgaṃ ca devi sarvatra viśrutam |
saṃdhyāpraṇāmakupitā yadā tasmin sureśvari ||
bahurūpaṃ samākhyāya devadevaḥ svayaṃ haraḥ |
daṇḍakaś ca tadā kṣipto devāgre sa prabhākaraḥ ||
tena tatra kṛtaṃ divyaṃ taḍāgaṃ lokaviśrutam |
krodhena prasthitā devi tuhinācalasaṃmukham ||
tāvad asya tadagre vai taḍāgaṃ mahad adbhutam |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tu tadā devi nivṛttā punar eva vā ||
veśma praviśya deveśi sthitā tatraiva bhāmini |
daṇḍena devadevasya sthitena sumahat saraḥ ||
daṇḍakhātam iti prāhur ye purāṇavido janāḥ |
[91]        tasmāt snānaṃ tu kartavyaṃ tatraiva śreya icchayā ||
tatra snāne kṛte devi kṛtakṛtyo bhaven naraḥ |
daṇḍakhāte naraḥ snātvā tarpayitvā svakān pitṝn ||
narakasthās tu ye devi pitṛloke vasanti te |
piśācatvaṃ gatā ye ca narāḥ pāpena karmaṇā ||
teṣāṃ piṇḍapradānena dehasyoddharaṇaṃ smṛtam |
daṇḍakhāte naraḥ snātvā kiṃ bhūyaḥ pariśocati ||
yasya smaraṇamātreṇa pāpasaṅghātapañjaram |
naśyate śatadhā devi daṇḍakhātasya darśanāt ||
tasya daṇḍasya māhātmyaṃ puṇyaṃ śṛṇu mahāyaśaḥ |
sūryoparāge deveśi narā āyānti suvrate ||
kurukṣetraṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtam |
nivṛtte grahaṇe devi kurukṣetrāt paraṃ padam ||
daṇḍakhātaṃ samāyānti ātmaśuddhyarthakāraṇam |
darśanāt tasya khātasya kṛtakṛtyo ‘bhijāyate ||
anyad āyatanaṃ tatra mama devi maheśvari |
jaigīṣavyeśvaraṃ nāma sthāpitaṃ sumahātmanā ||
jaigīṣavyaguhā tasmin devadevasya saṃnidhau |
trikālam arcayaṃl liṅgaṃ bhaktyā tad bhāvitātmanā ||
evam ārādhito devi jaigīṣavyeṇa dhīmatā |
tasya pṛṣṭaś cāhaṃ devi sarvān kāmān pradattavān ||
[92]        tasmāt tu sukṛtaṃ liṅgaṃ pūjayiṣyanti ye narāḥ |
jñānaṃ teṣāṃ dhruvaṃ devi acirāj jāyate bhuvi ||
trirātraṃ tatra kṛtvā vai yo naraḥ pūjayiṣyati |
guhyaṃ praviśyate caiva jñānayukto bhaven naraḥ ||
tasya vai paścime bhāge siddhakūpas tu dakṣiṇe |
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ devalena pratiṣṭhitam ||
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi jñānavān jāyate naraḥ |
tasyaiva ca samīpasthaṃ śatakālapratiṣṭhitam ||
tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge nātidūre tapasvini |
mukhaliṅgaṃ tu tad bhadre paścimābhimukhaṃ śubhe ||
śātātapeśvaraṃ nāma sthāpitaṃ ca maharṣiṇā |
tena dṛṣṭena labhate gatim iṣṭāṃ ca śāśvatīm ||
tasya paścimadigbhāge mahāliṅgaṃ ca tiṣthati |
hetukeśvaranāmānaṃ sarvasiddhiphalapradam ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe bhāge mukhaliṅgaṃ ca tiṣṭhati |
kaṇādeśvaranāmānaṃ paścimābhimukhaṃ sthitam ||
siddhas tatra samīpasthaḥ kaṇādas tu ṛṣiḥ purā |
kūpas tatra samīpasthaḥ puṇyadaḥ sarvadehinām ||
kaṇādeśād dakṣiṇena anyad āyatanaṃ śūbham |
paścānmukhaṃ tu bhūtīśaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam ||
tasyaiva paścime bhāge liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
[93]        caturmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgam āṣāḍhaṃ nāma viśrutam ||
anyāni tatra liṅgāni sthāpitāni mahānti ca |
teṣāṃ pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ tu daityena sthāpitaṃ purā ||
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi putravān jāyate naraḥ |
bhārabhūteśvaraṃ devaṃ tatra dakṣiṇataḥ sthitam ||
paścānmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ bhārabhūteśvaraṃ priye |
vyāseśvarasya pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
parāśareṇa muninā sthāpitaṃ mama bhaktitaḥ |
paścānmukhaṃ tu tad devai mukhaliṅgaṃ ca tiṣṭhati ||
atriṇā sthāpitaṃ bhadre mama bhaktipareṇa ca |
paścānmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ sarvaśāstrapradāyakam ||
vyāseśvarasya pūrveṇa dvau liṅgau tatra suvrate |
sthāpitau devadeveśi śaṅkhena likhitena ca ||
dvau dṛṣṭvā mānavo bhadre ṛṣilokam avāpnuyāt |
anyac ca devadevasya sthānaṃ guhyaṃ yaśasvini ||
liṅgaṃ viśveśvaraṃ nāma sarvadevais tu vanditam |
tena dṛṣṭena labhyeta vratāt pāśupatāt phalam ||
pūrvottaradiśābhāge tasya devasya sundari |
avadhūtaṃ mahat tīrthaṃ sarvapāpāpanuttamam ||
tasya pūrveṇa saṃlagnaṃ nāmnā paśupatīśvaram |
[94]        tasya darśanamātreṇa paśuyoniṃ na gacchati ||
caturmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ paścimābhimukhaṃ sthitam |
tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge liṅgaṃ pañcamukhaṃ sthitam ||
ṛṣiṇā sthāpitaṃ bhadre gobhilena mahātmanā |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi ṛṣilokaṃ sa gacchati ||
tasyaiva paścime devi liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
vidyādharādhipatinā kṛtaṃ jīmūtavāhinā ||
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe guhyāyatanavarṇanaṃ nāmaikādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
anyad āyatanaṃ devi vārāṇasyāṃ mama priye |
gabhastīśvaranāmānaṃ liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
sūryeṇa sthāpitaṃ bhadre mama bhaktipareṇa vai |
tasmin mamāpi sāṃnidhyaṃ nityam eva yaśasvini ||
aiśānīṃ mūrtim āsthāya yatra sthāne sthito hy aham |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi aiśānaṃ lokam āpnuyāt ||
tasya dakṣiṇapārśve tu dadhikarṇahradaṃ sthitam |
uttare kūpam evaṃ tu tasya nāmasya sundari ||
dadhikarṇeśvaraṃ devaṃ mukhaliṅgaṃ ca tiṣṭhati |
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu deveśi gabhastīśasya cottare ||
dakṣiṇena gabhastīśād vāruṇasyāṃ tu suvrate |
mānavānāṃ hitārthāya tvaṃ ca tatra vyavasthitā ||
[95]        ārādhayanti devi tvām uttarābhimukhīṃ sthitām |
ye ca tvā pūjayiṣyanti tasmin sthāne sthito hy aham ||
teṣāṃ tvaṃ vividhāṃl lokān saṃpradāsyasi modate |
jāgaraṃ ye prakurvanti tavāgre dīpadhāriṇaḥ ||
teṣāṃ tvam akṣayāṃl lokān vitariṣyasi bhāmini |
ālayaṃ ye prakurvanti tavārthe varavarṇini ||
teṣāṃ tvam akṣayāṃl lokān prayacchasi na saṃśayam |
ālayaṃ ye prakurvanti bhūmiṃ saṃmārjayanti ca ||
teṣām aṣṭasahasrasya suvarṇasya phalaṃ labhet |
tvām uddiśya tu yo devi brāhmaṇān brāhmaṇīś ca ha ||
bhojayiṣyati yo devi tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu |
tava loke vaset kalpam ihaivāgacchate punaḥ ||
naro vā yadi vā nārī sarvabhogasamanvitau |
dhanadhānyasamāyuktau jāyete ca mahākule ||
subhagau darśanīyau ca rūpayauvanadarpitau |
bhavetām īdṛśau devi sarvasaukhyasya bhājane ||
mānuṣaṃ durlabhaṃ prāpya vidyutsaṃpātacañcalam |
yena dṛṣṭāsi suśroṇi tasya janmabhayaṃ kutaḥ ||
māyāpūryāṃ tu lalitāṃ dṛṣṭvā yal labhate phalam |
tat phalaṃ tasya deveśi yas tvāṃ tatra nirīkṣayet ||
pṛthvīṃ pradakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā yat phalaṃ labhate naraḥ |
tat phalaṃ lalitāyāṃ ca vārāṇasyāṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||
[96]        tena te nāma vikhyātaṃ tathā mukhanirīkṣiṇī |
mukhaprekṣaṇikāṃ dṛṣṭvā saubhāgyaṃ cottamaṃ labhet ||
māghe māsi caturthyāṃ tu tasmin kāla upoṣitaḥ |
arcayitvā tu yo devi jāgaraṃ tatra kārayet ||
tasyarddhimat kulaṃ devi trailokye yāti durlabham |
sukhaprekṣā cottarato dvau liṅgau tatra viśrutau ||
paścānmukhau tu tau devi vṛtratvāṣṭreśvarāv ubhau |
kāñcanīṃ pṛthivīṃ datvā yat puṇyaṃ labhate naraḥ ||
suvarṇasya ca yat puṇyaṃ liṅgayor darśanena tat |
trirātraṃ yaḥ prakurute tatraiva varavarṇini ||
gaurīloko ‘kṣayas tasya punarāvṛttidurlabhaḥ |
tasmād yatnaḥ sadā kāryaḥ sarvadarśanakāṅkṣayā ||
lalitāyāś cottareṇa carcikādhiṣṭhitā śubhā |
mānavānāṃ hitārthāya varadā sarvadehinām ||
carcikāyās tathaivāgre tṣṭhate liṅgam uttamam |
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu tad devi revantena pratiṣṭhitam ||
tasyāgrato varārohe liṅgaṃ pañcanadīśvaram |
paścānmukhaṃ tu tad devi sarvasnānaphalapradam ||
lalitāyāś ca saṃlagnaṃ pūrve kūpas tu tiṣṭhati |
tasmin kūpe jalaṃ spṛṣṭvā atirātraphalaṃ labhet ||
tato dakṣiṇato devi tīrthaṃ pañcanadaṃ smṛtam |
naraḥ pañcanade snātvā dṛṣṭvā liṅgaṃ gabhastinaḥ ||
[97]        anantaṃ phalam āpnoti yatra tatrābhijāyate |
upamanyuś ca suśroṇi liṅgaṃ sthāpitavāṃs tathā ||
mukhāni tasya tiṣṭhanti tasmiṃl liṅge yaśasvini |
tac ca paścānmukhaṃ devi lilitādakṣiṇena tu ||
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi na punarjanmabhāg bhavet |
tasyaiva tu samīpe tu paścime varavarṇini ||
anyal liṅgaṃ tu suśroṇi vyāghrapādapratiṣṭhitam |
tasya saṃdarśanād devi sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
gabhastīśāgrato devi viśvakarmapratiṣṭhitam |
anyāni tatra liṅgāni sthāpitāni mahātmabhiḥ ||
gabhastīśasya liṅgasya nairṛte varavarṇini |
śaśāṅkeśvaranāmānaṃ liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukham sthitam ||
gandharvanagaraṃ gatvā rājñā citrarathena hi |
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi īpsitaṃ phalam āpnuyāt ||
citreśvarāt paścimato liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
jaiministhāpitaṃ pūrvaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam ||
agre tu jaiminīśasya kṛtaṃ liṅgaṃ sumantunā |
anyaiś ca ṛṣibhis tatra liṅgāni subahūni ca ||
teṣāṃ tu dakṣiṇe bhāge liṅgaṃ paścān mukhasthitam |
budheśvaraṃ tathā koṇe sarvasaukhyapradāyakam ||
[98]        budheśvarāt tu koṇena vāyavye nātidūrataḥ |
rāvaṇeśvaranāmānaṃ sthāpitaṃ rākṣasena tu ||
rāvaṇeśvarapūrve tu liṅgaṃ devi caturmukham |
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi yātudhānair na hanyate ||
rāvaṇeśād dakṣiṇato liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam |
varāheśvaranāmānaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam ||
varāheśād dakṣiṇato liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam |
tasyaivārādhanād devi ṣaṇmāsād yogam āpnuyāt ||
tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge liṅgaṃ vai dakṣiṇāmukham |
gālaveśvaranāmānaṃ gurubhaktipradāyakam ||
gālaveśvaradevasya liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
ayogasiddhināmānaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe devi nāmnā vāteśvaraṃ śubham |
tasyaiva cāgrato devi mukhaliṅgaṃ ca tiṣṭhati ||
someśvareti vikhyātaṃ liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā devadeveśaṃ sarvavyādhikṣayo bhavet ||
tasyaiva nairṛte bhāge liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam |
aṅgāreśvaranāmānaṃ sarvasiddhair namaskṛtam ||
pūrveṇa tasya devasya liṅgam anyac ca tiṣṭhati |
kukkuṭeśvaranāmānaṃ gatisaukhyapradāyakam ||
tasyaiva cottare devi pāṇḍavaiḥ sumahātmabhiḥ |
[99]        pañca liṅgāni puṇyāni paścimābhimukhāni tu ||
teṣām ete tu deveśi liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
saṃvarteśvaranāmānaṃ sthāpitaṃ yan maharṣiṇā ||
mamaivātyantasāṃnidhyaṃ tasmiṃl liṅge sureśvari |
tal liṅgam arcayed yo vai tasya siddhiḥ kare sthitā ||
saṃvarteśāt paścimato liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam |
śveteśvaraṃ tu vikhyātaṃ śvetena sthāpitaṃ purā ||
tena dṛṣṭena liṅgena gāṇapatyaṃ labhed dhruvam |
paścime tasya digbhāge kalaśeśvarasaṃjñitam ||
kalaśād utthitaṃ liṅgaṃ kālasya bhayadāyakam |
sūrya uvāca |
kathaṃ kālasya bhayadaṃ kalaśād utthitaḥ katham |
etad deva samācakṣva yadānugrahavān mayi ||
viṣṇur uvāca |
tasyaiva devadevasya prabhāvaṃ śṛṇu bhāskara |
śveto nāma mahātejā ṛṣiḥ paramadhārmikaḥ ||
pūjayāmāsa satataṃ liṅgaṃ tripuraghātinaḥ |
tasya pūjāprasaktasya kadācit kālaparyaye ||
ājagāma tam uddeśaṃ kālaḥ paramadāruṇaḥ |
raktāntanayano ghoraḥ sarpayaṣṭikaro mahān ||
[100]        daṃṣṭrākarālo vikṛto bhinnāñjanasamaprabhaḥ |
raktavāsā mahākāyaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ ||
pāśahastas tadābhyetya śvete pāśam avāsṛjat |
kaṇṭhārpitena pāśena śvetaḥ kālam athābravīt ||
kṣaṇamātraṃ parīkṣasva mama tribhuvanāntaka |
nivartayāmy ahaṃ yāvat pūjanaṃ manmathadviṣaḥ ||
tam abravīt tadā kālaḥ prahasan vai sureśvara |
na śrutaṃ tat tvayā manye vṛddhānāṃ jñātajanmanām ||
śvaḥkāryam adya kurvīta pūrvāhṇe cāparāhṇikam |
na hi pratīkṣate mṛtyuḥ kṛtaṃ vāsya na vā kṛtam ||
garbhe vāpy atha vā bālye vārdhake yauvane tathā |
āyuṣye karmaṇi kṣīṇe loko ‘yaṃ līyate mayā ||
naiṣadhāni na mantrāś ca na homā na punarjayaḥ |
trāyante mṛtyunopetaṃ jarayā vāpi mānavāḥ ||
bahūnīndrasahasrāṇi pitāmahaśatāni ca |
mayātītāni kartavyo nātra manyus tvayānagha ||
vidhatsva pūjanaṃ cāsya mahādevasya śūlinaḥ |
dehanyāso bahuvidho mayā vai śveta kāritaḥ ||
svayaṃ prabhur na caivāhaṃ karmāyattagatir mama |
karmanā hi tathā nāśo nāsti bhūtasya kasyacit ||
karmamārgānusāreṇa dhātrāhaṃ saṃprayojitaḥ |
[101]        nayāmi sarvam ākramya nīyamānas trilocane ||
evam uktas tu kālena nīyamānas trilocanam |
jagāma sarvabhāvena śaraṇaṃ bhaktavatsalam ||
śvete tu śaraṇaṃ prāpte liṅgaṃ satripurāntakam |
cintayāmāsa kālasya vadhopāyaṃ sureśvaraḥ ||
kalaśaṃ yat sthitaṃ tasya udakena prapūritam |
taṃ bhittvā tu samuttasthau kraudhavisphāritekṣaṇaḥ ||
tṛtīyalocanajvālāprakāśitajagattrayaḥ |
dṛṣṭamātras tadā tasya kālo vīkṣaṇatejasā ||
sahasā bhasmabhūtaḥ sa sarvabhūtanibarhaṇaḥ |
śvetasya gatvā sāmīpyaṃ gaṇeśatvaṃ tathaiva ca ||
kṛtvā vinigrahaṃ kālaṃ tatraivāntaradhīyata |
tataḥ prabhṛti deveśi kālaḥ saṃkalayet prajāḥ ||
na kaścit paśyate loke videhatvāj jagattraye |
tasmāt tatra svayaṃbhūto devadevaḥ surārihā ||
śvetasya kalaśaṃ bhittvā kalaśeśvaram ucyate |
tasmāt tatra sthitaṃ devaṃ yo nirīkṣati mānavaḥ ||
janmamṛtyujarāvyādhir naśyate tasya bhāmini |
yatra śvetakṛtaṃ liṅgaṃ bhaktyā yo ‘rcayate naraḥ ||
janmamṛtyubhayaṃ bhittvā saṃsāraṃ na viśet punaḥ |
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe vārāṇasīmāhātmye guhyāyatanavarṇane
dvādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
[102]
īśvara uvāca |
uttare tasya devasya citragupteśvaraṃ sthitam |
paścimābhimukhaṃ devaṃ vārāṇasyāṃ sureśvari ||
citraguptaṃ na paśyeta yo ‘tra drakṣyati mānavaḥ |
paścime citraguptasya anyal liṅgaṃ sthitaṃ śubhe ||
chāyayā sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā nātapaṃ bhavet |
vināyakaś ca tatraiva paścimena yaśasvini ||
tasya darśanamātreṇa vighnair naivābhibhūyate |
kuṇḍaṃ tasya tu pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
mukhaliṅgaṃ tu tad devi virūpākṣaṃ svayaṃ priye |
dakṣiṇena tu tasyaiva kūpas tiṣṭhati bhāmini ||
darśanāt tasya kūpasya yamadvāraṃ na paśyati |
kūpaṃ cāpi sthitaṃ tatra upasparśanapuṇyadam ||
anyāni tatra liṅgāni suraiḥ saṃsthāpitāni ca |
dakṣiṇe kalaśeśasya liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
guheśvareti nāmānaṃ sarvapuṇyaphalapradam |
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe pārśve dvāv etau tatra saṃsthitau ||
uttameśvaranāmānaṃ vāmadevam ataḥ param |
tasyaiva paścime devi liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
kambalāśvatarākṣaṃ tu gandharvapadadāyakam |
aparaṃ tasya devasya liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
nalakūbareśvaraṃ nāma sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
[103]        tasyaiva dakṣiṇe devi maṇikarṇī ca viśrutā ||
tasya cāgre mahat tīrthaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam |
maṇikarṇīśvaraṃ devaṃ kuṇḍamadhye ca tiṣṭhati ||
anenaiva tu dehena sidhyate tasya darśanāt |
tasya cottaradigbhāge liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
parimeśvaranāmānaṃ pūjanād ajaro bhavet |
tasyaiva ca samīpasthaṃ liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
dharmarājena suśroṇi sthāpitaṃ pāpanāśanam |
tasyaiva paścime devi liṅgam anyac caturmukham ||
nirjareśvaranāmānaṃ vyādhīnāṃ nāśanaṃ param |
tasya nairṛtakoṇe tu liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam ||
pitāmahāś cātikāyāḥ snātā ye śubhakarmiṇaḥ |
piṇḍaṃ datvā tathoktaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā devaṃ nadīśvaram ||
brahmalokāt tu te puṇyā na cyavanti kadācana |
dakṣiṇe tasya devasya liṅgam anyac ca tiṣṭhati ||
vāruṇēśvaranāmānaṃ sthāpitaṃ varuṇena hi |
tasya dakṣiṇapārśve tu liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
bāṇena daityarājena sthāpitaṃ mama bhaktitaḥ |
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe devi liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
kūṣmāṇḍeśvaranāmānaṃ sarvadharmaphalapradam |
tasyaiva pūrvato devi rākṣasena pratiṣṭhitam ||
tasya dakṣiṇapārśve tu gaṅgayā sthāpitena tu |
[104]         gaṅgeśvareti nāmnaṃ suralokapradāyakam ||
tasyottareṇa deveśi nimnagābhis tataḥ śubhe |
liṅgāni sthāpitānīha gaṅgātīre yaśasvini ||
vaivasvateśvaraṃ nāma dṛṣṭvā mṛtyubhayāpaham |
vaisvatāt paścime tu liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
ādityaiḥ sthāpitaṃ bhadre ātmanaḥ śreyaso ‘rthibhiḥ |
tasyaiva cāgrato bhadre liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
vajreśvareti nāmākhyaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam |
tasyaiva cāgrato bhadre liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
kanakeśvaranāmānaṃ guhyaṃ devi sanātanam |
chāyeva dṛśyate liṅge sthāpyamāne yaśasvini ||
chāyāṃ ca paśyate yo vai na sa pāpena lipyate |
tasyaiva cāgrato devi liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam ||
tārakeśvaranāmānaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham |
pūjanāc cāsya liṅgasya jñānāvāptir bhaven nṛṇām ||
aparaṃ tatra deveśi kanakeśvarasaṃjñitam |
pūjanāt svayam evātra hiraṇyaṃ saṃprayacchati ||
kanakeśvarasyottreṇa nāmnā ca manujeśvaram |
mukhaliṅgaṃ paścimataḥ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam ||
tasyaiva cāgrato devi liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam |
indreṇa sthāpitaṃ devi mama bhaktyā pratiṣṭhitam ||
[105]        tasya darśanamātreṇa devi jñānaṃ pravartate |
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe devi rambhayā saṃpratiṣṭhitam ||
mukhaliṅgaṃ ca taṃ devi dakṣiṇābhimukhaṃ sthitam |
indreśvarasyottareṇa liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
śacyā ca sthāpitaṃ bhadre devarājasya bhāryayā |
tasyottaradiśābhāge lokapālaiḥ pratiṣṭhitam ||
anyāni tatra liṅgāni devāsuramarudgaṇaiḥ |
yakṣair nāgaiś ca kinnarāpsarasāṃ gaṇaiḥ ||
lokapālaiḥ suraiś caiva liṅgāni sthāpitāni tu |
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe liṅgaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam ||
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu taṃ bhadre phālgunena pratiṣṭhitam |
tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge mahāpāśupateśvaram ||
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi sarvajñānasya bhājanam |
tasyaiva paścime devi samudreṇa pratiṣṭhitam ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe pārśve īśānaṃ lokaviśrutam |
ātmānam uddhared devi liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
tasyāpi devi pūrveṇa vārāṇasyāṃ tu lāṅgaliḥ |
mokṣapradaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ sarvaiśvaryamayaṃ śubham ||
jñātvā kaliyugaṃ ghoraṃ hāhābhūtam acetanam |
brāhmaṇānāṃ hitārthāya tasmin deśe sthito hy aham ||
divyā hi sā parā mūrtir divyajñānaṃ hi tat smṛtam |
anugrahāya viprāṇāṃ yojayiṣye vratena tu ||
[106]        brahmacārī gṛhasthaś ca vānaprastho yatis tathā |
eteṣāṃ hi vibhedas tu bhinnāś caiva pṛthak pṛthak ||
jñānena rahitāḥ sarve punarāvartakāḥ smṛtāḥ |
brāhmaṇānāṃ hitārthāya jñānaṃ caiva prakāśitam ||
vedāḥ sarve samādāya ṣaḍaṅgāḥ sapadakramāḥ |
sarvāṇi yogaśāstrāṇi dadhnā caiva ghṛtena ca ||
tathā vede mahābhāgo vrataṃ pāśupataṃ priye |
ṣaṇmāsais tu mahābhāge yogaiśvaryaṃ pravartate ||
yasya yasya prabhāvo ‘sti yogasyaiva vratasya ca |
yogajñeṣu hi tiṣṭheta dharmaṃ sukhaṃ hi teṣu ca ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ samo dharmo damo vātha yaśasvini |
ahiṃsā caiva satyaṃ ca vidyābhigama eva ca ||
maitro vai brāḥmaṇo nityaṃ gatiṃ prāpnoti cottamām |
bhasmaśāyī tu tiṣṭheta antaḥsavanakṛt tathā ||
liṅganirmālyadhārī ca yatiḥ svāyatane vaset |
japagītahuḍiṅkārastutikṛtyaparaḥ sadā ||
bhāvanād devadevasya dakṣiṇāṃ mūrtim āsthitaḥ |
akasmāt tatra mūtraṃ tu purīṣaṃ vā na saṃkṣipet ||
strīśūdrau nābhibhāṣeta śūdrānnaṃ varjayet sadā |
śūdrānnarasapuṣṭasya niṣkṛtis tasya kīdṛśī ||
[107]        amṛtaṃ brāḥmaṇsyānnaṃ kṣatriyānnaṃ payaḥ smṛtam |
vaiśyānnam annam ity āhuḥ śūdrānnaṃ rudhiraṃ smṛtam ||
tasmād varjeta tad devi yadīcchen māmakaṃ padam |
śmaśānavāsī dharmātmā yathālabdhena vartate ||
labheta rudrasāyujyaṃ sadā rudram anusmaran |
ṣaṇmāsāl labhate jñānam asmin kṣetre viśeṣataḥ ||
nityaṃ pūjayate devaṃ dhruvaṃ mokṣaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
rāgadveṣavinirmuktāḥ siddhāyatanapūjakāḥ ||
teṣāṃ mokṣo mayākhyātas tatra yair mānuṣaiḥ kṛtāḥ |
dvāviṃśe parivarte tu vārāṇasyāṃ mahāvrate ||
nāmnā tu nakulīśeti tasmin sthāne sthito hy aham |
drakṣyanti māṃ kalau tasminn avakīrṇaṃ divaukasaḥ ||
atra sthāne ‘pi deveśi mama putrā divaukasaḥ |
vakrānir madhupiṅgaś ca śvetaketus tathāparaḥ ||
asmin māheśvaraṃ yogaṃ prāpya yogagatiṃ parām |
nakulīśākhyadevasya liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam ||
caturbhiḥ puruṣair yuktaṃ tal liṅgaṃ tac ca saṃsthitam |
tad dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi rudrasyaiva salokatām ||
nakulīśeśvaraṃ devaṃ kapileśvaram eva ca |
pañcāyatanam etat tu yat tu pūrvam udāhṛtam ||
[108]        rahasyaṃ paramaṃ vedaṃ mama vrataniṣevaṇam |
teṣāṃ na kathanīyo ‘haṃ ye madbhaktivivarjitāḥ ||
śakraḥ pāśupate coktaṃ pade samyaṅ niṣevitam |
tat padaṃ vindate devi dṛṣṭair eva na saṃśayaḥ ||
prītimān satataṃ devi ebhir dṛṣṭais ca jāyate |
avimuktaṃ mahākṣetraṃ siddhasaṅghaniṣevitam ||
atra pūjayate devi dhruvaṃ mokṣo na saṃśayaḥ |
siddhikāmās tathā siddhiṃ yāsyanti dvijasattamāḥ ||
iha dattaṃ sadākṣayyaṃ bhaviṣyati mahātmanām |
dvijānāṃ dharmanityānāṃ mama vrataniṣeviṇām ||
ekāham upavāsaṃ yaḥ kariṣyati yaśasvini |
phalaṃ varṣaśatasyeha labhate matparāyaṇaḥ ||
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe vārāṇasīmāhātmye guhyāyatanavarṇanaṃ
nāma trayodaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
anyad āyatanaṃ vakṣye vārāṇasyāṃ sureśvari |
yatra vai devadevasya ruciraṃ sthānam īpsitam ||
nīyamānaṃ purā devi tal liṅgaṃ śaśimaulinaḥ |
rākṣasair antarikṣasthair vrajamānaṃ susatvaram ||
asmin deśe yadāyātās tadā devena cintitam |
avimukte na mokṣas tu kathaṃ me saṃbhaviṣyati ||
[109]        imam arthaṃ tu deveśo yāvac cintayate prabhuḥ |
tāvat kukkuṭaśabdas tu tasmin deśe samutthitaḥ ||
śabdaṃ śrutvā tu taṃ devi rākṣasās trastacetasaḥ |
liṅgam utsṛjya bhītās te prabhātasamaye gatāḥ ||
gatais tu rākṣasair devi liṅgaṃ tatraiva saṃsthitam |
sthāne tu rucire śubhre devadevaḥ svayaṃ prabhuḥ ||
avimuktas tatra madhye avimuktaṃ tataḥ smṛtam |
tadāvimukte tu surair harasya
nāma smṛtaṃ puṇyatamākṣarāḍhyam |
mokṣapradaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamnāṃ
ye prāṇinaḥ pañcatāṃ tatra yātāḥ ||
kukkuṭāś cāpi deveśi tasmin sthāne sthitāḥ sadā |
adyāpi tatra dṛśyante pūjyamānāḥ śubhārthibhiḥ ||
avimuktaṃ sadā liṅgaṃ yo ‘tra drakṣyati mānavaḥ |
na tasya punarāvittiḥ kalpakoṭiśatair api ||
devasya dakṣiṇe bhāge vāpī tiṣṭhati śobhanā |
tasyās tathodakaṃ pītvā punarjanma na vidyate ||
pītamātreṇa tenaiva udakena yaśasvini |
trīṇi liṅgāni vardhante hṛdaye puruṣasya tu ||
etad guhyaṃ mahādevi na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit |
daṇḍapāṇis tu tatrastho rakṣate taj jalaṃ sadā ||
paścimaṃ tīram āsādya devadevasya śāsanāt |
pūrveṇa tārako devo jalaṃ rakṣati sarvadā ||
[110]        nadīśaś cottareṇaiva mahākālas tu darkṣiṇe |
rakṣate taj jalaṃ nityaṃ madbhaktānāṃ tu mohanam ||
viṣṇur uvāca |
mamāpi sā parā devi tanur āpomayī śubhā |
aprāpyā durlabhā devi mānavair akṛtātmabhiḥ ||
yais tu tan na jalaṃ pītaṃ kṛtārthās te tu mānavāḥ |
teṣāṃ tu tārakaṃ jñānam utpatsyati na saṃśayaḥ ||
vāpījale naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā vai daṇḍanāmakam |
avimuktaṃ tato dṛṣṭvā kaivalyaṃ labhate kṣaṇāt ||
skandapurāṇe |
avimukteśvaraṃ nāma tac chmaśānaṃ vimuktaye |
jaigīṣavyeṇa muninā yatra rudro ‘bhirādhitaḥ ||
yatra saṃdhyām upāsitvā brāhmaṇaḥ sakṛd eva tu |
saṃdhyām upāstavān asmāc ca ṣaḍvarṣasamāḥ samāḥ (?) ||
purīṃ vārāṇasīṃ tāṃ tu śmaśānaṃ cāvimuktakam |
avimukteśvaraṃ taṃ ca dṛṣṭvā gaṇapatir bhavet ||
matsyapurāṇe daṇḍapāṇivarapradāne |
devadeva uvāca |
jarāmaraṇasaṃtyaktaḥ sarvaśokavivarjitaḥ |
bhaviṣyati gaṇādhyakṣo varadaḥ sarvapūjitaḥ ||
[111]        ajeyaś cāpi sarveṣāṃ yogaiśvaryasamanvitaḥ |
annadaś cāpi lokebhyaḥ kṣetrapālo bhaviṣyati ||
mahābalo mahāsattvo brahmaṇo mama ca priyaḥ |
akṣaś ca daṇḍapāṇiś ca mahāyogī tathaiva ca ||
udbhramaḥ saṃbhramaś caiva gaṇau te paricārakau |
tavājñayā kariṣyete lokasyodbhramasaṃbhramau ||
tathā |
devabrāhmaṇavidviṣṭā devabhaktividaṇḍakāḥ |
brahmaghnāś ca kṛtaghnāś ca tathā naikṛtikāś ca ye ||
lokadviṣṭā gurudviṣṭā tīrthāyatanadūṣakāḥ |
sarvapāparatāś caiva ye cānye kutsitā bhuvi ||
teṣāṃ nāstīti vāso vai sthito vai daṇḍanāyakaḥ |
rakṣaṇārthaṃ niyuktaṃ vai daṇḍanayakam uttamam ||
pūjayitvā yathāśakti gandhapuṣpādidhūpakaiḥ |
namaskāraṃ tu taṃ kṛtvā nāyakasya tu mantravit ||
sarvavarṇāvṛtakṣetre nānāvidhasarīsṛpe |
īśvarānugṛhītā hi gatiṃ gāṇeśvarīṃ gatāḥ ||
liṅgapurāṇe |
avimuktasya cāgre tu liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
prītikeśvaranāmānaṃ prītiṃ yacchati śāśvatīm ||
[112]        avimuktottareṇaiva liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
avimuktaṃ ca taṃ devi nāmnā vai mokṣakeśvaram ||
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi jñānavān jāyate naraḥ |
tasya cottarato devi liṅgaṃ caiva caturmukham ||
varuṇeśvaranāmānaṃ pāpānāṃ bhayamocanam |
pūrveṇa tasya saṃlagnaṃ mukhaliṅgaṃ ca tiṣṭhati ||
suvarṇākṣeśvaraṃ nāma yajñānāṃ phaladāyakam |
tasya caivottare gaurī svayaṃ tiṣṭhati puṇyadā ||
tasyās tu darśanād devyāḥ saubhāgyaṃ jāyate param |
dakṣiṇe tasya devasya nikumbho nāva vai gaṇaḥ ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānuṣo devi kṣetravāsaṃ tu vindati |
vināyakaś ca tatraiva paścimena yaśasvini ||
tasya darśanamātreṇa vighnair naivābhibhūyate |
nikumbhasya tu pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam ||
mukhaliṅgaṃ tu taṃ devi vijayākhyaṃ svayaṃ priye |
dakṣiṇena tu tatraiva śukreśvaram iti smṛtam ||
mukhaliṅgaṃ tu taṃ bhadre śukreṇa sthāpitaṃ purā |
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu taṃ bhadre śivaśokapradāyakam ||
tasyaiva cottare devi mukhaliṅgaṃ ca tiṣṭhati |
paścānmukhaṃ tu taṃ devi devayānyā tu sthāpitam ||
tasyaiva cāgrato bhadre liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
kacena sthāpitaṃ bhadre devācāryasya sūnunā ||
[113]        tasyaiva ca samīpe tu kūpas tiṣṭhati suvrate |
tasyopasparśanād devi sarvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
tasyaiva paścime bhāge devo devī va tiṣṭhataḥ |
bhaktidau tau tu sarveṣāṃ ye ‘pi duṣkṛtino narāḥ ||
śukreśvarasya pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
anarkeśvaranāmānaṃ mokṣadaṃ sarvadehinām ||
tasyaiva pūrvato bhāge gaṇais tu parivāritam |
gaṇeśvaram iti khyātaṃ sarvaharṣapradāyakam ||
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe vārāṇasīmāhatmye guhyāyatanavarṇanaṃ nāma
caturdaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
anyad āyatanaṃ vakṣye vārāṇasyāṃ sureśvari |
rāmeṇa sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ laṅkāyāś cāgatena hi ||
tasya dakṣiṇapārśve tu liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam |
tripurāntakaraṃ nāma sarvapāpapraṇāśanam ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe liṅgaṃ dattātreyapratiṣṭhitam |
jñānaṃ cotpadyate devi tasya liṅgasya darśanāt ||
tasya paścimadigbhāge harikeśeśvaraṃ śubham |
tatraivārādhito devi harikeśena suvrate ||
harikeśeśvaraṃ devaṃ sarvakilbiṣanāśanam |
tasya paścimadigbhāge gokarṇaṃ nāma viśrutam ||
[114]        tatra snāto varārohe rājate devi candravat |
paścimābhimukhaṃ liṅgaṃ kāśipuryāṃ ca suvrate ||
uttaraṃ sarvasiddhānām anantaphaladāyakam |
devadevasya caivāgre taḍāgaṃ devaviśrutam ||
tatra snāto varārohe rājate devi candravat |
tasyaiva paścime tīre liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam ||
devena sthāpitaṃ bhadre mama bhaktipareṇa vai |
tasyaiva vāgrato devi kuṇḍaṃ tiṣṭhati bhāmini ||
tasmin snāto varārohe devalokam avāpnuyāt |
deveśvarasyottareṇa piśācaiḥ sthāpitaṃ purā ||
piśāceśvaranāmānaṃ mokṣadaṃ sarvadehinām |
dhruveśasyāgrato devi mukhaliṅgaṃ ca tiṣṭhati ||
paścānmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ tīre kuṇḍasya bhāmini |
vaidyanāthaṃ tu taṃ vidyāt sarvasaukhyapradāyakam ||
tasyaiva nairṛte bhāge manunā sthāpitaṃ purā |
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ tasya kuṇḍasya dakṣiṇe ||
tena dṛṣṭena suśroṇi sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet |
vaidyanāthasya pūrveṇa liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam ||
mucukundeśvaraṃ nāma devānāṃ tu varapradam |
priyavratasya tad devi sarvayajñaphalapradam ||
[115]        tasyaiva dakṣiṇe devi liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam |
sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ gautameśaṃ ca nāmataḥ ||
tena dṛṣṭena deveśi sāmavedaphalaṃ labhet |
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe devi vibhāṇḍeśvarasaṃjñitam ||
ṛṣyaśṛṅgeśvaraṃ nāma tasya dakṣiṇataḥ sthitam |
tasyaiva pūrvato devi brahmeśvaram iti smṛtam ||
brahmeśvarāc ca koṇena piśāceśvarasaṃjñitam |
paścimābhimukhaṃ devi parjanyeśvaranāmataḥ ||
parjanyeśvaranāmānaṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradam |
parjanyeśvarapūrveṇa nāmnā tu nahuṣeśvaram ||
nahuṣeśvarapūrveṇa devadevī ca tiṣthati |
viśālākṣīti vikhyātā bhaktānāṃ tu phalapradā ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe bhāge jarāsandheśvaraṃ sthitam |
caturmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ dṛṣṭvā devi phalapradam ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe devi bhogadā sarvadehinām |
bhogā lalitakā devi sarvasiddhipradāyikā ||
jarāsandheśvarasyāgre liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam |
hiraṇyākṣeśvaraṃ nāma hiraṇyaphaladāyakam ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe liṅgaṃ yayātīśvaranāmataḥ |
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam ||
tasyaiva paścime bhāge brahmeśasya samīpataḥ |
[116]        paścānmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ dṛṣṭvā vedaphalaṃ labhet ||
agastyasya samīpe tu mukhaliṅgaṃ tu tiṣṭhati |
viśvāvasus tu gandharvo liṅgaṃ sthāpitavān purā ||
agastyeśvarapūrveṇa muṇḍeśo nāma nāmataḥ |
paścānmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ vīrasiddhipradaṃ nṛṇām ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe devi vidhis tiṣṭhati pārvati |
vidhinā sthāpitaṃ liṅgaṃ paścimābhimukhaṃ sthitam ||
vidhīśvarād dakṣiṇena tīrthaṃ sarvatra viśrutam |
daśāśvamedhikaṃ nāma liṅgaṃ tatra svayaṃ sthitam ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet |
daśāśvamedhāc cottarato mātaras tatra saṃsthitāḥ ||
tāsāṃ mukhe tu tat kuṇḍaṃ tiṣṭhate varavarṇini |
tatra sthānaṃ naraḥ kuryān nārī vā puruṣo ‘pi vā ||
īpsitaṃ phalam āpnoti mātṝṇāṃ ca prasādataḥ |
agastyeśād dakṣiṇato liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam ||
pulastyeśvaranāmānaṃ sarvārogyavivardhanam |
tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge liṅgam anyac ca tiṣṭhati ||
[117]        puṣpadanteśvaraṃ nāma sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
tasyaivāgre tu koṇe tu liṅgāni sumahānti ca ||
devarṣigaṇapuṣṭāni sarvasiddhikarāṇi ca |
tasyaiva pūrvadigbhāge mahadāścaryadāyakam ||
pañcopacārapūjāyāṃ svpnasiddhiṃ kariṣyati |
liṅgaṃ siddheśvaraṃ nāma pūrvābhimukhasaṃsthitam ||
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe vārāṇasīmāhātmye guhyāyatanavarṇaṇaṃ nāma
pañcadaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
anyac caiva pravakṣyāmi hariścandreśvaraṃ śubham |
yatra siddho mahātmā vai hariścando mahābalaḥ ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mānavo devi rudrasya padam āpnuyāt |
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ svargalokapradāyakam ||
hariścandreśvarād devi anyal liṅgaṃ tu paścime |
pūrvāmukhaṃ tu taṃ devi nāmnā vai nairṛteśvaram ||
tasya saṃdarśanād devi kaivalyaṃ jñānam āpnuyāt |
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukham avasthitam ||
nāmnā hy āṅgiraseśaṃ tad vairāgyasukhadāyakam |
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe devi kṣemeśvaram anuttamam ||
[118]        [tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge kedāraṃ nāma viśrutam |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā manujo devi rudrasyānucaro bhavet ||
kedārād dakṣiṇe caiva liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam |
nīlakaṇṭheti nāmānaṃ suralokapradāyakam ||
tasyaiva vāyave koṇe ambarīśeśvaraṃ śubham | ]
tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge liṅgaṃ vai dakṣiṇāmukham ||
nāmnā kālañjaraṃ devaṃ sarvapātakanāśanam |
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe bhāge lokārko nāma vai raviḥ ||
tasya darśanamātreṇa sūryalokam avāpnuyāt |
lolārkāt paścime bhāge durgādevī ca tiṣṭhati ||
mānavānāṃ hitārthāya kūṭe kṣetrasya dakṣiṇe |
durgāyāḥ paścime devi liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam ||
asitena ca tad devi bhaktyā vai saṃpratiṣṭhitam |
śuṣkanadyās tu nāmnā vai śuṣkeśvaram iti smṛtam ||
[119]        śuṣkeśvarāt paścimena nāmnā tu janakeśvaram |
janakena mahābhāge bhaktyā cāpi pratiṣṭhitam ||
paścānmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ darśanād avyathaḥ śubhe |
tasyaiva cottare bhāge nātidūre yaśasvini ||
śaṅkukarṇeśvaraṃ nāma liṅgaṃ tatraiva tiṣthati |
tasya darśanamātreṇa vratasiddhir bhaven nṛṇām ||
śuṣkeśvarāc cottareṇa liṅgaṃ paścānmukhaṃ sthitam |
siddheśvareti nāmānaṃ kuṇḍasyaiva taṭasthitam ||
tatra kuṇḍe naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā siddheśvaraṃ tu vai |
sarvāsām eva siddhīnāṃ pāraṃ gacchati mānavaḥ ||
vāyavye tu diśābhāge śaṅkukarṇeśvarasya tu |
māṇḍavyeśam iti khyātaṃ surasiddhais tu vanditam ||
tasya caiva samīpe tu svayaṃ devaś ca tiṣṭhati |
gaṇaiḥ parivṛto devi devyā saha mahāprabhuḥ ||
dvāre sve tiṣṭhate devi svayaṃ kṣetraṃ ca rakṣati |
devasya cottare bhāge nātidūre vyavasthitam ||
mukhaliṅgaṃ tu tatraiva liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ śubhe |
tasyaiva cottare pārśve chāgaleśvarasaṃjñitam ||
paścānmukhaṃ tu tal liṅgaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
anyad āyatanaṃ devi paścimena yaśasvini ||
kapardīśvaranāmānam uttamaṃ sarvadāyakam |
[120]        tasya pūrveṇa suśroṇi liṅgaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ sthitam ||
hariteśvaranāmānaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaram |
kātyāyaneśvaraṃ nāma tasya dakṣiṇataḥ sthitam ||
tena dṛṣṭena manujaḥ sarvayajjaphalaṃ labhet |
anyat tasyaiva pārśve tu aṅgāreśvarasaṃjñitam ||
taḍāgaṃ cāpi tatrastham aṅgāreśvarasaṃjñitam |
tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge nātidūre vyavasthitam ||
mukureśvaranāmānaṃ sarvayātrāphalapradam |
pāścimābhimukhaṃ liṅgaṃ kuṇḍasya purataḥ sthitam ||
tasya kuṇḍasya pārśve tu chāgaleśvarasaṃjñitam |
tasya darśanamātreṇa yogaiśvaryaṃ pravartate ||
anyāni santi liṅgāni śataśo ‘tha sahasraśaḥ |
na mayā tāni coktāni bahutvān nāmadheyataḥ ||
saptakoṭyas tu liṅgāni asmin sthāne sthitā bhuvi ||
teṣāṃ darśanamātreṇa jñānaṃ cotpadyate kṣaṇāt |
uddeśamātraṃ kathitaṃ mayā tubhyaṃ varānane ||
na śakyaṃ vistarād vaktuṃ varṣakōṭiśatair api |
etāni siddhaliṅgāni kūpāḥ puṇyā hradās tathā ||
vāpyo nadyo ‘tha kuṇḍāni mayā te parikīrtitāḥ |
eteṣu caiva yaḥ snānaṃ kariṣyati samāhitaḥ ||
liṅgāni sparśayitvā ca saṃsāre na viśet punaḥ |
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni antarikṣacarāṇi (ca?) ||
[121]        teṣāṃ madhye tu ye śreṣṭhā mayā te kathitā śubhe |
tīrthayātrā varārohe kathitā pāpanāśinī ||
yena caiṣā kṛtā devi so ‘vaśyaṃ muktibhāg bhavet |
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe vārāṇasīmāhātmye guhyāyatanavarṇanaṃ nāma
ṣoḍaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
anyac caiva pravakṣyāmi mahābhāgyaṃ varānane |
caturdaśāyatanaṃ kṛtvā aṣṭāyatanam eva ca ||
pañcāyatanam evaṃ tu lalitā ca vināyakaḥ |
navadurgās tathā proktā etat kṛtyaṃ varānane ||
rahasyam etat kathitaṃ na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit |
śaileśaṃ prathamaṃ dṛṣṭvā snātvā vai varaṇāṃ nadīm ||
snānaṃ tu saṅgame kṛtvā dṛṣṭvā vai saṃgameśvaram |
svarlīne tu kṛtasnāno dṛṣṭvā svarlīnam īśvaram ||
mandākinyāṃ naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā vai madhyameśvaram |
hiraṇyagarbhe snātas tu dṛṣṭvā caiva tu īśvaram ||
maṇikarṇyāṃ naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā caiśānam īśvaram |
tasmin kūpa upaspṛśya dṛṣṭvā goprekṣam īśvaram ||
kapilāyāṃ hrade snātvā dṛṣṭvā vai vṛṣabhadhavajam |
upaśāntasya devasya dakṣiṇe kūpam uttamam ||
[122]        tasmin kūpe upaspṛśya dṛṣṭvopaśāntam īśvaram |
pañcacūḍāhrade snātvā jyeṣṭhasthānaṃ tato ‘rcayet ||
catuḥsamudrakūpe tu snātvā devaṃ tato ‘rcayet |
devasyāgre tu kūpasya tatropasparśane kṛte ||
tato ‘rcayeta deveśaṃ śuddheśvaram ataḥ param |
daṇḍakhāte naraḥ snātvā vyādeśaṃ tu tato ‘rcayet ||
śaunakeśvarakuṇḍe tu snānaṃ kṛtvā tato ‘rcayet |
jambukeśvaranāmānaṃ dṛṣṭvā caiva yaśasvini ||
dṛṣṭvā na jāyate martyaḥ saṃsāre duḥkhasāgare |
pratipatprabhṛti deveśi yāvat kṛṣṇacaturdaśīm ||
etatkrameṇa kartavyaṃ mahad āyatanaṃ śubhe |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi aṣṭāyatanam uttamam ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā manujo devi lāṅgalīśaṃ tato vrajet |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tu tato devi āṣāḍhīśaṃ tato ‘rcayet ||
dṛṣṭvā cāṣāḍinaṃ devi bhārabhūtaṃ tato vrajet |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tu tato devaṃ gacched vai tripurāntakam ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvāpi tato devi nakulīśaṃ tato vrajet |
dakṣiṇe nakulīśasya tryambakaṃ ca tato vrajet ||
[123]        aṣṭāyatanam evaṃ hi mayā te parikīrtitam |
aṣṭāyatanam etad dhi kariṣyanti hi ye narāḥ ||
te mṛtāpi bahiḥ kṣetre rudralokasya bhājanāḥ |
īśvara uvāca |
pūrvaṃ caiva mayā devi pañcāyatanam uttamam |
rocate me sadā vāsaḥ pañcāyatana uttame ||
eṣāṃ dig uttarā devi vārāṇasyāṃ sadā priye |
mama cottarato nityam asmin sthāne viśeṣataḥ ||
ekāntavāsino viprā bhasmaniṣṭhā dṛḍhavratāḥ |
teṣāṃ tu cottamaṃ sthānaṃ tad vadanti ca kecana ||
divyā hi sā parā mūrtir oṃkāre ha sthitaḥ sadā |
utpattisthitikāle ‘haṃ tasminn āyatane sthitaḥ ||
evaṃ ca yo vijānāti na sa pāpena lipyate |
satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ satyaṃ triḥ satyaṃ nānyataśḥ śubhe ||
śīghraṃ tatra ca saṃyātu yadīcchen māmakaṃ padam |
evaṃ te kathitaṃ devi punar vistarato mayā ||
īśvara uvāca |
avimuktaṃ ca svarlīnaṃ tathā madhyamakaṃ śūbham |
etat trikaṇṭakaṃ nāma mṛtyukāle ‘mṛtapradam ||
kāraṇaṃ tasya kṣetrasya mayā te kathitaṃ śubhe |
iyaṃ vārāṇasī puṇyā śreṣṭhā pāśupatī sthalī ||
[124]        sarveṣāṃ caiva jantūnāṃ hetur mokṣasya sundari |
tathā |
avimuktaṃ ca svarlīnam oṃkāraṃ caṇḍam īśvaram |
madhyamaṃ kṛttivāsaṃ ca ṣaḍaṅgam īśvaraṃ smṛtam ||
avimukte mahākṣetre guhyam etat paraṃ mama |
sopadeśena jñātavyaṃ yadīcchet paramaṃ padam ||
etad rahasyamāhātmyaṃ na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yātrākālaṃ ca sarvadā ||
caitramāse tu devais tu yātreyaṃ ca kṛtā śubhā |
tasyaiva kāmakuṇḍe tu snānapūjanatatparaiḥ ||
vaiśākhe daityarājais tu yātreyaṃ ca kṛtā purā |
vimaleśvarakuṇḍe tu snānapūjanatatparaiḥ ||
jyeṣṭhamāse ‘pi siddhais tu yātreyaṃ ca kṛtā purā |
rudravāsasya kuṇḍe tu snānapūjanatatparaiḥ ||
āṣāḍhe cāpi gandarvair yātreyaṃ ca kṛtā mama |
śreyā devās tu kuṇḍasthaiḥ snānapūjanatatparaiḥ ||
vidhyādharais tu yātreyaṃ śrāvaṇe māsi tatparaiḥ |
lakṣmīkuṇḍasya saṃsthaiś ca snānapūjanatatparaiḥ ||
pitṛbhiś cāpi yātreyam āśvine māsi tatparaiḥ |
kapilāhradasaṃsthaiś ca snānapūjanatatparaiḥ ||
[125]        ṛṣibhiś cāpi yātreyaṃ kārtike māsi tatparaiḥ |
mārkaṇḍeyahradasthaiś ca snānapūjanatatparaiḥ ||
vidyādharaiś ca yātreyaṃ māsi mārgaśire kṛtā |
kapālamocanasthaiś ca snānapūjanatatparaiḥ ||
guhyakaiś caiva yātreyaṃ puṣyamāse tu tatparaiḥ |
piśācaiś caiva yātreyaṃ māghamāse ca tatparaiḥ ||
dhanadeśvarakuṇḍasthaiḥ snānapūjanatatparaiḥ |
yakṣeśaiś cāpi yātreyaṃ māghamāse ca tatparaiḥ ||
koṭitīrthe tu saṃsthaiś ca snānapūjanatatpariḥ |
piśācaiś caiva yātreyaṃ phālgune māsi tatparaiḥ ||
gokarṇakuṇḍasaṃsthaiś ca snānapūjanatatparaiḥ |
piśācais tu yadā yasmin phālgunasya caturdaśīm ||
tena sā procyate devi piśācī nāma viśrutā |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yātrāyāṃ niṣkṛtiḥ parā ||
udakumbhās tu dātavyā miṣṭānnena samanvitāḥ |
tena devi tadā prāptaṃ pūrvoktaṃ phalam eva ca ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yātrāyāṃ ca varānane |
śuklapakṣe tṛtīyāyāṃ tava yātrā mahāphalā ||
yatra gaurī tu draṣṭavyā tāṃ ca śṛṇu varānane |
snānaṃ kṛtvā tu gantavyaṃ goprekṣe tu yaśasvini ||
ahani kālikā devī arcitavyā prayatnataḥ |
jyeṣṭḥasthāne tato gaurī arcitavyā prayatnataḥ ||
tasmāt sthānāt tu gantavyam avimuktasya cottare |
[126]        tatra devī sadā gaurī pūjitavyā ca bhakitaḥ ||
anyā vāpi parā proktā samvartalalitā śubhā |
draṣṭavyā cāpi sā devī sarvakāmaphalapradā ||
sarvakāmān avāpnoti yadi dyāyeta mānavaḥ |
tatas tu bhojayed viprān śivabhaktān śucivratān ||
vāsaiḥ sadakṣiṇaiś caiva yathārham atipuṣkalaiḥ |
pañcagaurīṃ tu yaḥ kṛtvā bhaktyā devi samāhitaḥ ||
sarvāṃś caiva rasān gandhān gaurīm uddiśya brāhmaṇe |
dadyād iti śeṣaḥ |
uttamaṃ śreya āpnoti saubhāgyena samanvitam |
vināyakān pravakṣyāmi tasya kṣetrasya vighnadān ||
ḍhuṇḍhiṃ tu prathamaṃ dṛṣṭvā tathā koṇavināyakam |
devyā vināyakaṃ caiva goprekṣe hastinaṃ smṛtam ||
vināyakaṃ tathaivānyaṃ sindūraṃ nāma viśrutam |
caturtho devi draṣṭavya evaṃ pañca vināyakāḥ ||
laḍḍukāś ca pradātavyā etān uddiśya brāhmaṇe |
etena caiva dharmeṇa siddhimān jāyate naraḥ ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi caṇḍikāḥ kṣetrarakṣikāḥ |
dakṣiṇe rakṣate durgā nairṛte cottareśvarī ||
aṅgāreśī paścime ca vāyavya bhadrakālikā |
uttare bhīṣmacaṇḍī ca mahāmuṇḍā ca sā tataḥ ||
[127]        ūrdhvakeśī samāyuktā śāṅkarī sarvataḥ smṛtā |
ūrdhvakeśī ca āgneyyāṃ citraghaṇṭātha madhyataḥ ||
etāś ca caṇḍikā devi yo ‘tra drakṣyati ca mānavaḥ |
tasya tuṣṭāś ca tāḥ sarvāḥ kṣetraṃ rakṣanti tatparāḥ ||
vighnaṃ kurvanti satataṃ pāpānāṃ devi sarvadā |
tasmāc caiva sadā pūjyāś caṇḍikāḥ savināyakāḥ ||
yadīcchet satataṃ devi vārāṇasyāṃ śūbhāṃ sthitam |
anyac ca te pravakṣyāmi tasmin kṣetre sureśvari ||
tisro nadyas tu tatrasthā vahanti ca śubhodakāḥ |
yāsāṃ darśanamātreṇa brahmahatyā nivartate ||
ekā pitāmahasrotā mandākinī tathāparā |
matsyodarī tṛtīyā ca etās tisras tu puṇyadāḥ ||
mandākinī tathā puṇyā madhyameśvarasaṃsthitā |
pitāmhasrotikā ca avimukte tu puṇyadā ||
matsyodarī ca oṃkāre puṇyadā sarvadaivataiḥ |
tasmin sthāne yadi gaṅgā āgamiṣyati bhāmini ||
tadā puṇyatamaḥ kālo devānām api durlabhaḥ |
varaṇāsiktasalile jāhnavī jalamiśrite ||
tatra nādeśvare puṇye snātaḥ kum anuśocati |
tasmin kāle ca tatraiva snānaṃ devi kṛtaṃ mayā ||
tena hastatalād devi kapālaṃ patitaṃ kṣaṇāt |
kapālamocanaṃ nām tatraiva sumahatsaraḥ ||
[128]        pāvanaṃ sarvasattvānāṃ puṇyadaṃ sarvadehinām |
oṃkāreśvaranāmānaṃ tatra snānaṃ kṛtaṃ mayā ||
tena sa procyate deva oṃkāreśvaranāmataḥ |
matsodarījale gaṅgā oṃkāreśvarasaṃnidhau ||
tadā tasmin jale snātvā brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati |
matsyodarījale snātvā dṛṣṭvā coṃkāram īśvaram ||
śokamohajarāmṛtyur na ca taṃ spṛśate punaḥ |
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe vārāṇasīmāhātmye guhyāyatanavarṇanaṃ nāma
saptadaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
viṣṇur uvāca |
etac chrutvā vaco devī vismayotphullalocanā |
oṃkāradarśanārthaṃ vai kapileśam upāgatā ||
tasmāt tvam api deveśaṃ pūjayasva sadāśivam |
etat paramam ānandaṃ prāpsyate paramaṃ padam ||
etac chrutvā paraṃ guhyaṃ sakāśāc cakraprāṇinaḥ |
oṃkāram arcayed devaṃ sadā tadgatamānasaḥ ||
sūrya uvāca |
tasmāt tvam api durdharṣam ārādhaya sureśvaram |
tena tatpadam āpnoṣi yad anyair api durlabham ||
sūryasya vacanaṃ śrutvā vārāṇasyām upāgataḥ |
tatra devi tad oṃkāraṃ dṛṣṭvā caiva praṇamya ca ||
ārādhanaparo bhūtvā liṅgaṃ sthāpya caturmukham |
devadevasakāśād vai kṛtakṛtyo bhavec chuciḥ ||
[129]        yaḥ saṃprāpya mahattattvam īśvare kṛtaniścayaḥ |
tasmāt tvam api gāṅgeya yati śreyo ‘bhivāñchasi ||
ārādhayasva deveśaṃ manasaḥ sthairyam ātmanaḥ |
tathā |
tasmiṃs tu yaḥ śivaḥ sākṣād oṃkāreśvarasaṃjñitaḥ ||
etat guhyasya māhātmyaṃ tava snehān mahāmune |
akāraṃ ca ukāraṃ ca makāraṃ ca prakīrtitaḥ ||
asmiṃl liṅge tu saṃsiddho munikālikavṛkṣayaḥ (?) |
akāras tatra vijñeyo viṣṇulokagatipradaḥ ||
tasya dakṣiṇapārśve tu oṃkārākhyeti kīrtitaḥ |
tatra siddhiṃ parāṃ prāpto devācāryo bṛhaspatiḥ ||
ukāraṃ tatra vijñeyaṃ brahmaṇaḥ padam avyayam |
tasya cottaradigbhāge makāraṃ viṣṇusaṃjñakam ||
tasmiṃl liṅge ca saṃsiddhaḥ kapilarṣir mahāmuniḥ |
tasmāt tvam api gārgeya manaḥsthairyaṃ yadīcchasi ||
liṅgasyārādhane yatnaṃ kuruṣva niyatavrataḥ |
vidyāṃ pāśupatīṃ prāpya tasmin stutye vyapāśrayaḥ ||
nirmamo nirahaṃkāraḥ padam āpnoṣi śāśvatam |
etac chrutvā vacaḥ stutvā yājñavalkyasya darśitāḥ ||
vārāṇasīṃ samabhyetya pañcāyatanam uttamam |
ārādhyamāno deveśas tasmin sthāne sthitaḥ sadā ||
tasmād anye ‘pi ye kecil liṅgasyārādhane ratāḥ |
teṣāṃ vai paścime kāle jñānam utpadyate sadā ||
[130]        evaṃ jñātvā tu yo martyaḥ sadā liṅgārcane rataḥ |
na tasya punarāvṛttiḥ kalpakoṭiśatair api ||
tasmād vai saṃpradāyāc ca arcitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
mānuṣyaṃ durlabhaṃ prāpya vidyutsaṃpātacañcalam ||
liṅgaṃ yo ‘racayate vipra ātmānaṃ sa samuddharet |
ātmānaṃ ghātayen nityaṃ yo na liṅgaṃ samarcayet ||
iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe vārāṇasīmāhātme guhyāyatanavarṇanaṃ
nāmāṣṭādaśo ‘dhyāyaḥ |
skandapurāṇe |
sa tam udyānam āsādya devīm āha jagatpatiḥ |
asmin deśe purā devi tiṣṭhato mama śobhane ||
ūrdhvaṃ golokasaṃsthāne gavāṃ vatsaiḥ svayaṃbhavaiḥ |
vegataḥ pīyamānānāṃ pheno mūrdhni mamāpatat ||
tato na paśyati tāś ca gā vatsāḥ somapārśvagāḥ |
tatas tāḥ prekṣitās tatra mayā gāvas tadābhavan ||
tejasā dahyamānās tu naiva karmāstu (?) sādhitāḥ |
gāvaḥ pūrvam imā devi āsan kapilavarṇakāḥ ||
naikavarṇās tadā bhūtvā mayā saṃprekṣitāḥ sadā |
śaraṇaṃ tasya tā jagmur brahmāṇaṃ vibudhottamam ||
agrataḥ samupāgatya kṛtāñjalipuṭas tataḥ |
arditābhis tadā gobhir brahmā mām abravīt tataḥ ||
prasādaṃ kuru vedeśa surabhīṇāṃ sutejasām |
vinaśyeyur yathā nemās tathā kuru surārcitaḥ ||
[131]        tato ‘ham āsthito devi sthāne ‘smin svayam eva tu |
goprekṣaka iti khyātaḥ saṃstutaḥ sarvadevataiḥ ||
gauprekṣeśvaram āgatya dṛṣṭvābhyarcya ca mānavaḥ |
na durgatim avāpnoti kalmaśaiś ca vimucyate ||
tatas tā dahyamānās tu prasavaiḥ surabhī mayā |
hrade ‘smin petur abhyetya śrāntās toyaṃ papus tathā ||
kapilāhrada ity evaṃ tataḥ prabhṛti kathyate |
atrāpi svayam evāhaṃ vṛṣadhvaja iti sthitaḥ ||
sāṃnidhyaṃ kṛtavān devi sadāyaṃ dṛśyatāṃ sthitaḥ |
kapilāhradatīrthe ‘smin snātvā saṃyatamānasaḥ ||
vṛṣadhvajam idaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet |
svarlokatāṃ mṛtaś cāpi arcayitvā tu mām iha ||
labhate dehabhedena gaṇatvaṃ cātidurlabham |
asminn api pradeśe tu tā gāvo brahmaṇā svayam ||
śāntyarthaṃ sarvalokānāṃ sarvā dugdhāḥ payo ‘mṛtam |
tāsāṃ kṣīreṇa saṃjātaṃ hradam etan manoharam ||
bhadradoham iti khyātaṃ puṇyaṃ devahradaṃ śubham |
sarvair devair ahaṃ devi asmin deśe prasāditaḥ ||
gacchopaśamam īśeti upaśāṇtaḥ śivas tataḥ |
śivo bhūtvā ‘ham atrasthaḥ puṇyam asyāpi darśanam ||
dṛṣṭvainaṃ niyato martyaḥ svargalokam avāpnuyāt |
atrāhaṃ brahmaṇānīya sthāpitaḥ parameṣṭhinā ||
[132]        brahmaṇaś cāpi saṃgṛhya viṣṇunā sthāpitaḥ punaḥ |
brahmaṇā sa tato viṣṇuḥ proktaḥ saṃbhinnacetasā ||
mayānītam idaṃ liṅgaṃ kasmāt sthāpitavān asi |
tam uvāca punar viṣṇur brahmāṇaṃ kupitānanam ||
rudre deve mamātyantaṃ parā bhaktir mahattarā |
mayaiva sthāpito ‘pīha nāmnā tava bhaviṣyati ||
hiraṇyagarbha ity evaṃ tatrāhaṃ samavasthitam |
dṛṣṭvainam api deveśi mama loke mahīyate ||
punaś cāpi tato brahmā mama liṅgam idaṃ śubhe |
shtāpayāmāsa vidhivad bhaktyā paramayā punaḥ ||
svarlīneśvara ity evaṃ tatrāhaṃ samavasthitaḥ |
tasmin paratare līne pradhāne mama kāraṇe ||
tasmāt svarlīna ity evaṃ guhyakṣetram iti sthitam |
prāṇān iha naras tyaktvā na punar jāyate kvacit ||
anantā sā gatis tasya yoginām eva yā smṛtā |
asminn api mahādeśe daityo devasya kaṇṭakaḥ ||
vyāghrarūpaṃ samāsthāya nihato darpito balī |
vyāghreśvareti saṃkhyāto nityam atrāham āsthitaḥ ||
na punar durgatiṃ yāti dṛṣṭena sa maheśvaram |
utpalo vidalaś caiva yau daitau brahmaṇaḥ purā ||
strīvadhyau darpitau dṛṣṭvā tvayaiva nihatau śubhe |
sāvajñaṃ kandukenātra tasyedaṃ cihnam āsthitam ||
ādāv atrāham āgatya āsthito gaṇapaiḥ saha |
jyeṣṭhasthānam idaṃ tasmād etan me puṇyadarśanam ||
[133]        dṛṣṭvaivaṃ mama liṅgaṃ tu jyeṣṭhasthānasamāśritam |
na śocate punar martyaḥ siddho janmani janmani ||
samantāc cāpi devair me liṅgāni sthāpitāni ha |
dṛṣṭvā tu niyato martyo dehabhede gaṇo bhavet ||
idānīm aham āgatya svayam asmin vyavasthitaḥ |
na vimuktaṃ mayā yasmād avimuktamidaṃ tataḥ ||
kṣetraṃ vārāṇasīpuṇyaṃ muktidaṃ saṃbhaviṣyati |
avimukteśvaraṃ māṃ vai yo ‘tra drakṣyati mānavaḥ ||
gāṇapatyaṃ gatis tasya yatra tatra mṛtasya hi |
prāṇān hi ca saṃtyajya prāpyate muktir uttamā ||
pitrā te girirājena śaile himavatā svayam |
mama priyaṃ tu tat sthānaṃ jñātvā liṅgaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||
śaileśvaram iti khyātaṃ dṛśyatām iha saṃsthitam |
dṛṣṭvainaṃ manujo devi na durgatim avāpnuyāt ||
nadī vārāṇasī ceyaṃ puṇyā pāpapraṇāśinī |
kṣetram etad alaṃkṛtya jāhnavyā saha saṃgatā ||
sthāpitaṃ saṃgame cāsmin brahmaṇā liṅgam uttamam |
saṃgameśvaram ity evaṃ khyātaṃ jagati dṛśyate ||
saṃgame devanadyoś ca yaḥ snātvā manujaḥ śuciḥ |
arcayet saṃgameśānaṃ tasya janmabhayaṃ kutaḥ ||
sthāpitaṃ liṅgam etac ca śukreṇa tava sūnunā |
nāmnā śukreśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ sarvasiddhāmarārcitam ||
[134]        dṛṣṭvaiva mānavaḥ sadyo muktaḥ syāt sarvakilbiṣaiḥ |
na mṛtaś ca punarjanma saṃsāre tu labhen naraḥ ||
purā jambūkrūpeṇa asuro devakaṇṭakaḥ |
brahmaṇaḥ sa varaṃ labdhvā gomāyur vadhaśaṅkitaḥ ||
hato himavataḥ putri jambukeśas tato hy aham |
anavadyagatiḥ khyātaḥ surāsuranamaskṛtaḥ ||
dṛṣṭvainam api deveśaṃ sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt |
devaiḥ śakrapurogaiś ca etāni sthāpitāni ha ||
pañca liṅgāni puṇyāni sarvakāmapradāni tu |
evam etāni puṇyāni saṃnivāsāsi pārvati ||
kathitāni tava kṣetre guhyaṃ vākyamidaṃ śṛṇu |
mahālayagiristhaṃ māṃ kedāre ca vyavasthitam ||
gaṇatvaṃ labhate dṛṣṭvā kṣetre ‘smin muktir ucyate |
gāṇapatyapadaṃ tasmād yataḥ sā muktir uttamā ||
tato mahālayāt tasmāt kedārān madhyamād api |
smṛtaṃ puṇyatamaṃ kṣetram avimuktam idaṃ śubhe ||
kedāraṃ madhyamaṃ sthānaṃ sthānaṃ caiva mahālayam |
mama puṇyāni bhūloke tebhyaḥ śreṣṭhatamaṃ tv idam ||
mama sṛṣṭās tv ime lokās tv ataḥ kṣetram idaṃ śubhe |
loke vittasamāyuktam avimuktam ato ‘bhavat ||
avimukteśvaraṃ liṅgaṃ mama dṛṣṭveha mānavaḥ |
sadyaḥ pāpavinirmuktaḥ paśupāśair vimucyate ||
[135]        śaileśaṃ saṅgameśaṃ ca svarlīnaṃ madhyameśvaram |
hiraṇyagarbham īśānaṃ goprekṣaṃ vṛṣabhadvajam ||
upaśāntaśivaṃ caiva jyeṣṭhasthānanivāsinam |
śukreśvaraṃ ca vikhyātaṃ vyāghreśaṃ jambukeśvaram ||
dṛṣṭvā na jāyate martyaḥ saṃsāre duḥkhasāgare |
iti bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsaṃdhigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīlakṣmīdharviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe vārāṇasīmāhātmyaṃ samāptam ||
[136]
3
atha prayāgamāhātmyam
tatra matsyapurāṇe |
bhagavan śrotum icchāmi purākalpe yathā śrutam |
[brahmaṇā devamukhyena yathāvat kathitaṃ mune ] ||
kathaṃ prayāgagamanaṃ narāṇāṃ tatra kīdṛśam |
mṛtānāṃ kā gatis tatra snātānāṃ caiva kiṃ phalam |
etat sarvaṃ mamākhyāhi paraṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca |
kathayiṣyāmi te vatsa yac ceṣṭaṃ yac ca tat phalam |
purā ṛṣīṇāṃ viprāṇāṃ kathyamānaṃ mayā śrutam ||
yatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtās te ‘punarbhāvāḥ |
āprayāgapratiṣṭhānād āpurād vāsukerhadāt |
kambalāśvatarau nāgau nāgāc ca bahumūlakāt ||
etat prajāpateḥ kṣetraṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam |
yatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtās te ‘punarbhavāḥ ||
[137]        tatra brahmādayo devā rakṣāṃ kurvanti sarvadā |
anye ca bahavas tīrthāḥ sarvapāpaharāḥ śubhāḥ ||
na śakyāḥ kathituṃ rājan bahuvarṣaśatair api |
saṃkṣepeṇa pravakṣyāmi prayāgasya tu kīrtanam ||
ṣaṣṭir dhanuḥsahasrāṇi yāni rakṣati jāhnavī |
yamunāṃ rakṣati sadā savitā saptavāhanaḥ ||
prayāgaṃ tu viśeṣeṇa svayaṃ rakṣati vāsavaḥ |
maṇḍalaṃ rakṣati harir devataiḥ saha saṃgataḥ ||
taṃ vaṭaṃ rakṣati śivaḥ śūlapāṇir maheśvaraḥ |
sthānaṃ rakṣanti vai devāḥ sarvapāpaharāḥ śubhāḥ ||
adharmeṇāvṛto loko naiva gacchati tat padam |
alpam alpataraṃ pāpaṃ yadā tasya narādhipa ||
prayāgaṃ smaramāṇasya sarvam āyāti saṃkṣayam |
darśanāt tasya tīrthasya nāmasaṃkīrthanād api ||
mṛttikālambhanād vāpi naraḥ pāpāt pramucyate |
pañca kuṇḍāni rājendra yeṣāṃ madhye tu jāhnavī ||
prayāgasya praveśāt tu pāpaṃ naśyati tatkṣaṇāt |
tathā |
satyavādī jitakrodho ahiṃsāṃ paramāsthitaḥ ||
dharmānusārī tattvajño gobrāhmaṇahite rataḥ |
gaṅgāyamunayor madhye snāto mucyeta kilbiṣāt ||
[138]        manasā cintitān kāmāṃs tāṃś ca prāpnoti puṣkalān |
tato gatvā prayāgaṃ tu sarvadevābhirakṣitam ||
brahmacārī vasan māsaṃ pitṝn devāṃś ca tarpayet |
īpsitāṃl lobhate kāmān yatra yatrābhijāyate ||
tapanasya sutā devī tiṣu lokeṣu viśrutā |
samāgatā mahābhāgā yamunā yatra nimnagā ||
yatra saṃnihito devaḥ sākṣād devo maheśvaraḥ |
duṣprāpaṃ mānuṣaiḥ puṇyaṃ prayāgaṃ tu yudhiṣṭhira ||
devadānavagandharvā ṛṣayaḥ siddhacāraṇāḥ |
tatropaspṛśya rājendra svargalokam upāsate ||
tathā |
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca |
śṛṇu rājan prayāgasya māhātmyaṃ punar eva tu |
yac chrutvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
ārtānāṃ ca daridrāṇāṃ niścitavyavasāyinām |
sthānam uktaṃ prayāgaṃ tu nākhyeyaṃ tu kadācana ||
vyādhito yadi vā dīnaḥ kruddho vāpi bhaven naraḥ |
gaṅgāyamunām āsādy yas tu prāṇān parityajet ||
dīptakāñcanavarṇābhair vimānaiḥ sūryavarcasaiḥ |
gandharvāpsarasāṃ madhye svarge modati mānavaḥ ||
[139]        īpsitāṃl labhate kāmān vadanti ṛṣipuṅgavāḥ |
sarvaratnamayair dviyair nānādhvajasamākulaiḥ ||
varāṅganāsamākīrṇavimānaiḥ śubhalakṣaṇaiḥ |
gītavāditranirghoṣaiḥ prasuptaḥ pratibudhyate ||
yāvan na smarate janma tāvat svarge mahīyate |
tataḥ svargāt paribhraṣṭaḥ kṣīṇakarmā divaś cyutaḥ |
hiraṇyaratnasaṃpūrṇe samṛddhe jāyate kule ||
tad eva ca samaret tīrthaṃ smaraṇāt tatra gacchati |
deśastho yadi vāraṇye videśe yati vā gṛhe ||
prayāgaṃ smaramṇo ‘pi yas tu prāṇān parityajet |
brahmalokam avāpnoti vadanti ṛṣipuṅgavāḥ ||
sarvakāmaphalā vṛkṣā mahī yatra hiraṇmayī |
ṛṣayo munayaḥ sarve tatra loke sa gacchati ||
strīsahasrākule ramye mandākinyās taṭe śubhe |
modate ṛṣibhiḥ sārdhaṃ sukṛteneha karmaṇā ||
siddhacāraṇagandharvaiḥ pūjyate divi devataiḥ |
tataḥ svargāt paribhraṣṭo jambūdvīpapatir bhavet ||
tataḥ śubhāni karmāṇi cintayānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ |
guṇavān vittasaṃpanno bhavatīha na saṃśayaḥ ||
karmaṇā manasā vācā satydharmapratiṣṭhitaḥ |
gakgāyamunayor madhye yas tu gāṃ saṃprayacchati ||
sa goromasamābdāni labhate svargam uttamam |
yas tu gāṃ pratigṛhṇāti gaṅgāyamunasaṃgame ||
[140]        suvarṇamaṇimuktāṃ vā yadi vānyaṃ pratigraham |
svakārye pitṛkārye vā devatābhyarcane ‘pi vā ||
viphalaṃ tasya tat tīrthaṃ yāvat tad dhanam aśnute |
tīrthe kṛtapratigrahasya puruṣasya yāvat tīrthapratigrahopārjitadhane svatvam anuvartate, tāvat tīrthadānādiṣu koṭigunatvādilakṣaṇaṃ phalaṃ na janayati | taddhanatyāgāt tu svaphalaṃ janayaty eva |
evaṃ tīrthe na gṛhṇīyāt puṇyeṣv āyataneṣu ca |
nimitteṣu ca sarveṣu apramatto bhaved dvijaḥ ||
kapilāṃ pāṭalāṃ varṇāṃ yas tatraiva prayacchati |
svarṇaśṛṅgīṃ raupyakhurāṃ cailakaṃ gāṃ payasvinīm ||
prayāge śrotriyaṃ sādhuṃ grāhayitvā yathāvidhi |
“grāhayitvā” svīkārya |
śuklāmbaradharaṃ śāntaṃ dharmajñaṃ vedapāragam ||
sā gaus tasyaiva dātavyā gaṅgāyamunasaṃgame |
vāsāṃsi ca mahārhāṇi ratnāni vividhāni ca ||
yāvad romāṇi tasyā gauḥ santi gātreṣu sattama |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||
yatrāsau labhate janma sā gaus tatrābhijāyate |
na ca paśyati taṃ ghoraṃ narakaṃ tena karmaṇā ||
uttarān sa kurūn prāpya modate kālam akṣayam |
gavāṃ śatasahasrebhyo dadyād ekāṃ payasvinīm ||
[141]        putrān dārāṃs tathā bhṛtyān gaur ekā pratikārayet |
tasmāt sarveṣu dāneṣu godānaṃ tu viśiṣyate ||
durgame viṣame ghore mahāpātakasaṃbhame |
gaur eva rakṣāṃ kurute tasmād deyā dvijottame ||
prayāgatīrthayātrāvidhiḥ |
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca |
kathayiṣyāmi te vatsa tīrthayātrāvidhikramam |
ārṣeṇaiva vidhānena yathādṛṣṭaṃ yathāśrutam ||
prayāgatīrthayātrārthī yaḥ prayāti naraḥ kvacit |
balīvardasamārūḍhaḥ śṛṇu tasyāpi yat phalam ||
narake vasate ghore gavāṃ krodho hi dāruṇaḥ |
salilaṃ na gṛhṇanti pitaraś cāsya dehinaḥ ||
yas tu putrāṃs tathā bālān pāyayet snāpayet tathā |
yathātmānaṃ tathā sarvaṃ dānaṃ vipreṣu dāpayet ||
aiśvaryalobhamohād vā gacched yānena yo naraḥ |
niṣphalaṃ tasya tat tīrthaṃ tasmād yānaṃ vivarjayet ||
gaṅgāyamunayor madhye yas tu kanyāṃ prayacchati |
ārṣeṇaiva vidhānena yathā vibhavasaṃbhavam ||
na sa paśyati taṃ ghoraṃ narakaṃ tena karmaṇā |
uttarān sa kurūn gatvā modate kālam akṣayam ||
[142]        putradārāṃś ca labhate dhārmikān rūpasaṃyutān |
tatra dānaṃ pradātavyaṃ yathāvibhavasaṃbhavam ||
tena tīrthaphalaṃ caiva vardhate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
svarge tiṣṭhati rājendra yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam ||
vaṭamūlaṃ samāsādya yas tu prāṇān parityajet |
sarvalokān atikramya rudralokaṃ sa gacchati ||
tatra te dvādaśādityās tapante rudram āśritāḥ |
nirdahanti jagat sarvaṃ vaṭamūlaṃ na dahyate ||
naṣṭacandrārkapavanaṃ yadā caikārṇavaṃ jagat |
svapate tatra vai viṣṇur yatamānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ ||
devadānavagandharvā ṛṣayaḥ siddhacāraṇāḥ |
sadā sevanti taṃ tīrthaṃ gaṅgāyamunasaṃgamam ||
tathā |
tatra brahmādayo devā diśaś ca sadigīśvarāḥ |
hariś ca bhagavān āste prajāpatipuraskṛtaḥ ||
gaṅgāyamunayor madhye pṛthivyā jaghanaṃ smṛtam |
prayāgaṃ rājaśārdūla triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam ||
tataḥ puṇyatamaṃ nāsti triṣu lokeṣu bhārata |
śravaṇāt tasya tīrthasya nāmasaṃkīrtanād api ||
mṛttikālambhanād vāpi naraḥ pāpāt pramucyate |
tatrābhiṣekaṃ yaḥ kuryāt saṃgame saṃśitavrataḥ ||
[143]        tulyaṃ phalam avāpnoti rājasūyāśvamedhayoḥ |
na vedavacanāt tāta na lokavacanād api ||
matir utkramaṇīyā te prayāgamaraṇaṃ prati |
daśatīrthasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭiḥ koṭyas tathāparāḥ ||
yeṣāṃ sāṃnidhyam atraiva kīrtanāt kurunandana |
yā gatir yogayuktasya sattvasthasya ca dhīmataḥ ||
sā gatis tyajataḥ prāṇān gaṅgāyamunasaṃgame |
na te jīvanti loke ‘smin yatra yatra yudhiṣṭhira ||
ye prayāgaṃ na saṃprāptās triṣu lokeṣu vañcitāḥ |
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā tu tat tīrthaṃ prayāgaṃ paramaṃ padam ||
mucyate sarvapāpebhyaḥ śaśāṅga iva rāhuṇā |
kambalāśvatarau nāgau yamunādakṣiṇe taṭe |
tatra snātvā ca pītvā ca sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
tatra gatvā tu saṃsthānaṃ mahādevasya dhīmataḥ |
naras tārayate pūrvaṃ daśātītān daśāvarān ||
kṛtvābhiṣekaṃ tu naraḥ so ‘śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet |
svargalokam avāpnoti yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam ||
pūrvapārśve tu gaṅgāyās trirātraṃ yadi tiṣṭhati |
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā so ‘śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
uttareṇa pratiṣṭhānād bhāgīrathyās tu pūrvataḥ |
haṃsapratapanaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ trailokyaviśrutam ||
[144]        aśvamedhaphalaṃ tasmin snānamātreṇa bhārata |
yāvac candraś ca sūryaś ca tāvat svarge mahīyate ||
ūrvaśīpuline ramye vipule haṃsapāṇḍare |
parityajyati yaḥ prāṇān śṛṇu tasyāpi yat phalam ||
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭivarṣaśatāni ca |
sa yāti pitṛbhiḥ sārdhaṃ svargaloke narādhipa ||
ūrvaśīṃ ca sadā paśyed devaloke narottama |
pūjyate satataṃ devair ṛṣigandharvakinnaraiḥ ||
tataḥ svargāt paribhraṣṭaḥ kṣīṇakarmā divaś cyutaḥ |
ūrvaśīsadṛśīnāṃ tu kanyānāṃ labhate śatam ||
madhye nārīsahasrāṇāṃ bahvīnāṃ ca patir bhavet |
daśagrāmasahasrāṇāṃ bhoktā bhavati bhūmipaḥ ||
kāñcīnūpuraśabdena supto ‘sau pratibudhyate ||
bhuktvā tu vipulān bhogāṃs tat tīrthaṃ labhate punaḥ |
śuklāmbaradharo nityaṃ niyataḥ saṃyatendriyaḥ ||
ekakālaṃ tu bhuñjāno māṃsaṃ bhogapatir bhavet |
suvarṇālaṃkṛtānāṃ tu nārīṇāṃ labhate śatam ||
pṛthivyām āsamudrāyāṃ mahābhogapatir bhavet |
dhanadhānyasamāyukto dātā bhavati nityaśaḥ ||
sa bhuktvā vipulān bhogāṃs tat tīrthaṃ smarate punaḥ |
atha saṃdhyāvaṭe ramye brahmacārī jitendriyaḥ ||
upāsīta śucir bhūtvā brahmalokam avāpnuyāt |
koṭitīrthaṃ samāsādya yas tu prāṇān parityajet ||
[145]        koṭivarṣasahasrāṇāṃ svargaloke mahīyate |
tataḥ svargāt paribhraṣṭaḥ kṣīṇakarmā divaś cyutaḥ ||
suvarṇamaṇimuktādyaiḥ kule jāyeta rūpavān |
tato bhogavatīṃ gatvā vāsuker uttareṇa tu ||
daśāśvamedhikaṃ nāma tatra tīrthe paraṃ bhavet |
kṛtābhiṣekas tu naraḥ so ‘śvamedhaphalaṃ labet ||
dhanāḍhyo rūpavān sākṣād dātā bhavati dhārmikaḥ |
caturvedeṣu yat puṇyaṃ satyavādiṣu yat phalam ||
ahiṃsāyāṃ tu yo dharmo gamanād eva tat phalam |
tathā |
mānasaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ tu gaṅgāyā uttare taṭe |
trirātropoṣitaḥ snātvā sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt ||
gobhūhiraṇyadānena yat phalaṃ prāpnuyān naraḥ |
tat phalaṃ samavāpnoti tat tīrthaṃ smarate punaḥ ||
akāmo vā sakāmo vā gaṅgāyāṃ yo vipadyate |
mṛtas tu labhate svargaṃ narakaṃ na ca paśyati |
apsarogaṇasaṃgītaiḥ supto ‘sau pratibudhyate ||
haṃsasārasayuktena vimānena sa gacchati |
bahuvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaṃ rājendra bhuñjate ||
tataḥ svargāt paribhraṣṭaḥ kṣīṇakarmā divaś cyutaḥ |
suvarṇamaṇimuktāḍhye jāyate vipule kule ||
[146]        ṣaṣṭitīrthasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭitīrthaśatāni ca |
māghamāse gamiṣyanti gaṅgāyamunasaṃgame ||
gavāṃ śatasahasrasya samyagdattasya yat phalam |
prayāge māghamāse ca tyahaṃ snātasya tat phalam ||
gaṅgāyamunayor madhye karṣāgniṃ yas tu sādhayet |
ahīnāṅgo hy arogaś ca pañcendriyasamanvitaḥ ||
yāvanti romakūpāni tasya gātreṣu dhīmataḥ |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||
tataḥ svargāt paribhraṣṭo jambūdvīpapatir bhavet |
sa bhuktvā vipulān bhogāṃs tat tīrthaṃ labhate punaḥ ||
jalapraveśaṃ yat kuryāt saṃgame lokaviśrute |
rāhugrate yathā some vimuktaḥ sarvapātakaiḥ ||
somalokam avāpnoti somena saha modate |
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭivarṣaśatāni ca ||
svargalokam avāpnoti ṛṣigandharvasevitaḥ |
paribhraṣṭas tu rājendra samṛddhe jāyate kule ||
adhaḥśirās tu yo jvālām ūrdhvapādaḥ piben naraḥ |
śataṃ varṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||
paribhraṣṭas tu rājendra agnihotrī bhaven naraḥ |
bhuktvā tu vipulān bhogāṃs tat tīrthaṃ bhajate punaḥ ||
yaś ca dehaṃ vikartitvā śakunibhyaḥ prayacchati |
vihagair upabhuktasya śṛṇu tasyāpi yat phalam ||
[147]        śataṃ varṣasahasrāṇāṃ somaloke mahīyate |
tasmād api paribhraṣṭo rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ ||
guṇavān rūpasaṃpanno vidvāṃś ca priyavādakaḥ |
bhuktvā tu vipunān bhogāṃs tat tīrthaṃ labhate punaḥ ||
yāmune cottare kūle prayāgasya tu dakṣiṇe |
ṛṇapramocanaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ tu paramaṃ smṛtam ||
ekarātropiṣitaḥ snātvā ṛṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ pramucyate |
svargalokam avāpnoti anṛṇaś ca sadā bhavet ||
tathā |
śṛṇu rājan prayāge tu anāśakaṃ phalaṃ vibho |
prāpnoti puruṣo dhīmān śraddadhāno jitendriyaḥ ||
ahīnāṅgo hy arogaś ca pañcendiryasamanvitaḥ |
aśvamedhaphalaṃ tasya gacchatas tu pade pade ||
kulāni tārayed rājan daśa pūrvān daśāvarān |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo gaccheta paramaṃ padam ||
tathā |
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca |
śṛṇu rājan mahāvīra yad uktaṃ padmayoninā |
ṛṣīṇāṃ saṃnidhau pūrvaṃ kathyamānaṃ mayā śubham ||
pañcayojanavistīrṇaṃ prayāgasya tu maṇḍalam |
praveśāt tasya tadbhūmau so ‘śvamedhaḥ pade pade ||
[148]        vyatītān puruṣān sapta bhaviṣyāṃś ca caturdaśa |
naras tārayate sarvān yas tu prāṇān parityajet ||
evaṃ jñātvā tu rājendra sadā śraddhāparo bhava |
aśraddadhānāḥ puruṣāḥ pāpopahatacetasaḥ ||
na prāpnuvanti tat sthānaṃ prayāgaṃ devarakṣitam |
yudhiṣṭhira uvāca |
snehād vā dravyalobhād vā ye tu kāmavaśaṃ gatāḥ |
kathaṃ tīrthaphalaṃ teṣāṃ kathaṃ puṇyam avāpnuyuḥ ||
vikrayī sarvabhāṇḍānāṃ kāryākāryam ajānataḥ |
prayāge kā gatis tasya etad bhrūhi mahāmune ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca |
śṛṇu rājan mahāguhyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
māsam ekaṃ tu snāyīta prayāge niyatendriyaḥ ||
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo mayoddiṣṭaṃ svayaṃbhuvā |
śucis tu prayato bhūtvā ‘hiṃsakaḥ śraddhayānvitaḥ ||
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo sa gacchet paramaṃ padam |
viśvastaghātakānāṃ ca prayāge śṛṇu yat phalam ||
trikālaṃ eva snāyīta āhāraṃ bhaikṣam ācaret |
tribhir māsaiḥ pramucyeta prayāge nāntra saṃśayaḥ ||
ajñānena tu yasyeha tīrthayātrādikaṃ bhavet |
[149]        sarvakāmasamṛddhas tu svargaloke mahīyate ||
sthānaṃ ca labhate nityaṃ dhanadhānyasamākulam |
evaṃ jñānena saṃpūrṇaḥ sadā bhavati bhogavān ||
tāritāḥ pitaras tena narakāt sapitāmahāḥ |
tathā |
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca |
tapanasya sutā devī triṣu lokeṣu viśrutā |
samāgatā mahābhāgā yamunā yatra nimnagā ||
yenaiva niḥsṛtā gaṅgā tenaiva yamunā gatā |
yojanānāṃ sahasreṣu kīrtanāt pāpanāśinī ||
tatra sanātvā ca pītvā ca yamunāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira |
kīrtanāl labhate puṇyaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhadrāṇi paśyati ||
avagāhyātha pītvā ca punāty āsaptamaṃ kulam |
prāṇāṃs tyajati yas tatra sa yāti paramāṃ gatim ||
agnitīrtham iti khyātaṃ yamunādakṣiṇe taṭe |
paścime dharmarājasya tīrthaṃ tu navakaṃ smṛtam ||
tatra snātvā divaṃ yāti ye mṛtās te ‘punarbhavāḥ |
pañca tīrthasahasrāṇi yamunādakṣiṇe taṭe ||
uttareṇa pravakṣyāmi ādityasya mahātmanaḥ |
tīrthaṃ nirūjakaṃ nāma yatra devāḥ savāsavāḥ ||
[150]        upāsate sadā saṃdhyāṃ nityaṃ kāle yudhiṣṭhira |
devāḥ sevanti tat tīrthaṃ ye cānye viduṣo janāḥ ||
śraddadhānaparo bhūtvā kurutīrthābhiṣecanam |
anye ca bahavas tīrthāḥ sarvapāpaharāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
teṣu snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtās te ‘punarbhavāḥ |
gaṅgā ca yamunā caiva ubhe tulyaphale smṛte |
kevalaṃ jyeṣṭhabhāvena gaṅgā sarvatra pūjyate ||
evaṃ kuruṣva kaunteya sarvatīrthābhiṣecanam |
yāvajjīvakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇād eva naśyati ||
yas tv idaṃ kalyam utthāya paṭhate ca śṛṇoti ca |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyaḥ svargalokaṃ sa gacchati ||
tathā |
janmāntarasahasrebhyo yogo labhyeta vā na vā |
tathā yugasahsreṇa yogo labhyeta mānavaiḥ ||
yas tu sarvāṇi ratnāni brāhmaṇebhyaḥ prayacchati |
tena dānena dattena yogo labheta vā na vā |
prayāge tu mṛtasyedaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati nānyathā ||
tathā |
śṛṇu rājan pravakṣyāmi māhātmyaṃ punar eva tu |
naimiśaṃ puṣkaraṃ caiva gotīrthaṃ sindhusāgaram ||
gayā ca dhenukaṃ caiva gaṅgāsāgaram eva ca |
ete cānye ca bahavo ye ca puṇyāḥ śiloccayāḥ ||
[151]        daśatīrthasahasrāṇi tisraḥ koṭyas tathāparāḥ |
prayāge saṃsthitā nityam evam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
trīṇi cāpy agnikuṇḍāni yeṣāṃ madhye tu jāhnavī |
prayāgād abhiniṣkrāntā sarvatīrthapuraskṛtā ||
tapanasya sutā devī triṣu lokeṣu viśrutā |
gaṅgayā yamunāsārdhaṃ saṃgatā lokatāriṇī ||
gaṅgāyamunayor madhye pṛthivyāṃ jaghanaṃ smṛtam |
prayāgaṃ rājaśārdūla kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm ||
tisraḥ koṭy ardhakoṭī ca tīrthānāṃ vāyur abravīt |
divi bhuvy antarikṣe ca tat sarvaṃ jāhnavī smṛtā ||
prayāgaṃ sapratiṣṭhānaṃ kambalāśvatarāv ubhau |
bhogavaty atha yā caiṣā vedir eṣā prajāpateḥ ||
yatra vedāś ca yajñāś ca mūrtimanto yudhiṣṭhira |
prajāpatim upāsante ṛṣayaś ca tapodhanāḥ ||
yajante kratubhir vedās tathā cakradharā nṛpāḥ |
tataḥ puṇyatamo nāsti tiṣu lokeṣu bhārata ||
prayāge sarvatīrthebhyaḥ prabhavaty adhikaṃ vibho |
tathā |
pañcayojanavistīrṇaṃ prayāgasya tu maṇḍalam |
tiṣṭhate rakṣiṇo yatra pāpakarmanivāraṇāt ||
uttareṇa pratiṣṭhānaṃ śālmalī pratitiṣṭhati |
maheśvaro vaṭo bhūtvā tiṣṭhate parameśvaraḥ ||
[152]        tato devāḥ sagandharvāḥ siddhāś ca paramarṣayaḥ |
rakṣanti maṇḍalaṃ sarvaṃ pāpakarmanivāraṇāt ||
yuñjan yuñjan svakaṃ pāpaṃ narakaṃ na ca paśyati |
evaṃ brahmā ca viṣṇuś ca prayāge nityam īśvaraḥ ||
tathā |
sitāsiteṣu yat snānaṃ māghase yudhiṣṭhira |
na teṣāṃ punar āvṛttiḥ kalpakoṭiśatair api ||
daśatīrthasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭyas tathāparāḥ |
māghamāse prayāge tu gaṅgāyamunasaṃgame ||
prayāgamāhātmye mahābhārate |
tataḥ puṇyatamaṃ nāsti tiṣu lokeṣu bhārata |
prayāgaṃ sarvatīrthebhyo prabhavaty adhikaṃ vibho ||
śravaṇāt tasya tīrthasya nāmasaṃkīrtanād api |
[mṛttikālambhanād vāpi naraḥ pāpāt pramucyate ||
tatrābhiṣekaṃ yaḥ kuryāt saṃgame saṃśitavrataḥ |
tulyaṃ phalam avāpnoti rājasūyāśvamedhayoḥ ||
tulyaṃ phalam avāpnoti rājasūyāśvamedhayoḥ |
eṣā yajanabhūmir hi devānām api satkṛtā ||
yatra dattaṃ sūkṣmam api mahad bhavati bhārata |
na vedavacanād vāpi na lokavacanād api || ]
matir utkramaṇīyā te prayāgamaraṇaṃ prati |
daśatīrthasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭyas tathāparāḥ ||
[153]        yeṣāṃ sāṃnidhyam atraiva kīrtitaṃ kurunandana |
caturvedeṣu yat puṇyaṃ satyavādiṣu caiva yat ||
snāta eva tad avāpnoti gaṅgāyamunasaṃgame |
tatra bhogavatī nāma vāsukes tīrtham uttamam ||
tatrābhiṣekaṃ yaḥ kuryāt so ‘śvamedham avāpnuyāt |
tatra haṃsapratapanaṃ tīrthaṃ trailokyaviśrutam ||
daśāśvamedhikaṃ caiva gaṅgāyāṃ kurunandana |
yatra gaṅgā mahārāja sa deśas tu tapovanam ||
siddhikṣetraṃ hi taj jñeyaṃ gaṅgātīram āśritam ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe prayāgamāhātmyam |
[154]
4
atha gaṅgāmāhātmyam
tatra mahābhārate |
śilavṛttir uvāca |
ke deśāḥ ke janapadāḥ ke grāmāḥ ke ca parvatāḥ |
prakṛṣṭāḥ puṇyatamakā jñeyā nadyas tad ucyatām ||
siddha uvāca |
te deśās te janapadās te grāmās te ca parvatāḥ |
yeṣāṃ bhāgīrathī gaṅgā madhye yāti saridvarā ||
tapasā brahmacaryeṇa yajñais tyāgena vā punaḥ |
gatiṃ na labhate gantur gaṅgāṃ saṃsevya tāṃ labhet ||
spṛṣṭāni yeṣāṃ gāṅgeyais toyair gātrāṇi dehinām |
vyastāni ye punas teṣāṃ vāsaḥ svargād vidhīyate ||
sarvāṇi yeṣāṃ gāṅgeyais toyaiḥ karmāṇi dehinām |
gāṃ tyaktvā mānavā vipra divi tiṣṭhanti te janāḥ ||
pūrve vayasi karmāṇi kṛtvā pāpāni ye narāḥ |
paścād gaṅgāṃ niṣevante te ‘pi yānty uttamāṃ gatim ||
[155]        snātānāṃ śucibhis toyair gāṅgeyaiḥ prayatātmanām |
vyuṣṭir bhavati yā puṃsāṃ na sā kratuśatair api ||
yāvad asthi manuṣyāṇāṃ gaṅgātoyeṣu tiṣṭhati |
tāvad varṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||
apahatya tamas tīvraṃ yathā bhāty udaye raviḥ |
tathāpahatya pāpmānaṃ bhāti gaṅgājalokṣitaḥ ||
tathā |
triṣu lokeṣu ye kecit prāṇinaḥ sarva eva te |
tarpyamāṇāḥ parāṃ tṛptiṃ yānti gaṅgājalaiḥ subhaiḥ ||
yas tu sūryeṇa niṣṭaptaṃ gāṅgeyaṃ pibate jalam |
gavāṃ nirhāranirmuktā yāvatkāśaviśiṣyate ||
induvratasahasraṃ tu yaś caret kāyaśodhanam |
pibed yaś cāpi gaṅgāmbhaḥ samau syātāṃ na vā samau ||
[tiṣṭhed yugasahasraṃ tu pādenaikena yaḥ pumān |
māsam ekaṃ tu gaṅgāyāṃ samau syātāṃ na vā samau || ]
lambetāvakśirā yas tu yugānām ayutaṃ pumān |
tiṣṭhed yatheṣṭaṃ yaś cāpi gaṅgāyāṃ sa viśiṣyate ||
agnau prāptaṃ pradhūyante yathā tūlaṃ dvijottama |
tathā gaṅgāvagāḍhasya sarvapāpaṃ pradhūyate ||
bhūtānām api sarveṣāṃ duḥkhopahatacetasām |
gatim anveṣamāṇānāṃ na gaṅgāsadṛśī gatiḥ ||
[156]        bhavanti nirviṣāḥ sarpāḥ yathā tārkṣyasya darśanāt |
gaṅgāyā darśanāt tadvat sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
apratiṣṭhāś ca ye kecid aśarmaśaraṇāś ca ye |
teṣāṃ pratiṣṭhā gaṅgeha śaraṇaṃ śarma marma ca ||
prakṛṣṭair aśubhair grastastān anekaiḥ puruṣādhamān |
patato narake gaṅgā saṃśritān pretya tārayet ||
teṣāṃ vimuktā munibhir nūnaṃ devaiḥ savāsavaiḥ |
ye ‘bhigacchanti satataṃ gaṅgāṃ matimatāṃ vara ||
vinayācārahīnāś ca aśivāś ca narādhamāḥ |
te bhavanti śivā viprā ye vai gaṅgāsamāśritāḥ ||
tathā |
jāhnavītīrasaṃbhūtāṃ mṛdaṃ mūrdhnā bibharti yaḥ |
bibharti rūpaṃ so ‘rkasya tamonāśāya kevalam ||
tathā |
vāṅmanaḥkarmabhir grastaḥ pāpair api pumān iha |
vīkṣya gaṅgāṃ bhavet pūtas tatra me nāsti saṃśayaḥ ||
saptāvarān sapta parān pitṝn ebhyaś ca ye pare |
pumāṃs tārayate gaṅgāṃ vīkṣya spṛṣṭvāvagāhya ca ||
[gaṅgāṃ dṛṣṭvātha pāpī ca snātvā gaṅgeti kīrtanāt |
trayodaśārjitaṃ pāpaṃ janmajaṃ naśyati dhruvam || ]
[157]        śrutābhilapitā dṛṣṭā spṛṣṭā pītāvagāhitā |
gaṅgā tārayate puṃsām ubhau vaṃśau viśeṣataḥ ||
darśanāt sparśanāt snānāt tathā gaṅgeti kīrtanāt |
punāty apuṇyān puruṣān śataśo ‘tha sahasraśaḥ ||
ya icchet saphalaṃ janma jīvitaṃ śrutam eva ca |
sa pitṝṃs tarpayed gaṅgām abhigamya surāṃs tathā ||
na sutair ca vittena karmaṇā na ca tat phalam |
prāpnuyāt puruṣo ‘tyantaṃ gaṅgāṃ prāpto yadāpnuyāt ||
jātyandhair iha tulyās te mūkaiḥ paṅgubhir eva ca |
samarthā yena paśyanti gaṅgāṃ puṇyajalāṃ śubhām ||
bhūtabhavyabhaviṣajñair maharṣibhir upasthitām |
devaiḥ sendraiś ca ko gaṅgāṃ nopaseveta mānavaḥ ||
vānaprasthair gṛhasthaiś ca yatibhir brahmacāribhiḥ |
vidyāvadbhiḥ śritāṃ gaṅgāṃ pumān ko nāma nāśrayet ||
utkrāmadbhiś ca yat prāṇaiḥ prayataḥ śiṣṭhasaṃmataḥ |
cintayen manasā gaṅgāṃ sa gatiṃ paramāṃ labhet ||
na bhayebhyo bhayaṃ tasya na pāpebhyo na rājataḥ |
ā dehapatād gaṅgām upāsate yaḥ pumān iha ||
tahā |
tadbhāvas tadgatamānās tanniṣṭhas tatparāyaṇaḥ |
gaṅgāṃ yo ‘nugato bhaktyā sa tasyāḥ priyatāṃ vrajet ||
bhūsthaiḥ svasthair divasthaiś ca bhūtair uccāvacair ‘pi |
gaṅgāvagābhyā satatam etat kāryasamaṃ satām ||
[158] tathā |
gaṅgāṃ gatvā yaiḥ śarīraṃ visṛṣṭaṃ
gatā dhīrās te vibudhaiḥ samatvam |
andhān jaḍān dravyahīnāṃś ca gaṅgā
yaśasvinī bṛhatī viśvarūpā ||
devaiḥ sendrair munibhir mānuṣaiś ca
niṣevitā sarvakāmair yunakti ||
ujjīvantīṃ mahāpuṇyām aṃśumantīṃ trivartmagām |
trilokagoptrīṃ ye gaṅgāṃ saṃśritās te divaṃgatāḥ ||
yo vatsyate drakṣyati vāpi martya
tasmai prayacchati sukhāni devāḥ |
andhā klībā jaḍā vyaṅgā patitā rogiṇo ‘ntyajāḥ |
gaṅgāṃ saṃsevya puruṣā davair gacchanti tulyatām ||
dūrastho ‘pi hi brūyāt trisaṃdhyaṃ niyataḥ śuciḥ |
gaṅgā gaṅgeti gaṅgeti sa gacchet paramāṃ gatim ||
jaḍāndhaprabhṛtayaḥ karmāntarānadhikāriṇo ‘pi gaṅgāsevayābhimataphalabhāgino bhavantīty arthaḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇo yadvat tathā tīrtheṣu jāhnavī |
svargasthair gaganasthaiś ca tathā pātālavāsibhiḥ ||
avagāhyā sadā gaṅgā pūjyā vandyā ca sarvadā |
ṣaṣṭyādau kṛṣṇapakṣe tu bhūmau saṃnihitā bhavet ||
[159]        yāvat puṇyām amāvāsyāṃ dināni daśa nityaśaḥ |
śuklapratipad ārabhya dināni daśasaṃkhyayā ||
pātāle saṃnidhānaṃ tu karoti svayam eva hi |
śuklaikādaśikād ahna ārabhya daś yāni tu ||
pañcamyantāni sā svarge bhavet saṃnihitā sadā |
śrutābhilapitā dṛṣṭā spṛṣṭā pītāvagāhitā ||
gaṅgā tārayate nṝṇām ubhau vaṃśau bhavārṇavāt |
darśanāt sparśanāt pānāt tathā gaṅgeti kīrtanāt ||
punāty apuṇyān puruṣān māteva jagato hitā |
aśubhaiḥ karmabhir grastān majjamānān bhavārṇave ||
patato narake gaṅgāṃ saṃśritān uddharet sadā |
avagāhanamātreṇa mānavānāṃ sadaiva hi ||
pāpaughaṃ nirdahed gaṅgā tūlarāśim ivānalaḥ ||
tathā |
vinayācārahīnāś ca ye bhavanty aśubhā narāḥ |
gaṅgām āśritya sarve te bhavanti vimalāḥ śubhāḥ ||
[tathā] |
na putrair na mahādānair na cānyaiś ca saukarmabhiḥ |
bhavet puṇyaphalaṃ puṃsāṃ yathā labdhvānujāhnavīm ||
matsyapurāṇe |
kurukṣetrasamā gaṅgā yatra tatrāvagāhitā |
[160]        kurukṣetrād daśaguṇā yatra vindhyena saṃgatā ||
vindhyāc chataguṇā proktā kāśipuryāṃ tu jāhnavī |
yatra gaṅgā mahābhāgā bahutīrthatapodhanā ||
siddhikṣetraṃ hi taj jñeyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
kṣitau tārayate martyān nāgāṃs tārayate ‘py adhaḥ ||
divi tārayate devāṃs tena tripathagā smṛtā |
yāvad asthīni tiṣṭhanti gaṅgāyāṃ yasya dehinaḥ ||
tāvad varṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate |
tīrthānāṃ paramaṃ tīrthe nadīnāṃ paramā nadī ||
mokṣadā sarvabhūtānāṃ mahāpātakinām api |
sarvatra sulabhā gaṅgā triṣu sthāneṣu durlabhā ||
gaṅgādvāre prayāge ca gaṅgāsāgarasaṃgame |
te ‘tra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtās te ‘punarbhavāḥ ||
sarveṣām eva bhūtānāṃ tāpopahanacetasām |
gatim anveṣamāṇānāṃ nāsti gaṅgāsamā gatiḥ ||
pavitrāṇām pavitraṃ tu maṅgalānāṃ ca maṅgalam |
maheśvaraśirodubhūtā sarvapāpaharā śubhā ||
tathā |
yojanānāṃ sahasreṣu gaṅgāṃ yaḥ smarate naraḥ |
api duṣkṛtakarmāsau labhate paramāṃ gatim ||
kīrtanān mucyate pāpād dṛṣṭvā bhadrāṇi paśyati |
avagāhya ca pītvā ca punāty āsaptamaṃ kulam ||
[161] viṣṇupurāṇe |
tataḥ prabhavati [prabhṛti?] brahman sarvapāpaharā sarit |
gaṅgā devāṅganāṅgānām anulepanapiñjarā ||
vāmapādāmbujāṅguṣṭhanakhasrotovinirgatā |
viṣṇor bibharti yāṃ bhaktyā śirasāharniśaṃ śivaḥ ||
tataḥ saptarṣayo yasyāṃ prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇāḥ |
tiṣṭhanti vīcimālābhir uhyamānā jaṭā jale ||
vāyor vairāhatair yasyāḥ plāvitaṃ śaśimaṇḍalam |
bhūyo ‘dhikaratām kāntiṃ vahaty etad ahaḥkṣaye ||
merupṛṣṭhe pataty uccair niṣkrāntā śaśimaṇdalāt |
jagataḥ pāvanārthāya prayāti ca caturdiśam ||
śītā cālakanandā ca cakṣur bhadrā ca saṃsthitā |
ekaiva yā caturbhedhā digbhedagatilakṣaṇā ||
bhedaṃ cālakanandākhyaṃ yasyāḥ sarvopadakṣiṇam |
dadhāra śirasā prītyā varṣāṇām adhikaṃ śatam ||
śambhor jaṭākalāpāc ca niṣkrāntā asthiśarkarā |
plavayitvā divaṃ nityaṃ yā pāpān sagarātmajān ||
snātasya salile yasyāḥ sadyaḥ pāpaṃ praṇaśyati |
apūrvapuṇyaprāptiś ca sadyo maitreya jāyate ||
dattāḥ pitṛbhyo yatrāpas tanayaiḥ śraddhayānvitaiḥ |
samātrayaṃ prayacchanti tṛptiṃ maitreya durlabhām ||
yasyām iṣṭvā mahāyajñair yajñeśaṃ puruṣottamam |
dvija bhūpāḥ parāṃ siddhim avāpur divi ceha ca ||
[162]        snātā nirdhūtapāpāś ca yaj jale yat tapas tathā |
keśavāsaktamanasaḥ prāptā nirvāṇam uttamam ||
stutābhilaṣitā dṛṣṭā spṛṣṭā pītāvagāhitā |
yā pāvayati bhūtāni kīrtitāni dine dine ||
gaṅgā gaṅgeti yair nāma yojanānāṃ śatair api |
sthitair uccaritaṃ hanti pāpaṃ janmatrayārjitam ||
tataḥ sā pāvanāyātra jagatāṃ tisṛṇām api |
samudbhūtā paraṃ tat tu tṛtīyaṃ bhavatpadam ||
tathā |
bhagavadviṣṇupādāṅguṣṭhavinirgatasya hi jalasyaitanmāhātmyamayaṃ na kevalam abhisaṃdhipūrvakaṃ snānādyupayogeṣūpakārakam | anabhisaṃhitam apy apetaprāṇasyāsthicarmasnāyukeśādyutsṛṣṭaṃ śarīrajam api tadupanipatitaṃ sadyaḥ śarīriṇaṃ svargaṃ nayat |
mahābhārate |
meroḥ samudrasya ca sarvaratnaiḥ
saṃkhyopalānām udakasya vāpi |
śakyaṃ vaktuṃ neha gaṅgājalānāṃ
guṇākhyānaṃ parimāṇaṃ tathaiva ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīlakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe gaṅgāmāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
5
atha gayāmāhātmyam
tatra matsyapurāṇe |
pitṛtīrthaṃ gayā nāma sarvatīrthavaraṃ śubham |
yatrāste devadeveśaḥ svayam eva pitāmahaḥ ||
yatraiṣā pitṛbhir gītā gāthā bhogam abhīpsubhiḥ |
“eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā yady eko ‘pi gayāṃ vrajet |
yajeta vāśvamedhena nīlaṃ vā vṛṣam utsṛjet” ||
varāhapurāṇe |
sanatkumāra uvāca |
dhanyas tvam eva dvijamukhyavarya
yad vedavādābhirataḥ pitṝṃś ca |
prīṇāti mantravratahomajapyair
gayāṃ samāsādya tathānupiṇḍaiḥ ||
śṛṇuṣva vākyaṃ nṛpatir babhūva
viśālanāmā sa purīṃ viśālām |
uvāsa dhanyāṃ dhṛtimān aputraḥ
svayaṃ viśālādhipatir dvijāgryām ||
[164]        papraccha putrārtham amitrahantā
taṃ brāhmaṇāś cocur adīnasattvāḥ |
rājan pitṝṃs tarpaya putrahetor
datvā gayāyāṃ vidhivat tu piṇḍān ||
dhruvaṃ sutas te bhavitā nṛvīra
sahasradātā sakalakṣitīśaḥ |
itīrite brāhmaṇaiḥ saṃprahṛṣṭo
rājā viśālādhipatiḥ prayatnāt ||
samāgatas tīrthavaraṃ dvijeśa
gayām imāṃ tadgatamānasaḥ san |
āgatya so ‘tha pravaraṃ sutārthī
gayāśiro yāgaparaḥ pitṝṇām ||
piṇḍapradānaṃ vidhinā prayacchaṃs
tāvad viyaty uttamamūrtiyuktān|
paśyan sa puṃsaḥ sitaraktakṛṣṇān
uvāca rājā kim idaṃ bhavadbhiḥ ||
samīkṣyate śaṃsata sarvam etat
kutūhalaṃ me manasi pravṛttam |
sita uvāca |
ahaṃ sitas te janako ‘smi tāta
nāmnā ca vṛttena ca karmaṇā ca |
ayaṃ ca me janako raktavarṇo
nṛśaṃsakṛd brahmahā pāpakārī ||
[165]        adhīśvaraḥ śṛṇu paraḥ pitāsya
kṛṣṇo nāmnā karmaṇā varṇataś ca |
etena kṛṣṇena hataḥ purā vai
janmāny anekāny ṛṣayaḥ purāṇāḥ ||
tathā |
etau mṛtau dvāv api pitṛputrāv
avīcisaṃjñaṃ narakaṃ prapannau |
adhīśvaro ‘yaṃ janakaḥ paro ‘sya
tau kṛṣṇaraktāv api dīrghakālam ||
ahaṃ ca śuddhena nijena karmaṇā
śakrāsanaṃ prāpya sudurlabhaṃ tat |
tvayā punar mantravidā gayāyāṃ
piṇḍapradānena balād imau ca ||
mokṣāpitau tīrthavaraprabhāvād
avīcisaṃjñaṃ narakaṃ gatau tau |
pitṝn pitāmahāṃś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahān |
prīṇayāmīti yat toyaṃ tvayā dattam arindama ||
tenāsmadyugapad yogo jāto vākyena sattama |
tīrthaprabhāvāḍ gacchāmaḥ pitṛlokaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||
tatra piṇḍapradānena etau tava pitāmahau |
“pitāmahau” pitāmahaprapitāmahau |
[166]        durgatāv api saṃsiddhau pāpād vikṛtiliṅginau ||
tīrthaprabhāva eṣo ‘tra brahmaghnasyāpi sattama |
pituḥ piṇḍapradānena kuryād uddharaṇaṃ sutaḥ ||
etasmāt kāraṇāt putra aham etau vigṛhya tu |
āgato ‘smi bhavantaṃ vai draṣṭuṃ yāsyāmi sāṃpratam ||
“vigṛhya” gṛhītvā |
tathā |
etasmāt kāraṇād raibhya bhavān dhanyo mayocyate |
sakṛd gayābhigamanaṃ sakṛt piṇḍaprapātanam ||
durlabhatvaṃ punar nityam asminn eva vyavasthitiḥ |
kim atra procyate raibhya tava puṇyam iti prabho ||
yena śārṅgagadāpāṇir dṛṣṭo nārāyaṇaḥ svayam |
yato gadādharaḥ sākṣād asmiṃs tīrthe vyavasthitaḥ ||
ato hi vikhyātatamaṃ tīrtham etad dvijottama |
brahmapurāṇe |
[kiyat te patitānāṃ ca gate saṃvatsare kvacit |
deśadharmapramāṇātvād gayākūpe svayaṃ bhuvi ||
mārtaṇḍapādamūle vā śrāddhaṃ hariharaṃ smaran |
vāmanapurāṇe ] |
pretarājo vaṇijaṃ kiṃcit pratyāha |
gayāyāṃ tīrthajuṣṭāyāṃ snātvā śaucasamanvitaḥ |
[167]        mama nāma samuddiśya piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kuru ||
tatra piṇḍapradānena pretabhāvād ahaṃ sakhe |
muktas tu sarvadraṣṭṝṇāṃ yāsyāmi śubhalokatām ||
ity evam uktvā vaṇijaṃ pretarājo ‘nujaiḥ saha |
svanāmāni yathānyāyaṃ samyag ākhyātavāñ chuciḥ ||
