tathārcayitvā prayayau gayāśiram anuttamam |
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ tatra pretānām anupūrvaśaḥ ||
cakāra ca sadā prāṇān pitṝṃś cakre tv anantaram |
ātmanaś ca mahābuddhir mahābodhau tilair vinā ||
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ cakre tathānyān api gotrajān |
evaṃ pradatteṣv atha ca piṇḍeṣu pretabhāvataḥ ||
vimuktās te dvijāḥ prāpya brahmalokaṃ tato gatāḥ |
vāyupurāṇe |
ānantyāya bhaved annaṃ khaḍgamāṃsaṃ pitṛkṣaye |
kṛṣṇachāgas tathā loha ānantyāya prakalpayet ||
gayāyām akṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ japahomatapāṃsi ca |
pitṛkṣayo hi tatputra tena tatrākṣayaṃ bhavet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
kāṅkṣanti pitaraḥ putrān narakād bhayabhīravaḥ |
gayāṃ yāsyati yaḥ kaścit so ‘smān santārayiṣyati ||
gayāyāṃ dharmapṛṣṭhe ca sarasi brahmaṇas tathā |
gayāśīrṣe ‘kṣayavaṭe pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam ||
[168]        brahmāraṇyaṃ dharmapṛṣṭhaṃ dhenukāraṇyam eva ca |
dṛṣṭvaitāni pitṝṃś cārya vaṃśān viṃśatim uddharet ||
vāyupurāṇe |
gayāyāṃ dharmapṛṣṭhe ca sarasi brahmaṇas tathā |
gavāṃ gṛdhravaṭe caiva śrāddhaṃ tat tan mahāphalam ||
bharatasyāśrame puṇye nityaṃ puṇyatamair vṛte |
mataṅgasya padaṃ tatra dṛśyate sarvamānuṣaiḥ ||
khyāpitaṃ dharmasarvasvaṃ lokasyāsya nidarśanāt |
yac campakavanaṃ puṇyaṃ puṇyakṛdbhir niṣevitam ||
tasmin pāṇḍuviśalyeti tīrthaṃ sadyo nidarśanam |
tṛtīyāyāṃ tathā pāde niścīvāyāśvamaṇḍale ||
mahāhrade ca kauśikyāṃ dattaṃ śrāddhaṃ mahāphalam |
muṇḍapṛṣṭhe padaṃ nyastaṃ mahādevena dhīmatā ||
bahūn varṣagaṇāṃs taptvā tapas tīvraṃ suduścaram |
alpenāpy atra kālena bhaved dharmaparāyaṇaḥ ||
pāpnānam utsṛjaty āśu jīrṇāṃ tvacam ivoragaḥ |
nāmnā kanakhalaṃ vipra tīrthaṃ jagati viśrutam ||
udīcyāṃ muṇdapṛṣṭhasya brahmarṣigaṇasevitam |
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti svaśarīreṇa mānavāḥ ||
dattaṃ cāpi sadā śrāddham akṣayaṃ samudāhṛtam |
snātvā ṛṇatrayaṃ tatra niṣkrīṇāti narottama ||
mānase sarasi snātvā śrāddhaṃ nirvartayet tataḥ |
uttaraṃ mānasaṃ gatvā siddhiṃ prāpnoty anuttamām ||
[169]        tasmin nirvartayec chrāddhaṃ yathāśakti yathābalam |
kāmān sa labhate divyān mokopāyāṃś ca kṛtsnaśaḥ ||
mahābhārate |
pulastyatīrthayāyāyām
tato gayāṃ samāsādya brahmacārī jitendriyaḥ |
aśvamedham avāpnoti gamanād eva bhārata ||
yatrākṣayavaṭo nāma triṣu lokekṣu visrutaḥ |
yatra dattaṃ pitṛbhyas tu bhavaty akṣayyam ity uta ||
mahānadyām upaspṛśya tarpayet pitṛdevatāḥ |
akṣayān prāpnuyāl lokān kulaṃ caiva samuddhaet ||
tato brahmasaro gacched brahmāraṇyopaśobhitam |
brahmalokam avāpnoti prabhātam iva śarvarī ||
brahmaṇā tatra sarasi yūpaḥ puṇyaḥ prakalpitaḥ |
yūpaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā vājapeyaphalaṃ labhet ||
tato gaccheta rājendra dhenukaṃ lokaviśrutam |
ekarātroṣito yatra prayacchet tiladhenukām ||
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā somalokaṃ vrajed dhruvam |
tatra cihnaṃ mahārāja adyāpi mahad adbhutam ||
kapilā sahavatsā vai pūrvam eva caraty uta |
savatsāyāḥ padāni sma dṛśyante ‘dyāpi tatra vai ||
taṃ yūpaṃ spṛśya rājendra pādeśu nṛpasattama |
[170]        yat kiṃcid aśubhaṃ karma tat praṇaśyati bhārata ||
tato gṛdhravaṭaṃ gacchet sthānaṃ devasya dhīmataḥ |
snāyīta bhasmanā tatra abhigamya vṛṣadhvajam ||
brāhmaṇena bhavec cīrṇaṃ vrataṃ dvādaśavārṣikam |
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāṃ sarvapāpaṃ praṇaśyati ||
udyantaṃ ca tato gacchet parvataṃ gītavādinam |
sāvitryās tu padaṃ tatra dṛśyate bharatarṣabha ||
tatra samdhyām upāsīta brāhmaṇaḥ saṃśitavrataḥ |
upāsitā bhavet saṃdhyā tena dvādaśavārṣikī ||
yonidvāraṃ ca tatraiva dṛśyate bharatarṣabha |
tatrābhigamya mucyeta puruṣo yonisaṃkaṭāt ||
śuklakṛṣṇāv ubhau pakṣau gayāyāṃ yo vasen naraḥ |
punāty āsaptamaṃ caiva kulaṃ nāsty atra saṃśayaḥ ||
“eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā yady eko ‘pi gayāṃ vrajet |
yajeta vāśvamedhena nīlaṃ vā vṛṣam utsṛjet” ||
tataḥ phalguṃ vrajed rājaṃs tīrthasevī narādhipa |
aśvamedhavāpnoti [-dham avāpnoti?] siddhiṃ ca paramāṃ labhet ||
tato gacchet tu rājendra dharmapṛṣṭhaṃ mahāphalam |
tatra dharmo mahārāja nityam āste yudhiṣṭhira ||
abhigamya tatas tatra vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet |
tato gacchet tu rājendra brahmaṇas tīrtham uttamam ||
[171]        tatrābhigamya rājendra brahmāṇaṃ bharatarṣabha |
rājasūyāśvamedhābhyāṃ phalam āpnoti mānavaḥ ||
tathā |
dhaumyatīrthayātrāyam |
pūrvaṃ prācīṃ diśaṃ rājan rājarṣigaṇasevitam |
ramyāṃ te kīrtayiṣyāmi yudhiṣṭhira yathāsmṛti ||
yasyāṃ girivaraḥ puṇyo gato rājarṣisatkṛtaḥ |
śivaṃ brahmasaro yatra sevitaṃ tatra carṣibhiḥ ||
yadarthaṃ puruṣavyāghra kīrtayanti purātanāḥ |
“eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrāḥ yady eko ‘pi gatāṃ vrajet ||
saṃtarayati santatyā daśa pūrvān daśāvarān” |
mahānadī ca tatraiva tathā gayaśiro nṛpa ||
yatrāsau kīrtyate viprair akṣayyaṃkaraṇo vaṭaḥ |
yatra dattaṃ pitṛbhyo ‘nnam akṣayyaṃ bhavati prabho ||
sā ca puṇyajalā yatra phalgunāmā mahānadī |
bahumūlaphalā cāpi kauśikī bharatarṣabha ||
tathā |
lomaśatīrthayātrāyām |
tato mahīdharaṃ jagmur dharmajñenābhirakṣitam |
rājarṣiṇā puṇyakṛtā gayenānupamadyute ||
[172]        nago gayaśiro yatra puṇyā caiva mahānadī |
ṛṣijuṣṭaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ tīrthaṃ brahmasaro ‘tulam ||
agastyo bhagavān yatra yato vaivasvataṃ prati |
uvāsa ca svayaṃ tatra dharmarājaḥ savāhanaḥ ||
sarvāsāṃ saritāṃ yatra samudbhedo viśāṃ pate |
yatra saṃnihito nityaṃ mahādevaḥ pinākadhṛk ||
tatra te pāṇḍavā vīrāś cāturmāsyais tad ejire |
ṛṣiyajñena mahatā yatrākṣayyavaṭo mahān ||
tathā |
balabhadratīrthayātrāyām |
gayasya yajamānasya gayasyeva mahākratuḥ |
āhūtā susaric chreṣṭhā yatra yajñe sarasvatī ||
viśālāṃ tu gayeṣv āhur ṛṣayaḥ saṃśitavratāḥ |
“gayasyeva” iti yajñasya tenaivopamānam atipraśaṃsārtham |
tathā |
āṅgirasatīrthayātrāyām |
muṇḍapṛṣṭhaṃ gayāṃ caiva nairṛtaṃ devaparvatam |
tṛtīyāṃ krauñcapādīṃ ca brahmahatyā vimucyate ||
vāmanapurāṇe |
prahlādatīrthayātrāyām |
gayāyāṃ gopatiṃ draṣṭuṃ sa jagāma mahāsuraḥ |
sarasi brahmaṇaḥ snātvā kṛtvā cāsya pradakṣiṇam ||
[173]        piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ puṇyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ sa cakāra ha |
narasiṃhapurāṇe |
śivanadyāṃ śivakaraṃ gayāyāṃ ca janārdanam |
sarvatra paramātmānaṃ yaḥ paśyati sa mucyate ||
matsyapurāṇe |
gaurīsthānapratipādanaprastāve |
vārāṇasyāṃ viśālākṣī prayāge lalitā tathā |
gayāyāṃ maṅgalā nāma kṛtaśauce tu siṃhikā ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
yad dadāti gayāsthaś ca sarvam ānantyam aśnute |
viṣṇuḥ |
api jāyeta so ‘smākaṃ kule kaścin narottamaḥ |
gayāśīrṣe vaṭe śrāddhaṃ yas tu kuryāt samāhitaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
“eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrāḥ śīlavanto guṇānvitāḥ |
teṣāṃ vai samavetānāṃ yady eko ‘pi gayāṃ vrajet |
yajeta vāśvamedhena nīlaṃ vā vṛṣam utsṛjet ||
[174] vasiṣṭhaḥ |
nandanti pitaras tasya suvṛṣṭer iva karṣakāḥ |
yad gayāstho ‘dadāt puṇyaṃ pitaras tena putriṇaḥ ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe gayāmāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[175]
6
atha kurukṣetramāhātmyam
tatra vāmanapurāṇe |
kurur uvāca |
yāvad etan mayā kṛṣṭaṃ dharmakṣetraṃ tad astu ca |
snātānāṃ ca mṛtānāṃ ca mahāpuṇyaphalaṃ tv iha ||
upavāsaṃ ca dānaṃ ca snānaṃ japyaṃ ca mādhava |
homo yajñādikaṃ cānyac chubhaṃ vāpy aśubhaṃ vibho ||
tvatprasādād dhṛṣīkeśa śaṅkhacakragadādhara |
akṣayaṃ pravarakṣetre bavatv atra mahat phalam ||
tathā tvaṃ ca suraiḥ sārdhaṃ samaṃ devena śūlinā |
vasātra puṇḍarīkākṣa mannāmnā cedam ucyate ||
ity evam uktas tenāhaṃ rājñā vai dattavān varam |
tathā ca tvaṃ divyavapur bhava bhūyo mahīpate ||
tathāntakāle mām eva layam eṣyasi suvrata |
kīrtiś ca śāśvatī tubhyaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ ||
kalidvāparayor madhye vyāsena ca mahātmanā |
saraḥpramāṇaṃ yat proktaṃ tac chṛṇudhvaṃ dvijottamāḥ ||
[176]        viśveśvarād asti purā tathāraṇyā jaradgavī |
yāvad gaur jaratī proktā tāvat saṃnihitaṃ saraḥ ||
mayā śrutaṃ pramāṇaṃ tu pacyamānaṃ ca vāmane |
tac chṛṇudhvaṃ dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ puṇyavṛddhikaraṃ mahat ||
viśveśvarād ekarātrir yāvac cātha sarasvatī |
saraḥ saṃnihitaṃ jñeyaṃ samantād ardhayojanam ||
etad āśritya devāś ca ṛṣayaś samāgatāḥ |
sevanti muktikāmāś ca gandharvāpsarasaḥ sthitāḥ ||
yadā sūryasya grahaṇaṃ kālena bhavitā kvacit |
saṃnihatya tadā snātvā pūtāḥ svargaṃ gamiṣyatha ||
ye tu siddhā mahātmānas te liṅgasparśanena ca |
vrajanti paramāṃ siddhiṃ punarāvṛttir durlabhām ||
evaṃ jñātvā tato gaṅgā devānāṃ hitakāmyayā |
upary upari liṅgāni sapta tatra cakāra ha ||
tato ye muktikāmāś ca siddhā yajñaparāyaṇāḥ |
saṃsevya te prayatnena prayātāḥ paramaṃ padam ||
pāṃsavo ‘pi kurukṣetre vāyunā samudīritāḥ |
atiduṣkṛtakarmāṇaṃ nayanti paramāṃ gatim ||
tato ‘haṃ tāḥ prajā dṛṣṭvā pihitās tena tejasā |
krodhena mahatā yukto liṅgam utpāṭya cākiṣīpan ||
[177]        saṃkṣiptaḥ sarasor madhye ūrdhvam eva punaḥ sthitaḥ |
tadā prabhṛti lokeṣu sthāṇur ity eva viśrutaḥ ||
sakṛddarśaṇamātreṇa vimuktaḥ sarvakilbiṣaiḥ |
prayāti mokṣaṃ paramaṃ yasmān nāvartate punaḥ ||
yaś ceha tīrthe nivaset kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ samāhitaḥ |
sa muktaḥ pātakaiḥ sarvair agamyāgamanodbhavaiḥ ||
mahābhārate |
tato gaccheta rājendra kurukṣetram abhiṣṭutam |
pāpebhyo vipramucyante tadgatāḥ sarvajantavaḥ ||
kurukṣetraṃ gamiṣyāmi kurukṣetre vasāmy aham |
ya evaṃ satataṃ brūyāt so ‘pi pāpāt pramucyate ||
tatra vai saṃvased dhīraḥ sarasvatyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira |
yatra brahmādayo devā ṛṣayo siddhacāraṇāḥ ||
gandharvāpsaraso yakṣāḥ pannagāś ca mahīpate |
brahmakṣetraṃ mahāpuṇyam abhigacchanti bhārata ||
manasāpy abhikāmasya kurukṣetraṃ yudhiṣṭhira |
pāpāni ca vinaśyanti svargalokaṃ sa gacchati ||
gatvāpi śraddhayā yuktaḥ kurukṣetraṃ kurūdvaha |
phalaṃ prāpnoti ca tadā rājasūyāśvamedhayoḥ ||
[178] tathā |
tato gacchec ca dharmajña tīrthaṃ saṃnihatīm api |
yatra brahmādayo devā ṛṣayaś ca tapodhanāḥ ||
māsi māsi sameṣyanti puṇyena mahatānvitāḥ |
saṃnihatyām upaspṛśya rāhugraste divākare ||
aśvamedhaśataṃ tatra teneṣṭaṃ śāśvataṃ bhavet |
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni antarikṣacarāṇi ca ||
nadyo nadās taḍāgāś ca sarvaprasravaṇāni ca |
udapānāś ca viprāś ca puṇyaprayatanāni ca ||
snātamātrasya tat sarvaṃ naśyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
padmavarṇena yānena brahmalokaṃ prapadyate ||
abhipadya tato mokṣaṃ dvārapālam anantakam |
koṭīkūpam upaspṛśya labhed bahusuvarṇakam ||
gaṅgāhradaś ca tatraiva tīrthaṃ bharatasattama |
tatra snāyīta darmajño brahmacārī samāhitaḥ ||
rājasūyāśvamedhābhyāṃ phalaṃ vindati mānavaḥ |
pṛthivyāṃ naimiśaṃ puṇyam antarikṣe ca puṣkaram ||
trayāṇām api lokānāṃ kurukṣetraṃ viśiṣyate |
pāṃsavo ‘pi kurukṣetrād vāyunā samudīritāḥ ||
atiduṣkṛtakarmāṇaṃ nayanti paramāṃ gatim |
uttareṇa dṛṣadvatyāṃ dakṣiṇena sarasvatīm ||
[179]        ye vasanti kurukṣetre te vasanti triviṣṭape |
kurukṣetraṃ gamiṣyāmi kurukṣetre vasāmy aham ||
athaikāṃ vācam utsṛjya svargaloke mahīyate |
brahmavediḥ kurukṣetraṃ puṇyaṃ brahmarṣisevitam ||
tatrāvasanti ye rājan na te śocyāḥ kathaṃcana |
tadrantukārantukayor yad antaraṃ
rāmahradānāṃ ca macakrukasya |
etat kurukṣetrasamantapañcakaṃ
pitāmahasyottaravedir ucyate ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe kurukṣetramāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[180]
7
atha pṛthūdakamāhātmyam
tatra mahābhārate |
tato gaccheta rājendra tīrthaṃ trailokyaviśrutam |
pṛthūdakam iti khyātaṃ kārttikeyasya dhīmataḥ ||
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta pitṛdevārcane rataḥ |
ajñānāj jñānato vāpi striyā vā puruṣeṇa vā ||
yat kiṃcid aśubhaṃ karma kṛtaṃ mānuṣabuddhinā |
tat sarvaṃ naśyate rājan snātamātrasya kaurava ||
aśvamedhaphalaṃ cāsya svargalokaṃ ca gacchati |
puṇyam āhuḥ kurukṣetraṃ kurukṣetrāt sarasvatī ||
sarasvatyāś ca tīrthāni tīrthebhyaś ca pṛthūdakam |
śrutaṃ me sarvatīrthānāṃ yas tyajed ātmanas tanum ||
pṛthūdake japyaparo nainaṃ śvomaraṇaṃ tapet |
śvobhāvy api maraṇaṃ nainaṃ santāpayed ity arthaḥ |
gītaṃ sanatkumāreṇa vyāsena ca mahātmanā |
vede ca niyataṃ rājann abhigacchet pṛthūdakam ||
[181]        pṛthūdakāt puṇyatamaṃ nānyatīrthaṃ narādhipa |
etan medhyaṃ pavitraṃ ca pāvanaṃ ca na saṃśayaḥ ||
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye ‘pi pāpakṛto janāḥ |
pṛthūdake naraśreṣṭha prāhur evaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe pṛthūdakamāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[182]
8
atha puṣkaramāhātmyam
tatra mahābhārate |
triloke devalokasya tīrthaṃ trailokyaviśrutam |
puṣkaraṃ nāma vikhyātaṃ mahābhāgaḥ samāviśet ||
daśakoṭisahasrāṇāṃ tīrthānāṃ vai mahīpate |
sāṃnidhyaṃ puṣkare yeṣāṃ trisaṃdhyaṃ kurunandana ||
ādityā vasavo rudrāḥ śādhyāś ca samarudgaṇāḥ |
gandharvāpsarasaś caiva nityaṃ saṃnihitā vibho ||
yatra devās tapas taptvā daityā brahmarṣayas tathā |
divyabhogā mahārāja puṇyena manatānvitāḥ ||
manasāpy abhikāmasya puṣkarāṇi manasvinaḥ |
pūyante sarvapāpāni nākapṛṣṭhe ca pūjyate ||
tasmiṃs tīrthe mahārāja nityam eva pitāmahaḥ |
uvāsa paramaprīto devadānavasaṃmataḥ ||
puṣkareṣu mahābhāga devāḥ sarṣigaṇāḥ purā |
siddhiṃ samabhisaṃprāptāḥ puṇyena mahatānvitāḥ ||
[183]        tatrābhiṣekaṃ yaḥ kuryāt pitṛdevārcane rataḥ |
aśvamedhaṃ daśaguṇaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ ||
apy ekaṃ bhojayed vipraṃ puṣkarāraṇyam āśritaḥ |
tenāsau karmaṇābhīkṣṇaṃ pretya ceha ca modate ||
śākair mūlaphalair vāpi yena vartayate svayam |
tad vai dadyād brāhmaṇāya śraddhāvān anasūyakaḥ ||
tenaiva prāpnuyat prājño hayamedhaphalaṃ naraḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdro vā rājasattama ||
na viyoniṃ vrajet pretya snātvā tīrthe mahātmanaḥ |
kārttikyāṃ tu viśeṣeṇa yo ‘bhigaccheta puṣkaram ||
prāpnuyāt sa naro lokān brahmaṇaḥ sadane ‘kṣayān |
sāyaṃ prātaḥ smared yas tu puṣkarāṇi kṛtāñjaliḥ ||
upaspṛṣṭaṃ bhavet tena sarvatīrtheṣu bhārata |
janmaprabhṛti yat pāpaṃ striyo vā puruṣasya vā ||
puṣkare snātamātrasya sarvam eva praṇaśyati |
yathā surāṇāṃ sarveṣām ādis tu madhusūdanaḥ ||
tathaiva puṣkaraṃ rājaṃs tīrthānām ādir ucyate |
uṣitvā daśa varṣāṇi puṣkare niyataḥ śuciḥ ||
kratūn sarvān avāpnoti brahmalokaṃ ca gacchati |
yas tu varṣaśataṃ pūrṇam agnihotram upācaret ||
[184]        kārttikīṃ vā vased ekāṃ puṣkare samam eva tat |
duṣkaraṃ puṣkare snānaṃ duṣkaraṃ puṣkare tapaḥ ||
duṣkaraṃ puṣkare yānaṃ vastuṃ caiva ca duṣkaram |
padmapurāṇe |
ṛṣikoṭiḥ samāyātā dakṣiṇāpathavāsinām |
snānārthaṃ puṣkare rājan puṣkaraṃ bhuvi yad gatam ||
atha te munayaḥ sarve prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇāḥ |
dhyāyantaś ca paraṃ brahma sthitā dvādaśa vatsarān ||
brahmā maharṣayas tatra devāś cendrapurogamāḥ |
ṛṣīṃs tān vārayāmāsur niyamāt te suduṣkarāt ||
kāraṇena viyadyānaṃ mantreṇāhvayata dvijāḥ |
āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhiḥ ṛgbhiḥ sāṃnidhyam eṣyati ||
aghamarṣaṇajapyena jāyate phaladāyakam |
viprair vākyāvasāne tu sarvais tais tu tathākṛtam ||
kṛtena puṇyatāṃ prāptā jātidoṣād ṛte dvijāḥ |
garhitā dharmaśāstreṣu ye viprā dakṣiṇottarāḥ ||
ye cānye pārvatīyāś ca śrāddhe nārhanti ketanam |
etasmāt kāraṇād yānaṃ viyaty eva ciraṃ sthitam ||
[185]        kārttikyāṃ puṣkare snātuṃ svayam evābhigacchati |
brahmaṇā sahitaṃ rājan sarveṣāṃ puṇyadāyakam ||
tatrāgacchanti ye varṇāḥ sarve te puṇyabhājanāḥ |
dvijais tulyā na saṃdeho vinā mantreṇa te nṛpa ||
antarīkṣād avātīrṇaṃ tīrthaṃ paitāmahe śubham |
snānaṃ ye ‘tra kariṣyanti teṣāṃ lokā mahodayāḥ ||
nispṛhaṃ te anyapuṇyasya kṛtasyāpy akṛtasya ca |
kariṣyanti mahārāja satyam etad udāhṛtam ||
tīrthānāṃ paramaṃ tīrthaṃ pṛthivyām iha paṭhyate |
nāsmāt parataraṃ tīrthaṃ loke ‘smin paripaṭhyate ||
yamaḥ |
kārttikī puṣkare snātaḥ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
viṣṇuḥ |
puṣkare hy akṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ japahomatapāṃsi ca |
puṣkare snātamātras tu sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe puṣkaramāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[186]
9
atha mathurāmāhātmyam
tatra varāhapurāṇe |
varāha uvāca |
mathureti ca vikhyātaṃ muktikṣetraṃ paraṃ mama |
bhaviṣyati varārohe dvāpare yugasaṃsthite ||
suramyā ca praśastā ca janmabhūmir mama priyā |
mūrtīś catasra ādāya utpatsyāmi varānane ||
“mūrtīḥ” kṛṣṇabalabhadrāniruddhapradyumnasvarūpāḥ |
tatra guhyāni me bhadre bhaviṣyanti priyāṇi vai |
puṇyāni ca praśastāni nṛṇāṃ saṃsāramokṣiṇām ||
“guhyāni” sthānāni vakṣyamāṇāni |
tatrāhaṃ ghātayiṣyāmi dānavāṃś ca varānane |
nadī tatra varārohe śubhā puṇyatarodakā ||
vivasvataḥ sutā ramyā yamunā vai bhaviṣyati |
sā prayāge tu suśroṇi brahmakṣetre tathā śūbhā ||
bhaviṣyati na saṃdeho gaṅgāyās tadanantaram |
[187] “sā prayāge tu” iti | “tadanantaraṃ” mathurāyā anantaram “prayāge” brahmakṣetre gaṅgāyāḥ saṃbandhād yathā yamunā śubhā bhaviṣyati, tathātra prathamam eva mathurāyāṃ śubhā bhaviṣyatīty arthaḥ |
labhante manujāḥ siddhim ātmakarmasu bhāvitāḥ |
mama kṣetraprabhāveṇa bhūmi saṃsāramokṣaṇam ||
asikuṇḍaṃ paraṃ nāma surabhyaṃ yamunāmbhasi |
kūle ca dakṣiṇe tatra mathurāyāṃ sumadhyame ||
tatra snānaṃ prakurvīta ṣaṣṭhakāloṣito naraḥ |
nandanaṃ vanam āśritya kṛtakṛtyaḥ sa modate ||
athātra muñcati prāṇāṃs tasmin kūle taṭe śubhe |
nandanaṃ vanam utsṛjya mama lokaṃ prapadyate ||
asti vṛndāvanaṃ nāma tasmin kṣetre paraṃ mama |
tatrāhaṃ krīḍayiṣyāmi gobhir gopālakaiḥ saha ||
tatra kuṇḍaṃ mahābhāge latāgulmaśatāvṛtam |
tatra snānaṃ prakurvīta catuḥkāloṣito naraḥ ||
modate vipule divye gandharvāṇāṃ kuleṣu ca |
tatra yo muñcati prāṇān satataṃ kṛtaniścayaḥ ||
gandharvapuram utsṛjya mama lokaṃ sa gacchati |
tatra bhāṇḍīrakaṃ nāma guhyaṃ kṣetraṃ paraṃ mama ||
devā api na jānanti nāgā yakṣā mahāsurāḥ |
tatra snānaṃ prakurvīta ekabhaktoṣīto naraḥ ||
[188]        modate nāgalokeṣu evam etan na saṃśayaḥ |
athātra muñcati prāṇān kṛtvā karma suduṣkaram ||
nāgalokaṃ samutsṛjya mama lokaṃ prapadyate |
tatra kuṇḍaṃ mahābhāge drumagulmalatāvṛtam ||
tatra pūrveṇa pārśveṇa tasmiṃs tīrthaṃ sudhārmikam |
“dhārmikaṃ” dharmaprayojanaṃ dharmotpādakam ity arthaḥ |
campakaiḥ pāṭalaiś caiva nānāpuṣpaiś ca śobhitam |
tatra snānaṃ tu kurvīta ahorātroṣito naraḥ ||
lokaṃ vaidyādharaṃ gatvā modate kṛtaniścayaḥ |
athātra muñcati prāṇān mallokaṃ praipadyate ||
yamalārjunakuṇḍeti kṣetraṃ kuṇḍaṃ paraṃ mama |
tac ca kuṇḍaṃ mahābhāge vṛkṣagulmalatāvṛtam ||
tatra snānaṃ tu kurvīta ahorātoṣito naraḥ |
apsarobhavanaṃ gatvā modate gatakilbiṣaḥ ||
athātra muñcati prāṇān kṛtvā karma suniścayaḥ |
apsarobhavanaṃ gatvā mallokaṃ pratipadyate ||
tathaivārkasthalṃ nāma tasmin kṣetre paraṃ mama |
dṛśyate tatra cādityo dvādaśātmā vyavasthitaḥ ||
kūpas tatra mahābhāge prasannasalilodakaḥ |
durlabhaḥ pāpakāryāṇāṃ sulabhaḥ puṇyakāriṇām ||
[189]        athātra snānaṃ kurvīta ekarātroṣito naraḥ |
modate sūryalokeṣu evam etan na saṃśayaḥ ||
athātra muñcati prāṇān kṛtvā cendriyanigraham |
sūryaloke parityajya mama lokaṃ sa gacchati ||
tatra vīrasthalaṃ nāma jyeṣṭhaṃ guhyaṃ paraṃ mama |
gulmavallīlatākīrṇaṃ bahupuṣpaphalodakam ||
snānaṃ tatraiva kurvīta pañcarātroṣito naraḥ |
mamaiva sa prasādena vīralokeṣu modate ||
athātra muñcati prāṇān ekacitto dṛḍhavrataḥ |
vīralokaṃ samutsṛjya mama lokaṃ prapadyate ||
aparaṃ kanakaṃ nāma guhyakṣetraṃ paraṃ mama |
vindhyapṛṣṭhe mahābhāge kūpas tatra vidhīyate ||
tatra snānaṃ prakurvīta ekabhaktoṣito naraḥ |
modate nākapṛṣṭhe sa kṛtakṛtyo na saṃśayaḥ ||
athātra muñcati prāṇān yāti divyāṃ parāṃ gatim |
nāmnā prakīrtanaṃ nāma guhyakṣetraṃ paraṃ mama ||
tatra caikaṃ śilāpaṭṭaṃ raktacandanasaṃnibham |
manojñaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ ca mama bhaktasukhāvaham ||
yas tatra tiṣṭhate bhūmim ekarātroṣito naraḥ |
sa vāyubhavanaṃ gatvā modate kṛtaniścayaḥ ||
[190]        athātra muñcati prāṇān yāti lokaṃ mamākṣayam |
somakuṇḍeti vikhyātaṃ tasmin kṣetre paraṃ mama ||
tatra snānaṃ tu kurvīta somaloke sa modate |
athātra muñcati prāṇān lobhamohavivarjitaḥ ||
somalokaṃ samutsṛjya modate māṃ ca paśyati |
rādhākuṇḍeti vikhyātaṃ tasmin kṣetre paraṃ mama ||
tatra snānaṃ tu kurvīta ekarātroṣito naraḥ |
tataḥ piṇḍārakaṃ nāma kṣetraṃ guhyaṃ paraṃ mama ||
snāne dakṣiṇakūle tu śuddhe tu yamunāmbhasi |
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta ekarātroṣito naraḥ ||
yāti puṇyakṛtāṃ lokaṃ meruśṛṅgasamāśritam |
jambīraṃ campakaṃ nāma tasmin kṣetre paraṃ mama ||
tatra kuṇḍaṃ viśālākṣi prasannaṃ vimalodakam |
tatra snānaṃ prakurvīta ekarātroṣito naraḥ ||
agniṣṭomasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ |
karmāvarohaṇaṃ nāma tasmin kṣetre paraṃ mama ||
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta vindhyapramukhataḥ śubhe |
nāmnā vitānakaṃ kṣetraṃ tatra guhyaṃ paraṃ mama ||
mathurāpaścime bhoge adūrād ardhayojanāt |
tato ballabhakaṃ nāma kṣetraṃ guhyaṃ paraṃ mama ||
[191]        athātra muñcati prāṇāṃs tīrthe ballabhake mama |
svargalokaṃ parityajya mama loke mahīyate ||
ūṣabhañjanakaṃ nāma tasmin guhyaṃ paraṃ mama |
yatra dhārā pataty ekā vindhyakūṭād viniḥsṛtā ||
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta ekarātroṣito naraḥ |
athātra muñcati prāṇān śīghraṃ yāti parāṃ gatim ||
asti binduprabhaṃ nāma tasmin guhyaṃ paraṃ mama |
bindur ekaḥ pataty atra parvatād duratikramāt ||
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta ekarātroṣito naraḥ |
athātra muñcati prāṇān śīghraṃ yāti parāṃ gatim ||
asti astamanaṃ nāma tasmin guhyaṃ paraṃ mama |
dhārātra patate caikā vindhyaparvataniḥsṛtā ||
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta upaspṛśya yathāvidhi |
asti tāmraprabhaṃ nāma tasya kṣetrasya dakṣiṇe ||
atha dūrāt pañcakrośāt tatra kuṇḍaṃ paraṃ mahat |
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta catuḥkālasamanvitaḥ ||
vatsaprakrīḍanaṃ nāma tasmin guhyaṃ paraṃ mama |
[192]        mathurāpaścime pārśve adūrād ardhayojanāt ||
tatra kuṇḍaṃ mahābhāge prasannavimalodakam |
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta ahorātroṣito naraḥ ||
agneḥ sa bhavanaṃ gatvā modate vigatajvaraḥ |
athātra muñcati prāṇān siddhiṃ yāti parāṃ gatim ||
asti govardhanaṃ nāma kṣetraṃ guhyaṃ paraṃ mama |
adūrād aṣṭakośāni mathurāyāś ca paścime ||
hradaṃ tatra mahābhāge drumagulmalatāvṛtam |
aindraṃ pūrveṇa pārśvena yāmyāṃ vai dakṣiṇe navam ||
vāruṇe paścime tīrthaṃ kauberaṃ cottareṇa tu |
tatra madhye sthito bhadre krīḍito ‘yaṃ yadṛcchayā ||
saṃstuto ‘haṃ lokapālaiḥ stutibhir mantravādibhiḥ |
tatrāpi mahad āścaryaṃ paśyanti mama catasaḥ ||
tasmin govardhane bhūmiḥ sarvabhāgavatapriye |
caturviṃśati dvādaśyāṃ tasmin śikharamūrdhani ||
dṛśyate sthūladīpā vai dyotayantī diśo daśa |
yaś ca te paśyate dīpaṃ mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ ||
labhate paramāṃ siddhim evam etan na saṃśayaḥ |
kāliyasya hradaṃ puṇyam asti guhyaṃ paraṃ mama ||
tatra kaṃsaṃ haniṣyāmi saṃsthite dvāpare yuge |
tatra snānaṃ tu kurvīta pañcarātroṣito naraḥ ||
gandharvabhavanaṃ gatvā modate vigatajvaraḥ |
tatra tiṣṭhāmy ahaṃ bhadre diśaṃ pūrvāṃ samāśritaḥ ||
[193]        hiraṇyapratimāṃ kṛtvā bṛhadrūpaś caturbhujaḥ |
dakṣiṇe gacchataḥ pārśve kūpo ‘tra vimalodakaḥ ||
saptasāmudrakaṃ nāma yatra saṃtiṣṭhate jalam |
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta catuḥkāloṣito naraḥ ||
modate saptadvīpeṣu svacchandagamanālayaḥ |
athātra muñcati prāṇān mama loke sa modate ||
viṃśadyojanavistāre māthure mama maṇḍale |
tatra prāṇān pramuñcanti siddhā yānti parāṃ gatim ||
asti nandiguhā nāma tasmin guhyaṃ paraṃ mama |
mucukundaḥ svapity atra dānavāsuraśātanaḥ ||
tatra kuṇḍaṃ mahābhāge prasannavimalodakam |
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta ṣaṣṭhakāloṣito naraḥ ||
daśavarṣasahasreṣu modate kṛtaniścayaḥ |
ya caranti mahābhāge māthurādvane (?) janāḥ ||
te ‘pi yānti parāṃ siddhiṃ matprasādān na saṃśayaḥ |
etat te kathitaṃ bhadre māhātmyaṃ mathurāgatam ||
etan maraṇakāle ‘pi smartavyaṃ manasāpi ca |
yadīcchet paramāṃ siddhiṃ sarvasaṃsāramokṣaṇīm ||
viṣṇupurāṇe |
yamunāsalile snātvā puruṣo munisattama |
jyeṣṭhe māsy amale pakṣe dvādaśyām upavāsakṛt ||
[194]        samabhyarcyācyutaṃ samyaṅ mathurāyāṃ samāhitaḥ |
aśvamedhasya yajñasya prāpnoty avikalaṃ phalam ||
ālokyarddhim athānyeṣām uditānāṃ svavaṃśajaiḥ |
etat kilocur anyeṣāṃ pitaraḥ sapitāmahāḥ ||
kaścid asmatkule jātaḥ kālindīsalile plutaḥ |
arcayiṣyati govindaṃ mathurāyām upoṣitaḥ ||
jyeṣṭhamūlā site pakṣe samabhyarcya janārdanam |
parāṃ siddhim avāpsyāmas tāritāḥ svakulodbhavaiḥ ||
dhanyānāṃ kulajaḥ piṇḍaṃ yamunāyāṃ pradāsyati |
tasmin kāle samabhyarcya tatra kṛṣṇaṃ samāhitaḥ ||
iti kalpatarau tīrthakāṇde mathurāmāhātmyam |
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe mathurāmāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[195]
10
atha ujjayinīmāhātmyam
tatra brahmapurāṇe |
dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi mahat kṣetram ujjayinīti ca |
tatra liṅgaṃ mahākālaṃ darśanāt sarvapāpajit ||
tapasvī paramṃ siddhiṃ yatra prāpnoti kāmikīm |
tathā |
rudra uvāca |
avantyāṃ tu yadā skando mayā pūrvaṃ tu bhadritaḥ |
cūḍākarmanivṛtte tu kumārasya tadā śubhe ||
āgatya mātaro bhojyam apūrvaṃ tūpayācire |
devalokād devagaṇo mātṝṇāṃ bhoktum āgataḥ ||
evam uktas tadā tābhir devadevo maheśvaraḥ |
dharmārthaṃ tās tadā proktāḥ pārvatyā devasaṃnidhau ||
mayā vai sādhitaṃ cānnaṃ prakārair bahubhiḥ kṛtam |
tat sarvaṃ ca vyayībhūtaṃ na cānyad iha dṛśyate ||
āgatāsu bhavantīṣu kiṃ deyaṃ vai mayā bhavet |
[196]        apūrvaṃ bhavatīnāṃ ca mayā deyaṃ viśeṣataḥ ||
āsvāditaṃ na cānyais tu bhakṣyaṃ te ca dadāmy aham |
adhobhāge ca yau nābher vartulau phalasaṃnidhau ||
bhakṣayadhvaṃ hi sahitā lambau me vṛṣaṇāv ubhau |
anena cāpy abhojena parā tṛptir bhaviṣyati ||
mahāprasādaṃ tāḥ kṛtvā devyaḥ sarvāś ca vai śubhe |
praṇipatya gatāḥ sarvā idaṃ vacanam abruvan ||
kariṣyanti śubhācārā vinā hāsyena ye ca tat |
teṣāṃ nūnaṃ paśuḥ putrā dārāś caiva gṛhādikam ||
bhaviṣyanti mayā dattaṃ yac cānyan manasepsitam |
hāsyena dīrgharogitvaṃ dāridryaṃ cāpi ninditam ||
tasmān na nindā hāsye ca kartavyaṃ ca vijānatā |
yenākhyā mātaraḥ khyātās tato loke bhaviṣyati ||
upayācitaṃ narā ye tu kārayiṣyanti kaumude |
caṇakāḥ pūrikāś caiva vṛṣaṇaiḥ saha pūrikāḥ ||
bandhubhiḥ svajanaiś caiva teṣāṃ vaṃśo na bhidyate |
aputro labhate putraṃ dhanārthī labhate dhanam ||
rūpavān subhago bhogī sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ |
haṃsayuktena yānena brahmaloke mahīyate ||
[197] matsyapurāṇe |
prayāge vā bhaven mokṣo mahākāle ‘tha vā priye |
amarakaṇṭake tadvat tathā kāyāvarohaṇe ||
kālañjare mahākāle iha vā matparigrahe |
yadvan mukhe sthite netre tadvat tīrthe ime bhuvaḥ ||
mahākālo ‘vimuktaś ca gaṅgayā siprayānvitau |
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe ujjayinīmāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[198]
11
atha narmadāmāhātmyam
tatra matsyapurāṇe |
yudhiṣṭhiraṃ prati mārkaṇḍeya uvāca |
narmadā saritāṃ śreṣṭhā sarvapāpapraṇāśinī |
tārayet sarvabhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca ||
tathā |
puṇyā kanakhale gaṅgā kurukṣetre sarasvatī |
grāme vā yadi vāraṇye puṇyā sarvatra narmadā ||
tribhiḥ sārasvataṃ toyaṃ saptāhena tu yāmunam |
sadyaḥ punāti gāṅgeyaṃ darśanād eva nārmadam ||
kāliṅgadeśapaścārdhe parvate ‘marakaṇṭake |
puṇyā ca triṣu lokeṣu mānanīyā manoramā ||
[199]        tatra snātvā naro rājan niyamastho jitendriyaḥ |
upoṣya rajanīm ekāṃ kulānāṃ tārayec chatam ||
jaleśvare naraḥ snātvā piṇḍān datvā yathāvidhi |
pitaras tasya tṛpyanti yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam ||
parvatasya samantāt tu rudrakoṭiḥ pratiṣṭhitā |
snānaṃ yaḥ kurute tatra gandhamālyānulepanaiḥ ||
prītā cāsya bhavet sarvā rudrakoṭir na saṃśayaḥ |
parvatasyāpare bhāge svayaṃ devo maheśvaraḥ ||
tatra snātvā śucir bhūtvā brahmacārī jitendriyaḥ |
pitṛkāryaṃ ca kurvīta vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ||
tilodakena tatraiva saṃtarpya pitṛdevatāḥ |
āsaptamaṃ kulaṃ tasya svarge modati pāṇḍava ||
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate |
tataḥ svargāt paribhraṣṭo jāyate vipule kule ||
dhanavān dānaśīlaś ca dhārmikaś caiva jāyate |
punaḥ smarati tat tīrthaṃ gamanaṃ tatra vāñchati ||
tārayitvā kulān sapta rudralokaṃ sa gacchati |
yojanānāṃ śataṃ sāgraṃ śrūyate sarid uttamā ||
vistareṇa tu rājendra yojanadvayam āyatā |
ṣaṣṭitīrthasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭyas tathaiva ca ||
[200]        parvate tatra suśubhe tiṣṭhaty amarakaṇṭake |
brahmacārī śucir bhūtvā jitakrodho jitendriyaḥ ||
sarvahiṃsānivṛttas tu sarvabhūtahite rataḥ |
evaṃ śuddhasamācāro yas tu prāṇān parityajet ||
tasya puṇyaphalaṃ rājan śṛṇuṣvāvahito mama |
śataṃ varṣasahasrāṇāṃ svarge modati pāṇḍava ||
tataḥ svargāt paribhraṣṭo rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ |
gṛhaṃ tu labhate śaurer nānāratnavibhūṣitam ||
tasmin gṛhe vasitvā tu krīḍābhogasamanvite |
jīved varṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ sarvarogavivarjitaḥ ||
evaṃ bhogān bhajante vai mṛtā ye ‘marakaṇṭake |
agnipraveśe ‘tha jale tathā caiva anāśake ||
anivṛttā gatis tasya pavanasyāmbare yathā |
patanaṃ patate yas tu amareśe narādhipa ||
kanyās trīṇi sahasrāṇi ekaikasyāpi cāparā |
tiṣṭhanti bhavane tasya preṣaṇaṃ prārthayanti ca ||
divyabhogasusaṃpannaḥ kṛīḍate kālam akṣayam |
pṛthivyām ā samudrāyām īdṛśo naiva jāyate ||
[201]        yādṛśo ‘yaṃ mṛtaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ parvate ‘marakaṇṭake |
tāvat tīrthaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ parvatasya tu paścime ||
hrado jaleśvaro nāma triṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ |
tatra piṇḍapradānena saṃdhyopāsanakarmaṇā ||
pitaro daśa varṣāṇi tarpitās tu bhavanti te |
dakṣiṇe narmadākūle kapilākhyā mahānadī ||
saralārjunasaṃpannā nātidūre vyavasthitā |
sā tu puṇyā mahābhāgā triṣu lokeṣu viśrutā ||
tatra koṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ tīrthānāṃ tu yudhiṣṭhira |
purāṇe śrūyate rājan śatakoṭiguṇaṃ phalam ||
tasmiṃs tīrtheṣu ye vṛkṣaḥ patitāḥ kālaparyayāt |
narmadātoyasaṃspṛṣṭās te ‘pi yānti parāṃ gatim ||
dvitīyā tu mahābhāgā viśalyakaraṇī śubhā ||
tatra tīre naraḥ snātvā viśalyo bhavati kṣaṇāt |
tatra devagaṇāḥ sarve sakinnaramahoragāḥ ||
yakṣarākṣasagandharvā ṛṣayaś ca tapodhanāḥ |
sarve samāgatās tatra parvate ‘marakaṇṭake ||
taiś ca sarvaiḥ samāgatya munibhiś ca tapodhanaiḥ |
narmadā saṃśritā puṇyā viśalyā nāma nāmataḥ ||
utpāditā mahābhāgā sarvapāpapraṇāśinī |
tatra snātvā naro rājan brahmacārī jitendriyaḥ ||
[202]        upoṣya rajanīm ekāṃ kulānāṃ tārayec chatam |
kapilā ca viśalyā ca śrūyate rājattama [rājasattama?] ||
īśvareṇa purā proktā lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā |
[sarasvatyāṃ ca gaṅgāyāṃ narmadāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira] |
tatra snātvā naro rājann aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
anāśakaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāt tasmiṃs tīrthe narādhipa |
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā rudralokaṃ sa gacchati ||
narmadāyāṃ ca rājendra purāṇe yac chrutaṃ mayā |
yatra tatra naraḥ snātvā aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
ye vasanty uttare kūle rudraloke vasanti te |
sarasvatyāṃ ca gaṅgāyāṃ narmadāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira ||
samaṃ snānaṃ ca dānaṃ ca yathā me śaṅkaro ‘bravīt |
parityajati yaḥ prāṇān parvate ‘marakaṇṭake ||
varṣakoṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ rudraloke mahīyate |
narmadāyā jalaṃ puṇyaṃ phenormisamalaṃkṛtam ||
pavitraṃ śirasā vandyaṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
tathā |
narmadā tu nadī śreṣṭhā puṇyā puṇyatamā hi sā |
munibhis tu mahābhāgair vibhaktā dharmakāṅkṣibhiḥ ||
yajñāḥ pavitramantrāṇi pravibhaktāni pāṇḍava |
teṣāṃ snātvā tu rājendra sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
[203] tathā |
narmadā sarvataḥ puṇyā brahmahatyāpahāriṇī |
ahorātropavāsena mucyate brahmahatyayā ||
evaṃ puṇyā ca ramyā ca narmadā pāṇḍunandana |
trayāṇām api lokānāṃ puṇyā eva mahānadī ||
vaṭeśvare mahāpuṇye gaṅgādvāre tapodhane |
eteṣu sarvasthāneṣu ye dvijāḥ saṃśitavratāḥ ||
śrutaṃ daśaguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ narmadodadhisaṅgame |
candrasūryoparāgeṣu gacched amarakaṇṭakam ||
aśvamedhād daśaguṇaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ |
svargalokam avāpnoti dṛṣṭvā tatra maheśvaram ||
saṃnihatyāṃ gamiṣyāmi rāhugraste divākare |
tad eva nikhilaṃ puṇyaṃ parvate ‘marakaṇṭake ||
manasā saṃsmared yas tu giriṃ tv amarakaṇṭakam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ śataṃ sāgraṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
trayāṇām api lokānāṃ vikhyāto ‘marakaṇṭakaḥ |
tathā |
pradakṣiṇaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāt parvate ‘marakaṇṭake |
puṇḍarīkasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
[tatra jvāleśvaro nāma parvate ‘marakaṇṭake |
[204]        tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye ‘mṛtās te ‘punarbhavāḥ] ||
jvāleśvare mahārāja yas tu prāṇān parityajet |
candrasūryoparāge tu tasyāpi śṛṇu yat phalam ||
amarāṇāṃ patir evaḥ parvate ‘marakaṇṭake |
rudralokam avāpnoti yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam ||
amareśasya devasya parvatasya ubhe taṭe |
tatra tā ṛṣīkoṭyas tu tapasyanti hi suvratāḥ ||
samantād yojanaṃ kṣetraṃ giriś cāmarakaṇṭakaḥ |
akāmo vā sakāmo vā narmadāyāḥ śubhe jale ||
snātvā mucyeta pāpebhyo rudralokaṃ tu gacchati |
tathā |
kāverīsaṃgamaṃ tatra sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
ye narā nābhijānanti vañcitās te na saṃśayaḥ ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena tatra snāyīta mānavaḥ |
kāverī ca mahāpuṇyā narmadā ca mahānadī ||
tatra snātvā tu rājendra arcayed vṛṣabhadhvajam |
aśvamedhaphalaṃ prāpya rudraloke mahīyate ||
agnipraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryād yaś ca kuryād anāśanam |
anivṛttā gatis tasya yathā me śaṅkaro ‘bravīt ||
sevyamāno naraḥ strībhiḥ krīḍate divi rudravat |
[205]        ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭīs tathāparāḥ ||
modate rudralokastho yatra yatraiva gacchati |
puṇyakṣayāt paribhraṣṭo rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ ||
dhanavān dānaśīlaś ca mahaty eva kule bhavet |
tatra pītvā jalaṃ samyak cāndrāyaṇaphalaṃ labhet ||
svargaṃ gacchanti te martyā ye pibanti jalaṃ śubham |
gaṅgāyamunayoḥ saṃge yat phalaṃ prāpnuyān naraḥ ||
kāverīsaṃgame snātas tat phalaṃ tasya jāyate |
iti [śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite] kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe narmadāmāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[206]
12
atha kubjāmrakamāhātmyam
tatra varāhapurāne |
varāha ucāca |
evaṃ kubjāmrakaṃ khyātaṃ tīrtham etad yaśasvini |
ye mṛtās tatra gacchanti te mallokāya niṣkalāḥ ||
tīrthaṃ kubjāmrakaṃ puṇyaṃ mama lokasukhāvaham |
tīrthaṃ kumudārakaṃ caiva tasmin kubjāmrake sthitam ||
snānamātreṇa suśroṇi svargamārgapradaṃ tu tat |
kaumudasya tu māsasya tathā mārgaśirasya ca ||
vaiśākhasya ca māsasya kṛtvā vai karma duṣkaram |
yo vai parityajet prāṇān pumān strī vā napuṃsakaḥ ||
niṣkalāṃ labhate siddhiṃ mama lokāya gacchati |
tīrthaṃ mānasa ity evaṃ yas tu tatrābhidhīyate ||
tasmin snātvā viśālākṣī gacchate nandanaṃ vanam |
tatra yo muñcati prāṇān kaumudasya tu dvādaśīm ||
puṣkalāṃ labhate siddhiṃ mama lokāya gacchati |
[207]        māyātīrtham iti khyātaṃ yena māyāṃ vijānate ||
tasmin kṛtvodakaṃ brahman māyātīrthe mahāyaśāḥ |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi madbhakto jāyate naraḥ ||
atha vā mriyate tatra māyātīre yaśasvini |
māyāyogī tato bhūtvā mama lokāya gacchati ||
tīrthaṃ sarvapikaṃ nāma sarvakarmaguṇānvitam |
yas tatra snāti vai kaścid vaiśākhasya tu dvādaśīm ||
niṣkalaṃ labhate svargaṃ sahasrān daśa pañca ca |
tīrthaṃ pūrvāmukhaṃ nāma tatra kecin na jāyate ||
sarvadā tatra gaṅgāyāḥ śītalaṃ jāyate jalam |
yatra coṣṇaṃ bhavaty ambu jñeyaṃ pūrṇamukhaṃ ca tat ||
snātvā gacchati suśroṇi mama lokaṃ mahāyaśāḥ |
punar anyat pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu tattvena mādhavi ||
aśōkaṃ nāma tatraiva snātvā śokaṃ na paśyati |
sarvasaṅgaiś ca nirmukto mama lokāya gacchati ||
tīrthaṃ ca karavīrākhyaṃ mama lokasukhāvaham |
tasya cihnaṃ pravakṣyāmi yena vijñāyate bhuvi ||
puruṣair jñānavadbhiś ca mama bhaktiviniścitaiḥ |
māghamāsasya suśroṇi śuklapakṣasya dvādaśīm ||
puṣyate karavīraṃ tu madhyāhne tu na saṃśayaḥ |
tatrātha mriyate bhūmi māghamāse tu dvādaśīm ||
[208]        brahmāṇaṃ māṃ ca paśyeta tathaiva vṛṣabhadhvajam |
puṇḍarīkam iti khyātaṃ tīrthaṃ tatra mahad yaśaḥ ||
snātvā prāpnoti suśroṇi phalaṃ tatra mahāguṇam |
puṇdarīkasya yajñasya yajamānasya yat phalam ||
atha vā mriyate tatra naṣṭatrāso mahātapāḥ |
daśānāṃ puṇḍarīkāṇāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
siddhiṃ ca labhate dhanyāṃ mama lokāya gacchati |
agnitīrthe tu vai snātas tasmin kubjāmrake sthitaḥ ||
saptadhāgnipraveśena yat phalaṃ labhate naraḥ |
prāpnoti tan mahābhāge snātamātro na saṃśayaḥ ||
atha vā mriyate tatra śuklām ekāṃ ca dvādaśīm |
viṃśadrātroṣitas tatra mama lokāya gacchati ||
eṣā te kathitā bhūmi vyuṣṭiḥ kubjāmrakasya ca |
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe kubjāmrakamāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[209]
13
atha sūkaramāhātmyam
tatra varāhapurāṇe |
varāha uvāca |
paraṃ kokāmukhaṃ sthānaṃ sthānaṃ kubjāmrakaṃ param |
paraṃ ca saukaraṃ sthānaṃ sarvasaṃsāramokṣaṇam ||
yatra saṃsthā mayā devi uddhṛtāsi rasātalāt |
tatra bhāgīrathī gaṅgā mama śaucartham āgatā ||
śṛṇu puṇyaṃ mahābhāge mama kṣetre tu sundari |
yad āpnoti mahābhāge gatvā ca saukaraṃ prati ||
daśa pūrvān svakān saṃśyān parān sapta ca pañca ca |
svargaṃ nayati tāṃs tatra mātṛtaḥ pitṛtaś ca ha ||
gamanād eva suśroṇi tathaiva mukhadarśanāt |
saptajanmāntaraṃ bhadre jāyate vipule kule ||
evaṃ vai mānuṣo bhūtvā aparādhavivarjitaḥ |
gamanaṃ tasya kṣetrasya maraṇaṃ tatra kārayet ||
ye mṛtās tatra suśroṇi kṣetre sūkarake mama |
tāritāḥ sarvasaṃsārāc chvetadvīpāya yānti te ||
[210]        cakratīrthaṃ mahābhāge cakraṃ yatra pratiṣṭhitam |
tatra gatvā mahābhāge tadanvāyaśubhaḥ śuciḥ ||
snānaṃ kuryād yathānyāyaṃ mādhave māsi dvādaśīm |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi daśavarṣaśatāni ca ||
dhanadhānyasamṛddhas tu jāyate vipule kule |
tāritaḥ sarvasaṃsārāc chvetadvīpāya gacchati ||
dhanvī tūṇī śarī khaḍgī jāyate ca caturbhujaḥ |
naro bhūtvā mahābhāge sa muktaḥ sarvakilbiṣāt ||
antakāle mahābhāge yo gacchec chaukaraṃ prati |
tasya puṇyaṃ pravakṣyāmi snātasyāpi yaśasvini ||
pātakāny api yaḥ kṛtvā mūrkho bhūtvā dṛḍhavrataḥ |
kaumudasya tu māsasya śuklapakṣe tu dvādaśīm ||
tāritāḥ pitaras tena tathaiva ca pitāmahāḥ |
yāvanti jalabindūni tasya gātreṣu sundari ||
tāvad varṣasahasrāṇi madbhaktaś caiva jāyate |
mṛtās tatra viśālākṣi rūpatīrthe mahaujasi ||
dīptimantaś ca jāyeran dyutimantaś caturbhujāḥ |
punar anyat pravakṣyāmi tatra saukarake mama ||
yogitīrtham iti khyātaṃ durvijñeyam ṛṣer api |
yogitīrthe ca ye snātvā mama karmaparāyaṇāḥ ||
krodharāgavinirmuktās te yānti kṛtaniścayāḥ |
ahorātroṣitas tatra yaḥ karoti yaśasvini ||
[211]        tīrthe snānaṃ vinirmukto mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ |
[sa taṃ mṛgayate tīrthaṃ yogāyatanasaṃsthitaḥ ||
sa ca taṃ labhate tīrthaṃ matprasādān na saṃśayaḥ |
tatra prāṇān parityajya mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ] ||
sarvāyudhasamāyukto dīptimāṃś ca caturbhujaḥ |
yogiśreṣṭhatamo bhūtvā śvetadvīpaṃ sa gacchati ||
etat te kathitaṃ bhadre yogitīrthaṃ mahāphalam |
asya cihnaṃ pravakṣyāmi yogitīrthasya sundari ||
mārgaśīrṣasya māsasya śuklapakṣe tu dvādaśīm |
akasmād andhakāro ‘tra tasmiṃs tīrthe tu jāyate ||
trīṇi hastasahasrāṇi trīṇi hastaśatāni ca |
trayo hastā viśālākṣi parimāṇaṃ vidhīyate ||
etac chnaṃ tato dṛṣṭvā maraṇaṃ yo ‘tra kārayet |
snānaṃ caiva viśālākṣi pūrvoktāṃ gacchate gatim ||
etac cihnaṃ mahābhāge yogitīrthe mayi sthite |
āśrayet siddhikāmas tu yadīcchet paramāṃ gatim ||
punar antyat pravakṣyāmi tīrthe saukarake mama |
yatra taptaṃ tapaś caiva somena tadanantaram ||
somena racitā bhūmir dṛśyate candramaḥprabhā |
ātmanaḥ paśyati chāyāṃ yathā nyāyapathe sthitaḥ ||
[212]        somaś caiva na dṛśyeta eṣa vai vismayaḥ paraḥ |
etat te kathitaṃ bhadre somatīrthaviniścitam ||
bhavanti manujā yena tyaktvā saṃsārasāgaram |
tasya pūrveṇa pārśvena tīrthaṃ gṛdhravaṭaṃ smṛtam ||
yatra kāmo mṛto gṛdhro mānuṣatvam upāgataḥ |
snānaṃ sākoṭake tīrthe yaḥ prāpnoti ca mādhavi |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi daśavarṣaśatāni ca ||
nandanaṃ vanam āśritya modate tatra vai sadā |
punar anyat pravakṣyāmi tac chṛṇuṣva vasundare ||
tīrthaṃ vaivasvataṃ nāma ādityo yatra tapyate |
tena taptaṃ mahābhāge putrārthena yaśasvini ||
aṣṭamena tu bhaktena yas tu snāyīta sundari |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi ādityena sa modate ||
atha vā mriyate tatra tīrthe vaivasvate śubhe |
na sa gacchati suśroṇi yamasya bhavanaṃ kvacit ||
maraṇaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi tatra saukarake bhuvi |
kṛtvā hy anaśanaṃ caiva dināni daśa paña ca ||
sarvasaṅgān parityajya mama lokāya gacchati |
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe sūkaramāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[213]
14
atha kokāmikhamāhātmyam
tatra varāhapurāṇe |
varāha uvāca |
nāsti kokāmukhāt kṣetraṃ nāsti kokāmukhāc chubham |
nāsti kokāmukhāt snānaṃ nāśti kokāmukhāt priyam ||
śuciḥ kokāmukhaṃ gatvā paraṃ yogam avāpnuyāt |
karmāṇi raktaḥ kuryāc ca diṣṭaṃ bhavati cātmanaḥ ||
yāni yāni ca kṣetrāṇi tvayā pṛṣṭhe vasundare |
kokāmukhasamaṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati ||
mama sā paramā mūrtir na tāṃ jānanti mohitāḥ |
sthitāṃ kokāmukhe bhūmi etat te kathitaṃ mayā ||
tathā |
pañcayojanavistāre kṣetre kokāmukhe mama |
yas tīrthāni vijānāti na sa pāpen lipyate ||
tatra kokāmukhe tīrthe sthito ‘haṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
varāharūpī bhagavāṃs tiṣṭhām ipuruṣākṛtiḥ ||
[214]        vamonnatamukhaṃ kṛtvā pañcadaṃṣṭrāsamanvitam |
paśyāmi ca jagat sarvaṃ ye ca bhaktā mama priyāḥ ||
yadi kokāmukhaṃ gacchet kadācit kālaparyayāt |
na tataḥ sa nivarteta yadīcched gatim uttamām ||
na sāṃkhyena na yogena siddho yāti mahat padam |
yadi kokāmukhaṃ yas tu tavaitat kathitaṃ mayā ||
tatra mahābhārate |
kokāmukham upaspṛśya brahmacārī yatavrataḥ |
jātismaratvaṃ prāpnoti diṣṭam etat purātane ||
“diṣṭaṃ” kathitam, “purātane” purāṇe |
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīlakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe kokāmukhamāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[215]
15
atha badirakāśramamāhātmyam
tatra varāhapurāṇe |
varāha uvāca |
tasmin himavataḥ pṛṣṭhe sthānaṃ guhyaṃ paraṃ mama |
tatrāham agninā bhūmi tapasā paritoṣitaḥ ||
agninā sadṛśo bhūtvā karma kṛtvā suduṣkaram |
prāpnuvanti ca madbhaktā badaryāśramam uttamam ||
sudurlabhaṃ ca tat kṣetraṃ himakūṭaśilātalam |
yas tu tat prāpnute kṣetraṃ kṛtakṛtyo bhaven naraḥ ||
brahmakuṇḍeti vikhyātam asti tatra śiloccaye |
snānaṃ karoti yas tatra trirātropoṣito naraḥ ||
agniṣṭomaśatāt tulyaṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ |
uṣitvā tatra kṛcchrāṇi yadi prāṇān parityajet ||
brahmalokam atikramya mama lokaṃ sa gacchati |
agnikuṇḍeti vikhyātaṃ tatra kuṇḍe paraṃ mama ||
[216]        yadi tatra kṛtaprāṇaḥ kṛtacāndrāyaṇavrataḥ |
agnilokam atikramya mama lokaṃ sa gacchati ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe badarikāśramamāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
16
atha mandāramāhātmyam
tatra varāhapurāṇe |
varāha uvāca |
punar anyat pravakṣyāmi ekānte śṛṇu sundari |
sthānaṃ me paramaṃ guhyaṃ karmiṇāṃ tu sukhāvaham ||
jāhnavyā dakṣiṇe kūle vindhyāpṛṣṭhasamāśrite |
mandāreti ca vikhyātaṃ sarvabhāgavatapriyam ||
tatra tretāyuge bhūmi kāmo nāma mahādyutiḥ |
bhaviṣyati na saṃdehaḥ sa me ‘rcāṃ sthāpayiṣyati ||
krīḍamāno ‘smy ahaṃ tatra dṛṣṭvā mandārapuṣpitam |
kuṇḍāny ekādaśānyatra viddhi me santi sundari ||
mama caiva prabhāvena mandāraś ca mahādrumaḥ |
dvādaśyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ sa puṣyati drumottamaḥ ||
tīrthe mandārakuṇḍe tu ekabhaktoṣito naraḥ |
snānaṃ karoti śuddhātmā sa gacchet paramāṃ gatim ||
atra prāṇān pramuñceta kuṇḍe mandārasaṃjñite |
tapaḥ kṛtvā mahābhāgo mama lokaṃ sa gacchati ||
[218] tathā |
mandāraṃ paramaṃ guhyaṃ tasmin guhye śiloccaye |
dakṣiṇārdhe sthitaṃ cakraṃ vāmabhāge ca vai gadā ||
lāṅgalaṃ musalaṃ caiva śaṅkhaṃ tiṣṭhati cāgrataḥ |
etan na jānate kecin mama māyāpramohitāḥ ||
muktā bhāgavatāḥ sarvān śvetavārāhasāṃjñitān |
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe mandāramāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[219]
17
atha śālagrāmamāhātmyam
tatra varāhapurāṇe |
varāha uvāca |
asti sthānaṃ paraṃ guhyaṃ tasmin guhye śiloccaye |
gaṇḍakyāś cottare pārśve giritājasya dakṣiṇe ||
śālagrāma iti khyātaṃ mama bhaktasukhāvaham |
mamaitad rocate sthānaṃ girikūṭe śiloccaye ||
śālagrāma iti khyātaṃ bhaktasaṃsāramokṣaṇam |
pañcādaśātra guhyāni śālagrāme yaśasvini ||
adyāpi tan na jānanti muktā vārāhasāṃjñitān |
tathā |
tatra devahradaṃ nāma mama kṣetraṃ yaśasvini |
tatra krāntā mayā bhūmi baliyajñavināśane ||
sa hrado varadaś śreṣṭho manojñaḥ śubhaśītalaḥ |
agādhaś ca sadā bhūmi devānām api durlabhaḥ ||
tasmin hrade mahābhāge sadā mama kṛtodake |
[220]        cakrāṅkāḥ parito matsyāḥ paryaṭanti itas tataḥ ||
tasmin devahrade bhūmi caturviṃśatidvādaśīm |
sauvarṇāni ca padmāni dṛśyante udite ravau ||
dṛśyante tāvad etāni yāvan madhyandine sthitaḥ |
tatra snānaṃ prakurvīta daśabhaktoṣito naraḥ ||
daśānām aśvamedhānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ |
athātra muñcati prāṇān mama cittavyavasthitaḥ ||
aśvamedhaphalaṃ bhuktvā mama lokāya gacchati |
pūrvāmukhas tv ahaṃ tatra śālagrāme yaśasvini ||
bhaviṣyāmi na saṃdeho bhūmi bhāgavatapriyaḥ |
anyac ca te pravakṣyāmitac chṛṇuṣva vasundare ||
yatra guhyaṃ paraṃ kṣetraṃ na jānanti vimohitāḥ |
śivo me dakṣiṇe sthāne tiṣṭhate vigatajvaraḥ ||
lokānāṃ pravaraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ sarvalokadharo haraḥ |
tad avandya tu govindaṃ vandamānasya sundari ||
vṛthā gamanam ity āhur evam etan na saṃśayaḥ |
śivaṃ devaṃ tu vanditvā bhūmi māṃ yaś ca vandate ||
labhate puṣkalāṃ siddhiṃ yā mayā ca prakīrtitā |
samas taṃ ca mama kṣetraṃ daśayojanavistaram ||
mṛtā atra narā yānti māṃ satkarmānusāriṇaḥ |
[221] mahābhārate |
sadā saṃnihito yatra harir vasati bhārata |
śālagrāma iti khyāto viṣṇor adbhutakarmaṇaḥ ||
abhigamya trilokeśaṃ varadaṃ viṣṇum avyayam |
aśvamedham avāpnoti viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati ||
tilopadānaṃ dharmajña sarvapāpapramocanam |
samudrās tatra catvāraḥ kūpe saṃnihitāḥ sadā ||
tatropaspṛśya rājendra na durgatim avāpnuyāt |
abhigamya trilokeśaṃ varadaṃ viṣṇum avyayam ||
virājati yathā somo meghair mukto yudhiṣṭhira |
jātismara upaspṛśya śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ ||
jātismaratvam āpnoti snātvā tatra na saṃśayaḥ ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe śālagrāmamāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[222]
18
atha stutasvāmimāhātmyam
tatra varāhapurāṇe |
varāha uvāca |
stutasvāmīti vikhyātaṃ mama kṣetraṃ bhaviṣyati |
dvāparaṃ yugam āsādya tatra sthāsyāmi sundari ||
putro ‘haṃ vasudevasya devakyā garbhaniḥsṛtaḥ |
vāsudeva iti khyātaḥ sarvadānavasūdanaḥ ||
kaumudasya tu māsasya śuklapakṣe tu dvādaśīm |
trayāṇāṃ vājapeyānāṃ yajñānāṃ snānamātrataḥ ||
phalaṃ prāpnoti suśroṇi dhṛtam ātmani māṃ naraḥ |
athātra muñcati prāṇān mama karmānusārataḥ ||
vājapeyaphalaṃ bhuktvā mama lokaṃ prapadyate |
āyasī pratimā tatra mamābhedyā na saṃśayaḥ ||
bruvanti keciit kāṣṭheti āyasīty apare ‘bruvan |
pāṣāṇīti pare brūyuḥ pare vajramayīti ca ||
ūrdhvā vā yadi vādhastād bhavanti mama pārśvataḥ |
teṣāṃ tatheti spṛśanti śiromadhye tu na kvacit ||
ye ca paśyanti māṃ bhūmi maṇipūragirau sthitam |
[223]        trisaṃdhyam āvasantas tu matprasādāc ca sundari ||
te sarve kilbiṣān muktā yānti te paramāṃ gatim |
tathā |
suguhyaṃ pūrvapārśve tu mama kṣetrasya sundari |
adūrāt trīṇi krośāni parimāṇaṃ vidhīyate ||
dhūtapāpeti vikhyātaṃ tatra guhyaṃ paraṃ mama |
adūrāt pañca krośāni mama kṣetrasya paścime ||
tatra kuṇḍe mahābhāge mama yad rocate jalam |
tatra snānaṃ prakurvīta pañcarātroṣito naraḥ ||
yad vaṃśe duṣkṛtaṃ kiṃcid yac ca ātmani saṃsthitam |
tatra kṛtvodakaṃ bhadre dhūtapāpe yaśasvini ||
mahendreṇaiva modeta indraloke na saṃśayaḥ |
athātra muñcati prāṇān dhūpapāpe yaśasvini ||
indralokaṃ samutsṛjya mama lokaṃ sa gacchati |
tatrāścaryaṃ mahābhāge dhūtapāpe śṛṇuṣva me ||
vartate ca viśālākṣi manipūragirau sthitam |
tāvan na patate dhārā yāvat pāyaṃ na dhūyate ||
dhūtapāpe ca suśroṇi dhārātra patate mahīm |
tat tu kṣetraṃ varārohe samantāt pañcayojanam ||
tatra tiṣṭhāmy ahaṃ devi paścimāṃ diśam āsthitaḥ |
[224]        tatra cāmalakā bhadre adūrād ardhayonanāt ||
tān na kaścid vijānāti pāpakarmā narādhamaḥ |
upoṣya ca trirātrāṇi śraddadhāno jitendriyaḥ ||
tatra gatvā varārohe udite tu divākare |
atha madhyāhnavelāyāṃ yadi vāstaṃ gate ravau ||
ekacittena gantavyaṃ dhṛtiṃ kṛtvā suniścalām ||
yas tatra labhate bhadre phalam āmalaṃ śubham |
pañcarātreṇa paśyet tu tasmin bhūtagirau sa mām ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe stutasvāmimāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[225]
19
atha dvārakāmāhātmyam
tatra varāhapurāṇe |
varāha uvāca |
asti dvāravatī nāma nirmitā viśvakarmaṇā |
sudharmā nāma ca sabhā vāyur ānītavām mama ||
pañcayojanavistīrṇaṃ daśayojanam āyatā |
vasāmy atra varārohe pañcapañcāśataṃ samāḥ ||
bhārāvataraṇaṃ kṛtvā devānāṃ ca mahat priyam |
punar eṣyāmi suśroṇi ghātayitvā mahāsurān ||
mūrtim ātmani me bhūmi tasmin kathyamānāni me śṛṇu |
asti pañcaśaro nāma tasmin guhyaṃ paraṃ mama ||
samudratīram utsṛjya yatra bhaktamukhāvaham |
tatra snānaṃ tu kurvīta ṣaṣṭhanaktoṣito naraḥ ||
modate nākapṛṣṭhe tu apsarogaṇasaṃkule |
atha vā muñcati prāṇān mām eva pratipadyate ||
plakṣā vai tatra suśroṇi śataśākho mahādrumaḥ |
[226]        caturviṃśatidvādaśyāṃ sa bhavet phalabhāg asau ||
phalaṃ na labhate kaścin muktā bhāgavataṃ śucim |
labhante ye phalaṃ tatra guhye pañcaśarasya ca ||
te labhante parāṃ siddhim evam etan na saṃśayaḥ |
prabhāsam iti vikhyātaṃ tasmin guhyaṃ paraṃ mama ||
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta pañcarātroṣito naraḥ |
modate saptadvīpeṣu guhyāni ca sa gacchati ||
atha vā muñcati prāṇān prabhāse gatakilbiṣaḥ |
sarvalokān parityajya mallokaṃ prāpnute naraḥ ||
pañcakuṇḍam iti khyātaṃ tasmin guhyaṃ paraṃ mama |
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta pañcarātroṣito naraḥ ||
caturviṃśatidvādaśyāṃ madhyāhne ca divākare |
raupyasauvarṇakaṃ padmaṃ dṛśyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
tathā |
kādevakam iti khyātaṃ kuṇḍaṃ kṣetre paraṃ mama |
atra pītvā mahābhāge vṛṣṇayoparyam akṣayam ||
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta catuḥkālasamanvitaḥ |
tathā |
saṅgamaṃ nagaraṃ nāma tasmin kṣetre paraṃ mama |
mama cottarapārśve tu adūrāt saptayojanam ||
[227]        tat saro mama pārśve tu devānām api durlabham |
asti raivatakaṃ nāma tasmin kṣetre paraṃ mama ||
sarvalokeṣu vikhyātaṃ candramāḥ krīḍate yataḥ |
bahūdakaśilāpaṅkā guhāś cātra diśo daśa ||
vāpī ca śobhanā caiva devānām api durlabhā |
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta ṣaṣṭhakāloṣito naraḥ ||
gacchate somalokāya kṛtakṛtyo bhaven naraḥ |
athātra muñcati prāṇān mama lokaṃ prapadyate ||
viṣṇucaṅkramaṇaṃ nāma tasmin kṣetre paraṃ mama |
viddho ‘smi tatra vyādhena prāpto mūrtiṃ svakāṃ punaḥ ||
tatra kuṇḍaṃ mahābhāge maṇipūragirau sthitam |
tatrābhiṣekaṃ kurvīta nityakālam atandritaḥ ||
tathā |
tasmin kṣetre mahābhāge sthito ‘ham uttarāmukhaḥ |
sarvabhāgavataprītyai samudrataṭam āśritaḥ ||
ahaṃ rāmeṇa sahitaḥ sā ca rākā svasā śubhā |
trayas tatraiva tiṣṭhāmo dvārakāyāṃ yaśasvini ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe dvārakāmāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[228]
20
atha lohārgalamāhātmyam
tatra varāhapurāṇe |
varāha uvāca |
guhyam anyat pravakṣyāmi kāraṇaṃ satataṃ śubhe |
ahaṃ sindhos taṭe bhadre gatvā vai triṃśayojanam ||
mlecchamadhye varārohe himavantaṃ samāśritaḥ |
tatra lohārgalaṃ nāma nivāso me vidhīyate ||
guhyaṃ ca paramaṃ sthānaṃ samantāt pañcayojanam |
durgamaṃ duḥsahaṃ caiva dhāvanaiḥ pariveṣṭitam ||
tatra tiṣṭhāmy ahaṃ bhadre udīcīṃ diśam āsthitaḥ |
kecin na jānate tatra svamūrtiṃ mama saṃsthitām ||
ye tu yānti varārohe svakarmapariniṣṭhitāḥ |
te māṃ vaivātra paśyanti guhyāni ca yathā tathā ||
tatra kuṇḍe tu suśroṇi snānaṃ kurvīta niścitam |
upoṣya tu trirātrāṇi vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā ||
tatra svargasahasreṣu modate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
anyac ca te pravakṣyāmi tatra yat paramādbhutam ||
[229]        caturviṃśatidvādaśyāṃ na māṃsena vinā mama |
balir hi dīyate tatra sarvakāmaviśodhanaḥ ||
aśvo me kalpitas tatra sarvaratnavibhūṣitaḥ |
śvetaḥ kuudacakābhaḥ śaṅkhacakrasamanvitaḥ ||
śārṅgam etad dhanus tatra akṣasūtraṃ kamaṇḍaluḥ |
āsanaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ divyaṃ dīyate cānnam uttamam ||
asmin guhye mahābhāge kṣetre lohārgale mama |
siddhikāmena martyena gantavyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
samantāt pañcaviśaṃ tu yojanāni varānane |
na tasya karma vidyeta eṣa me niścayaḥ paraḥ ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe lohārgalamāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[230]
21
atha kedāramāhātmyam
tatra devīpurāṇe |
īśānaśikharaṃ nāma himakundendusaṃnibham |
tatra devaḥ svayaṃ sākṣāt tiṣṭhate parameśvaraḥ ||
īśāno lokavikhyātas trailokyaviditaḥ sa vai |
tatra retodakaṃ śakra pūrvaṃ devena nirmitam ||
gopitaṃ pihitaṃ tac ca na vijñānaṃ surāsuraiḥ |
tathā |
saṃsāratāraṇaṃ cānyapāpajālanikṛntanam |
kedāram udakaṃ pītvā punarjanma na vidyate |
na yoniṣu niyuñjyeta sa gacchec chāśvataṃ padam ||
tathā |
kṛtaṃ prakāśyaṃ tat kuṇḍe yad āsīd gopitaṃ mama |
manuṣyāṇāṃ hitārthāya majjatāṃ ca bhavārṇave ||
darśitaṃ jñānam udakaṃ pītvā na janmasaṃbhavaḥ |
[231]        na pātuṃ labhate brahmā na viṣṇur na purandaraḥ ||
pibanti mānuṣāḥ sarve yasya vai tuṣyate śivaḥ |
na kedārāt paro mokṣa evaṃ sākṣāc chivo ‘bravīt ||
pṛthivyāṃ yāti tīrthāni ā samudrāt sarāṃsi ca |
jñānaretodakasyaite kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm ||
aśvamedhasahasraṃ ca yojayet pṛthivīpatiḥ |
jñānaretodakasyaitat sahasrāṃśena pūryate ||
agniṣṭomasahasraṃ tu pauṇḍarīkaśataṃ tathā |
yo jayed dhavyasaṃpanno brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyas tathā ||
kedārasya tu tat sarvaṃ sahasrāṃśaṃ na pūrayet |
kāmapas strīsahasrāṇi piba khāda ca moda ca ||
kedāram udakaṃ pītvā sarvaṃ tarati duṣkṛtam ||
tathā |
jñānaṃ dīkṣātha vā śakra dhāraṇaṃ bhasmanas tathā |
retodakasya tat sarvaṃ kalāṃ nārhati ṣoḍaśīm ||
tathā |
yathā pibanti tat toyaṃ vidhiṃ tasya vadāmy aham |
gatvā mandākinīṃ puṇyāṃ tatra snātvā sa mānavaḥ ||
devānām udakaṃ datvā piṇḍaṃ pitṛṣu dāpayet |
namaskṛtvā tatheśānaṃ bhāvayuktena cetasā ||
gatvā kuṇḍasamīpe tu vidhivad dhimavat sthitaḥ |
natvā devaṃ sivaṃ tatra umāṃ cāpi maheśvarīm ||
nandīṃ gaṇapatiṃ caiva sarvāṃs tāṃś ca gaṇeśvarān |
[232]        śivo ‘ham iti saṃcintya pibed vāmena pāṇinā ||
triḥ pītvā vāmahastena dakṣiṇena punaḥ pibet |
trīn vārāṃs tena pītvā ca bhūyaś cāñjalinā pibet ||
tena pītvā tu vārāṃs trīn pibet tu balivardavat |
bhūmim ākramya jānubhyāṃ tathā hastadvayena tu ||
śiraḥ prasārya vakreṇa triḥ pibet punar eva tu |
utthāya nardet trīn vārān sphoṭet trīṃs tathāparān ||
namaskṛtya tatheśānaṃ kṛtāñjalipuṭaḥ sthitaḥ |
kṛtārthaḥ kṛtapuṇyo ‘sau śivasāyujyam āgataḥ ||
vidhinā pītam udakaṃ kulānāṃ tārayec chatam ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe kedāramāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[233]
22
atha naimiśamāhātmyam
tatra māhābhārate |
tatas tu naimiśaṃ gacchet puṇyaṃ siddhaniṣevitam |
tatra nityaṃ nivasati brahmā devagaṇair vṛtaḥ ||
naimiśaṃ prārthayānasya pāpasyārdhaṃ praṇaśyati |
praviṣṭamātras tu naraḥ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
tatra māsaṃ vased dhīro naimiśe tīrthatatparaḥ |
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni naimiśe tāni bhārata ||
abhiṣekān naras tatra niyato niyatāśanaḥ |
gavāmayasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
punāty āsaptamaṃ caiva kulaṃ bharatasattama |
yas tyajen naimiśe prāṇān upavāsaparāyaṇaḥ ||
sa modate svargaloke evam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
nityaṃ medhyaṃ ca puṇyaṃ ca naimiśaṃ nṛpasattama ||
gaṅgodbhedaṃ samāsādya trirātropoṣito naraḥ |
vājapeyam avāpnoti brahmabhūtaś ca jāyate ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe naimiṣamāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[234]
23
atha nānātīrthamāhātmyam
vāmanapurāṇe |
tasmiṃs tīrthavare snātvā dṛṣṭvā devaṃ trilocanam |
pūjayitvā suvarṇākhyaṃ naimiśaṃ prayatau tataḥ ||
tatra tīrthasahasrāṇi triṃśat pāpaharāṇi ca |
gomatyāṃ kāñcanasthāṃś ca sunandāmadhyavāsinaḥ ||
teṣu snātvārcya deveśaṃ pītavāsasamacyutam |
devadevaṃ tatheśānaṃ saṃpūjya vidhinā tataḥ ||
gayāyāṃ gopatiṃ draṣṭuṃ jagāma sa mahāsuraḥ |
sarasi brahmaṇaḥ snātvā kṛtvā cāsya pradakṣiṇam ||
piṇḍaṃ nirvapaṇaṃ puṇyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ sa cakāra ha |
udapāne tathā snātvā sarayūṃ ca jagāma saḥ ||
tasyāṃ snātvā samabhyarcya gopratāre jaleśayam |
mahābhārate |
gopratāraṃ tato gacchet sarayvās tīrtham uttamam |
[235]        yatra rāmo gataḥ svargaṃ sabhṛtyabalavāhanaḥ ||
dehaṃ tyaktvā divaṃ yātas tasya tīrthasya tejasā |
rāmasya ca prasādena vyavasāyāc ca bhārata ||
tasmiṃs tīrthe naraḥ snātvā gopratāre narādhipa |
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā svargaloke mahīyate ||
vāmanapurāṇe |
gopratāram abhidhāya |
upoṣya rajanīm ekāṃ virajāṃ sa nadīṃ yayau |
snātvā virajase tīrthe datvā piṇḍaṃ pitus tathā ||
darśanārthaṃ yayau śrīmān ajitaṃ puruṣottamam |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā puṇḍarīkākṣam akṣaraṃ paramaṃ śuciḥ ||
upoṣya ṣaḍdināny eṣa mahendraṃ dakṣiṇāṃ yayau |
tatra devavaraṃ śaṃbhum ardhanārīśvaraṃ haram ||
dṛṣṭvā saṃpūjya ca pitṝn mahendrasyottaraṃ gataḥ |
tatra devavaraṃ śaṃbhuṃ gopālaṃ somaśītalam ||
dṛṣṭvā snātvā somatīrthe sahyācalam upāgataḥ |
tatra snātvā madhodadhau vaikuṇṭhaṃ cārcya bhaktitaḥ ||
pitṝn piṇḍaiḥ samabhyarcya pāriyātraṃ giraṃ gataḥ |
tatra snātvā lāṅgulinyāṃ pūjayitvāparājitam ||
karapāde samāhṛtya viśvarūpaṃ dadarśa saḥ |
yatra devavaraḥ śaṃbhur devānāṃ tu supūjitaḥ ||
[236]        viśvarūpam athātmānaṃ darśayāmāsa yogavit |
tatra saṃkuṇikātoye snātvābhyarcya maheśvaram ||
jagāmādriṃ ca saugandhaṃ prahlādo malayāacalam |
mahāhrade tataḥ snātvā pūjayitvā ca śaṅkaram ||
tato jagāma yogātmā draṣṭuṃ vindhye sadāśivam |
trirātraṃ samupoṣyeśam avantīgarīṃ yayau ||
tatra śiprājale snātvā viṣṇuṃ saṃpūjya bhaktitaḥ |
śmaśānaṃ tu jagāmātha mahākālavapur haram ||
tasmin sa sarvasattvānāṃ tena rūpeṇa śaṅkaraḥ |
tāmasaṃ rūpam āsthāya saṃhāraṃ kurute vaśī ||
tatrasthena suveṣeṇa śvetakir nāma bhūpatiḥ |
rakṣitas tv antakaṃ hatvā sarvabhūtāpahāriṇam ||
sa tatra hṛṣṭo vasati nityaṃ caiva sahomayā |
vṛtaḥ prathamakoṭībhir vandadbhis tridaśārcitaḥ ||
dṛṣṭvātha ca mahākālaṃ kālakālāntakārakam |
daityānāṃ yamasaṃyamanaṃ mṛtyumṛtyuṃ vicitrakam (?) ||
śmaśānanilayaṃ śaṃbhuṃ bhūtanāthaṃ jagatpatim |
pūjayitvā śūladharaṃ jagāma niṣadhaṃ prati ||
tatrāmareśvaraṃ devaṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃpūjya bhaktitaḥ |
[237]        mahodayaṃ samabhyetya hayagrīvaṃ dadarśa saḥ ||
aśvatīrthe naraḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā ca turagānanam |
śrīdharaṃ tu vibhuṃ pūjya pāñcālaviṣayaṃ yayau ||
bhadreśvaraṃ gaṇair guptaṃ putram arthapater atha |
pañcātmakaṃ rathī dṛṣṭvā prayāgaṃ prayato yayau ||
snātvā saṃnihite tīrthe yāmune lokaviśrute |
dṛṣṭvā vaṭeśvaraṃ rudraṃ mādhavaṃ yogaśāyinam ||
dvāv eva bhaktitaḥ pūjya pūjayitvā maheśvaram |
mādhamāsam athopoṣya tato vārāṇasīṃ gataḥ ||
daśāśvamedhe gaṅgāyāṃ tīrthe suragṛhādiṣu |
sarvapāpaharāsv eṣu saṃpūjya pitṛdevatāḥ ||
pradakṣiṇīkṛtya purīṃ pūjyāvimuktakeśavau |
lolaṃ divākaraṃ dṛṣṭvā tato madhuvanaṃ yayau ||
tatra svayaṃbhuvaṃ devaṃ dṛṣṭvā cāsurasattamaḥ |
tam abhyarcya mahātejāḥ puṣkarāraṇyam āgatam ||
teṣu triṣv api tīrtheṣu snātvārcya pitṛdevatāḥ |
puṣkarākṣam ayogandhiṃ brahmāṇaṃ cāpy apūjayat ||
tato bhūyaḥ sarasvatyās tīrthe trailokyaviśrute |
koṭitīrthe rudrakoṭiṃ dadarśa vṛṣabhadhvajam ||
naimiśe ye dvijavarā māgadheyāḥ sasaindhavāḥ |
dharmāraṇyāḥ pauṣkareyā daṇḍakāraṇyakās tathā ||
[238]        cāmpeyā bhṛgukacchīyā devikā tīragāś ca ye |
te tatra śaṅkaraṃ draṣṭuṃ samāyātā dvijātayaḥ ||
koṭisaṃkhyāstriyaḥ siddhā haradarśanalālasāḥ |
ahaṃpūrvam ahaṃpūrvam ity evaṃ vadato mune ||
tān saṃkṣudyān haro dṛṣṭvā maharṣīn dagdhakilbiṣān |
teṣām evānukampārthaṃ koṭimūrtir abhūd dharaḥ ||
tatas te munayaḥ prītāḥ sarva eva maheśvaram |
saṃpūjayanti te bhaktyā tīrthaṃ kṛtvā pṛthak pṛthak ||
ity evaṃ rudrakoṭīti nāma śobham ajāyata |
tān dadarśa mahātejāḥ prahlādo bhaktimān vāśī ||
koṭitīrthe naraḥ snātvā tarpayitvā vasūn pitṝn |
rudrakoṭīṃ samabhyarcya jagāma kurujāṅgalam ||
tatra devavaraṃ sthāṇuṃ śaṅkaraṃ pārvatīpriyam |
sarasvatījale magnaṃ dadarśa surapūjitam ||
sārasvate ‘mbhasi snātvā sthāṇuṃ saṃpūjya bhaktitaḥ |
kṣīrikāṃ ca samabhyetya nīlakaṇṭhaṃ dadarśa ha ||
nīlatīrthajale snātvā pūjayitvā tataḥ śivam |
jagāma sāgarānte sa prabhāse draṣṭum īśvaram ||
snātvā ca sa saṃgame nadyāḥ sarasvatyārṇavasya ca |
someśvaraṃ lokapatiṃ dadarśa ca kapardinam ||
yo dakṣaśāpanirdagdhaḥ kṣayī tārādhipaḥ śaśī |
[239]        āpyāyitaḥ śaṅkareṇa viṣṇunā ca kapardinā ||
tān abhyarcya varān devān ājagāma mahālayam |
tatra rudraṃ samabhyarcya sa jagāmottarān kurūn ||
padmanābhaṃ samabhyarcya saptagodāvaraṃ yayau |
tatra snātvārcya deveśaṃ bhīmaṃ trailokyavedinam ||
gatvā dāruvane śrīmān śrīliṅgaṃ pradadarśa ha |
tam abhyarcyātha brahmāṇīṃ gatvārcya tridaśeśvarīm ||
plakṣāṃ ca taraṇaṃ gatvā śrīnivāsam apūjayat |
tataś ca kuṇḍinaṃ gatvā saṃpūjya prāṇitṛptidam ||
sūryārake caturbāhuṃ pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ |
māgadhāraṇyam āsādya dadarśa vasudhādhipa ||
tam arcayitvā viśveśaṃ sa jagāma prajāmukham |
mahātīrthe tataḥ snātvā vāsudevaṃ praṇamya ca ||
śoṇaṃ saṃprāpya saṃpūjya rukmadharmāṇam īśvaram |
mahākāśyāṃ mahādevaṃ haṃsākhyaṃ bhaktimān atha ||
pūjayitvā jagāmātha saindhavāraṇyam uttamam |
taṃ dṛṣṭvārcya hariṃ cāsau tīrthaṃ kanakhalaṃ yayau ||
tatrārcya rudraṃ kāmeśaṃ vīrabhadraṃ ca dānavaḥ ||
gaṇādhipaṃ ca meghābhaṃ yayāv atha girivrajam |
tatra devaṃ paśupatiṃ lokanāthaṃ maheśvaram ||
saṃpūjayitvā vidhivat kāmarūpaṃ jagāma ha |
[240]        śaśiprabhaṃ devavaraṃ trinetraṃ
saṃpūjayitvā sahitaṃ mṛḍānyā |
jagāma tīrthapravaraṃ mahākhyaṃ
tasmin mahādevam apūjayac ca ||
tatra trikūṭaṃ girirājaputraṃ
draṣṭuṃ jagāmātha sa cakrapāṇim |
tam īḍya bhaktyā ca gajendramokṣaṇam
jajāpa japyaṃ paramaṃ pavitram ||
tatroṣya daityeśvarasūnur ādadān
māsatrayaṃ mūlaphalaṃ bubhakṣī |
nivedya viprapravareṣu kāñcanaṃ
jagāma ghoraṃ sa hi daṇḍakaṃ vanam ||
tatra divyaṃ mahāśaṅkhaṃ vanaspativapurdharam |
dadarśa puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ mahāntaṃ cāpadhāriṇam ||
tasyādhastāt trirātraṃ tu mahābhāgavato ‘suraḥ |
sthitaḥ sthaṇḍilaśāyī ca paṭhan sārasvataṃ stavam ||
tasmāt tīrthavaraṃ vindhyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
jagāma dānavo draṣṭuṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ harim ||
mahābhārate |
tato vārāṇasīṃ gatvā devam arcya vṛṣadhvajam |
kapilāhradam upaspṛśya rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet ||
[241]        mārkaṇḍeyasya rājendra tīrtham āsādya durlabham |
gomatīgaṅgayoś caiva saṃgame lokaviśrute ||
agniṣṭomam avāpnoti kulaṃ caiva samuddharet |
matsyapurāṇe |
kṛtaśaucaṃ mahātīrthaṃ sarvapāpaniṣūdanam |
yatrāste narasiṃhas tu svayam eva janārdanaḥ ||
tathā |
vaśvāpadaṃ rudrakoṭiṃ siddheśvaramahālayam |
gokarṇaṃ bhadrakarṇaṃ ca suvarṇākhyaṃ tathaiva ca ||
etāni hi pavitrāṇi sāṃnidhyāt saṃdhyayor dvayoḥ |
kālañjaravanaṃ caiva śaṅkukarṇaṃ sthaleśvaram ||
etāni hi pavitrāṇi sāṃnidhyaitaṃ mama priye |
devīpurāṇe |
gaṅgādvāraṃ kurukṣetraṃ narmadāmarakaṇṭakam |
yamunāsaṅgamaṃ puṇyaṃ vidiśā vetravaty api ||
sarayūḥ kauśikī vindhyā gaṇḍakī ca sarasvatī |
candrabhāgā nadī puṇyā nadī godāvarī tathā ||
kāverī gomatī devī devikā varaṇā tathā |
etāḥ puṇyatamā nadyo grahaṇādiṣu kīrtitāḥ ||
anyāś ca bahavaḥ puṇyāḥ sarvakāleṣu puṇyadāḥ |
[242]        ayane viṣuve khyātā vyatīpāte tathaiva ca ||
dīnacchidre tathā darśe brāhmaṇānāṃ ca saṃgame |
saṃmodeṣu samājeṣu ekārthe sapta pañca ca ||
“saṃmodeṣu” utsaveṣu; “samājeṣu” sabhāsu; “ekārthe” ekasmin prayojane; “sapta pañca ca” militā bhavanti tadā pūrvoktā nadyaḥ puṇyatamās tarpaṇaṃ ca tatra sarvakāmapradam |
tathā |
evaṃ parvasu sarveṣu candre sarvakalāsu ca |
tṛtīyāyāṃ tu vaiśākhyām aṣtamyāṃ kujavāsare ||
caturdaśyāṃ ca kṛṣṇāyāṃ bhaumahe pitṛtarpaṇe |
kartavyaṃ sarvakāmānāṃ pūraṇāya dvijottamaiḥ ||
amāvāsyāyāṃ saṃkrāntau śivādityau ca yo naraḥ |
yajate bhagavān prītaḥ sa pūto bhavate mune ||
tathā |
kārttike grahaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ gaṅgāyamunasaṃgame |
mārge tu grahaṃ puṇyaṃ devikāyāṃ mahāmune ||
pauṣe tu narmadā puṇyā māghe saṃnihitā śubhā |
phālgune varaṇā khyātā caitre puṇyā sarasvatī ||
vaiśākhe tu mahāpuṇyā candrabhāgā sarid varā |
jyeṣṭhe tu kauśīkī puṇyā āṣāḍhe tāpikā nadī ||
[243]        śrāvaṇe sindhunāmā ca bhādramāse ca gaṇḍakī |
āśvine sarayūś caiva bhūyaḥ puṇyā tu narmadā ||
godāvarī mahāpuṇyā candre rāhusamanvita |
sūrye caiva śaśigraste tato bhūte mahāmune ||
narmadā toyasaṃsparśāt kṛtakṛtyā bhavanti te |
ye sūrye saiṃhike yena graste revājalaṃ narāḥ ||
spṛśanti cāvagāhante na te prakṛtimānavāḥ |
spṛṣṭvā śatakratuphalaṃ dṛṣṭvā godānajaṃ phalam ||
snātvāśvamedhatulyaṃ ca spṛṣṭvā sautrāmaṇīṃ labhet |
ravicandroparāge tu ayane cottare tathā ||
evaṃ gaṅgāpi draṣtavyā tadvad eva sarasvatī |
tathā |
śivādityaphalaṃ yac ca maṇḍale samudāhṛtam |
saṃgrahe maṇḍale yāge tad atra prāpnuyān naraḥ ||
śivādityayor maṇḍale kṣetre tayor yat phalaṃ tad rāhugraste śaśisūryamaṇḍale yāge pūjāyāṃ prāpnotīty arthaḥ |
araṇyeṣūṣare kṣetre yat puṇyaṃ samudāhṛtam |
tad atra kālamāhātmyād uparāge ‘dhikaṃ bhavet ||
yena āhṛtya toyena snānaṃ kuryur gṛhe ‘pi vā |
samantreṇaiva pūtena teṣāṃ puṇyaṃ tato ‘dhikam |
ātmacintānusāreṇa pātre taijasapārthive ||
iṣṭakāśailakāṣṭhair vā phalaṃ prāpnoty aninditam |
yenaiva mṛttikāṃ tasmāt tīrthād āhṛtya bhojane ||
[244]        prātaḥ prātaḥ samutthāya vādayanti narottamāḥ |
te sarve pāpanirmuktā bhavanti vigatāmayāḥ ||
phalapuṣpopahāreṇa yo vā tasmin ravīśvarau |
snātvā saṃpūjayed vipraḥ sa bhaved vigatāmayaḥ ||
mantrapūtena toyena kumbhaiḥ puṇyajalānvitaiḥ |
saphalair vidhinā snātvā sarvakāmāṃl labheta saḥ ||
tad etat kathitaṃ puṇyaṃ mayā brahmamukhāc chrutam |
tat samagraṃ bhavet tasya araṇyeṣūṣareṣu ca ||
araṇyāni pravakṣyāmi tathā caivoṣarāṇi ca |
saindhavaṃ daṇḍakāraṇyaṃ naimiśaṃ kurujāṅgalam ||
utpalāvartakāraṇyaṃ jambūmārgaṃ ca puṣkaram |
himavān sahyato ‘raṇyam ity etat parikīrtitam ||
naraś caiteṣv araṇyeṣu yas tu prāṇān parityajet |
brahmaloke ‘tithir bhūtvā sa yāti paramāṃ gatim ||
kālikā śikharākhye ca kālakālālaye ‘pi yā |
kālañjare mahākāle tulyaṃ vai teṣu yat phalam ||
vāyupurāṇe |
kālañjare daśārṇāyāṃ naimiśe kurujāṅgale |
vārāṇasyāṃ nagaryāṃ ca deyaṃ śrāddham prayatnataḥ ||
gatvā caitāni pūtaḥ syāc chrāddham akṣayyam eva ca |
japahomatapodhyānaṃ yat kiṃcit sukṛtaṃ bhavet ||
[245] mahabhārate |
śatadruṃ candrabhāgāṃ ca vitastāṃ cormimālinīm |
vigāhya vai nirāhāro nirmamo munivad bhavet ||
kāśmīramaṇḍale nadyo yāḥ patanti mahānadīḥ |
tā nadīḥ sindhum āsādya śīlavān svargam āpnuyāt ||
puṣkaraṃ ca prayāgaṃ ca naimiśaṃ sāgarodakam |
devikā sindhumārgaṃ ca svargabinduṃ vigāhya ca ||
vibodhate vimānasthaḥ so ‘psarobhir abhiṣṭutaḥ |
hiraṇyabinduṃ vikṣobhya prayataś cābhivandanam ||
kuśeśayaṃ ca deveśaṃ pūyate tasya kilbiṣam |
gaṅgādvāre kuśāvarte vindhyake nīlaparvate ||
tathā kanakhale snātvā dhūtapāpmā divaṃ vrajet |
apāṃ hrada upaspṛśya vājapeyaphalaṃ labhet ||
brahmacārī jitakrodhaḥ satyasandhas tv ahiṃsakaḥ |
yatra bhāgīrathī gaṅgā uttarāṃ bhajate diśam ||
maheśvarasyādhiṣṭhāne yo nars tv abhiṣiñcati |
ekamāsaṃ nirāhāraḥ svayaṃ paśyati devatām ||
saptagaṅge trigaṅge ca indramārge ca tat payaḥ |
śubhaṃ vigāhate yo vai na naro jāyate punaḥ ||
mahāhrada upaspṛśya yo ‘gnihotraparaḥ śuciḥ |
ekakālaṃ nirāhāraḥ siddhiṃ kālena sa vrajet ||
[246]        mahāhrada upaspṛśya bhṛgutuṅge tv alolupaḥ |
trirātropoṣito bhūtvā mucyate brahmahatyayā ||
kanyākūpa upaspṛśya parṇaśāyī kṛtodakaḥ |
deveṣu kīrtiṃ labhate yaśasā ca virājate ||
mahāgaṅgām upaspṛśya kṛttikāṅgārage tathā |
pakṣaṃ ekaṃ nirāhāraḥ svargam āpnoti nirmalam ||
vimānakam upaspṛśya parṇaśayyāṃ kṛtodakaḥ |
deveṣu kīrtiṃ labhate yaśasā ca virājate ||
[vaimānika upaspṛśya] kiṅkiṇīkāśrame tathā |
nivāse ‘psarasāṃ puṇye kāmacāro mahīyate ||
kālikāśramam āsādya vipāśāyāṃ kṛtodakaḥ |
brahmacārī jitakrodhas trirātrān mucyate pumān ||
āśrame kṛttikāyāṃ tu snātvā yas tarpayet pitṝn |
toṣayitvā mahādevaṃ nirmalaṃ svargam āpnuyāt ||
mahāsara upaspṛśya trirātropoṣito naraḥ |
trayāṇāṃ sthāvarāṇāṃ ca dvipadātvād bhayam tyajet ||
devadāruvane snātvā pūtapāpmā kṛtodakaḥ |
devavaṃśam avāpnoti saptarātroṣitaḥ śuciḥ ||
kauśāmbe ca kuśastambe droṇadharmapade tathā |
atha prapatane madhye sevyate ‘psarasāṃ gaṇaiḥ ||
citrakūṭe janasthāne tathā mandākinījale |
[247]        vigāhya vai nirāhāro rājalakṣmīṃ niyacchati ||
śyāmāyās tv āśramaṃ gatvā uṣitvā cābhiṣicya ca |
trīṃs trirātrān samādhāya gandharvanagaraṃ vrajet ||
kauśikīdvāram āsādya vāyubhakṣas tv alolupaḥ |
ekaviṃśatirātreṇa svargam ārohate naraḥ ||
mataṅgavāpyāṃ yaḥ snāyād ekarātreṇa sidhyati |
jambūmārge tribhir māsaiḥ saṃyataḥ susamāhitaḥ ||
ahorātreṇa caitreṇa siddhiṃ samadhigacchati |
prabhāse tv ekarātreṇa amāvāsyāṃ samāhitaḥ ||
sidhyate tu mahābāho na naro jāyate punaḥ |
ujjānakam upaspṛśya ārṣṇīṣeṇasya cāśrame ||
piṅgāyāś cāśrame snātvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
kulyāyāṃ samupaspṛśya japtvā caivāghamarṣaṇam ||
aśvamedham avāpnoti trirātropoṣitaḥ śuciḥ |
piṇḍārakam upaspṛśya ekarātroṣito naraḥ ||
agniṣṭomam avāpnoti prabhātāṃ śarvarīṃ śuciḥ |
mainākaparvate snātvā tathā saṃdhyām upāsya ca ||
nadaṃ brahmasaro gatvā dharmāraṇyopaśobhitam |
pauṇḍarīkam avāpnoti prabhātāṃ śarvarīṃ śuciḥ ||
mainākaparvate snātvā tathā saṃdhyām upāsya ca |
kāmaṃ jitvātha vai māsaṃ sarvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
[248]        kālodakaṃ nandikūṭaṃ tathaivottaramānasam |
abhyetya yojanaśatād bhrūṇahāpi pramucyate ||
nandīśvarasya mūrtiṃ tu dṛṣṭvā mucyeta kilbiṣaiḥ |
svargamārgahrade snātvā svargalokaṃ sa gacchati ||
vikhyāto himavān puṇyaḥ śaṅkaraś ca suro giriḥ |
ākaraḥ sarvaratnānāṃ siddhacāraṇasevitaḥ ||
śarīram utsṛjet tatra vidhipūrvam anāśake |
adhruvaṃ jīvitaṃ jñātvā yo vai vedāntago dvijaḥ ||
abhyarcya devatās tatra namaskṛtya munīṃs tathā |
tataḥ siddho divaṃ. gacched brahmalokaṃ sanātanam ||
kāmaṃ krodhaṃ ca lobhaṃ ca yo jitvā tīrtham āvaset |
na tena kiṃcid aprāpyaṃ tīrthābhigamanād bhavet ||
yāny agamyāni tīrthāni durgāṇi viṣamāṇi ca |
manasā tāni gamyāni sarvāṇy eva samāsataḥ ||
yamaḥ |
kārtikīṃ puṣkare snātvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
māghe prātaḥ prayāge tu mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ ||
gayāśīrṣe vaṭe snātvā māghakṛṣṇāṣṭamīṃ śuciḥ |
narmadāmbhasi ca snātaḥ pauṣakṛṣṇāṣṭamīṃ śubhām ||
śālagrāme tathā caitrīṃ sanake ca caturdaśī |
śukle snātvā daśamyāṃ tu puṇḍarīke tathaiva ca ||
[249]        saṃnihatyam amāvāsyāṃ prabhāte vā tathā punaḥ |
ojase tu naraḥ snātvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ ||
yamunāyāṃ tathā māghe kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśīm |
vaiśākhe śuklapakṣe tu tṛtīyāyāṃ tathaiva ca ||
gaṅgātoye naraḥ snātvā mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ |
proṣṭhapadasya māsasya śuklā yā syāc caturdaśī ||
tasyāṃ snāto vitastāyāṃ mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ |
candrabhāgāmbhasi snātvā māghapuṣyatrayodaśīm ||
aṣṭamīrevatītoye tathā revāvatāmbhasi |
āṣāḍhyāṃ sarayūtoye śrāvaṇyāṃ satataṃ tathā ||
śravaṇarkṣam anuprāpya yatra kvacana saṃgame |
sarvāṃ caturdaśīṃ pūte devikāyāṃ tathāmbhasi ||
mahāgaṅgām upaspṛśya kṛttikāṅgārake tathā |
trayodaśyām āśvayuje viśālāyāṃ tathaiva ca ||
devalaḥ |
kṛtvā pāpam avijñātaṃ bhrūṇahatyāpi tat punaḥ |
vinaśyati mahāyajñair atha vā tīrthacaryayā ||
atha sarasvatī-gaṅgā-yamunā-narmadā-vipāśā-vitastā-kauśikī-nandāvarī-candrabhāgā-rūtyalāvatī-sindhur-arjunīyāparṇāśā-śoṇasnāyī-airāvatī-pāṣāṇātīrtha-gomati-gaṇḍakī-bāhudā-ramyā-devikā-godāvarī-kāverī-tāmraparṇī[250]-parṇī-carmaṇvatī-revatī-bhīmā-rakṣuvīraṇā ceti mahānadyaḥ puṇyatamāḥ |
gaṅgādvāraṃ kanakhalaṃ śukaraṃ prayāgo gaṅgāsāgara iti gaṅgāyās tīrthāni |
plakṣaprasravaṇaṃ vṛddhakanyākaṃ sārasvatam ādityatīrthaṃ kauberaṃ vaijayantaṃ pṛthūdakam naimiśaṃ vinaśanaṃ vaṃśodbhedaṃ prabhāsam iti sārasvatāni |
puṣkaram urvaśaṃ gaṅgayāyāṃ gayāśiraḥ kurukṣetraṃ prayāga iti [tīrthāni] | kṣetrāṇi vārāṇasī mahābhairavaṃ devadāruvanaṃ kedāram udyamaṃ rudramahālayam | trastāvatāraṃ chagalaṇḍaviṣṇuśiraṃ mahākālaṃ kālañjaraṃ jambumārgaṃ ceti āyatanāni |
himavān sahyo vidvāro mahendro hariścandraḥ śataśṛṅgo devaparvato vindhyaparvataś ceti parvatānāṃ nāmāni |
evam etāny anyāny api kṣetrāṇy āyatanaparvatāśramatīrthāni puṇyatamāni | tadartham abhigamya vratopavāsaniyamayuktas tryaham agāhamānas trirātram uṣitvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate svastimāṃś ca bhavatīti | tatra ślokau |
abhisaṃgamya tīrthāni puṇyāny āyatanāni ca |
naraḥ pramucyate pāpād brāhmaṇāś ca tapasvinaḥ ||
sarvāḥ samudragāḥ puṇyāḥ sarve puṇyā nagottamāḥ |
sarvam āyatanaṃ puṇyaṃ sarve puṇyā vanāśramāḥ ||
[251] brahmapurāṇe |
nadyāṃ pratyekaśaḥ snāte bhaved godānajaṃ phalam |
gopradānaiś ca daśabhis tāsāṃ puṇyaṃ tu saṃgame ||
narasiṃhapurāṇe |
mandarasthaṃ hariṃ devaṃ brahmā pṛcchati keśavam |
bhagavān devadeveśa śaṅkhacakragadādhara ||
keṣu keṣu ca kṣetreṣu draṣṭavyo ‘si mayā prabho |
bhaktaiś cānyaiḥ suraśreṣṭha muktikāmair viśeṣataḥ ||
yāni te guhyanāmāni kṣetrāṇi ca jagatpate |
tāny ahaṃ śrotum icchāmi tvattaḥ padmāyatekṣaṇa ||
kiṃ japan sugatiṃ yāti naro nityam atandritaḥ |
tvadbhaktānāṃ hitārthāya tan me vada sureśvara ||
bhagavān uvāca |
śṛṇuṣvāvahito brahmaṇ guhyanāmāni me ‘dhunā |
kṣetrāṇi caiva gulmāni tava vakṣyāmi tattvataḥ ||
kokāmukhe varāhaṃ ca mandāre madhusūdanam |
anantaṃ kapilaṃ dvīpe prabhāse ravinandanam ||
matsyodapāne vaikuṇṭhe mahendre tu nṛpāntakam |
ṛṣabhe tu mahāviṣṇuṃ dvārakāyāṃ tu bhūpatim ||
pāṇḍisahye tu deveśaṃ vasutuṅge jagatpatim |
bhallivane mahāyogiṃ citrakūṭe narādhipam ||
[252]        naimiśe pītavāsaṃ ca gayāṃ niṣkramaṇe harim |
śālagrāme tapovasam acintyaṃ gandhamādane ||
kubjāmrake hṛṣīkeśaṃ gaṅgādvāre payodharam |
garuḍadhvajaṃ toṣalake govindaṃ nāgasāhvaye ||
vṛndāvane tu gopālaṃ mathurāyāṃ svayaṃbhuvam |
kedāre mādhavaṃ vidyād vārāṇasyāṃ tu keśavam ||
puṣkare puṣkarākhyaṃ tu dhṛṣadvatyāṃ dhṛṣadhvaje |
tṛṇabinduvane vīramaṇokaṃ sindhusāgare ||
kuśīvaṭe mahābāhum amṛtaṃ taijase vane |
viśākhayūpe viśveśaṃ nārasiṃhaṃ mahāvane ||
lohārgale ripuharaṃ devaśāle trivikramam |
puruṣottamaṃ kuśavane kubjake vāmanaṃ viduḥ ||
vidyādharaṃ vitastāyṃ vārāhe dharaṇīdharam |
devadāruvane guhyaṃ kāveryāṃ nāgaśāyinam ||
prayāge yogamūrtiṃ ca payoṣṭyāṃ sundaraṃ viduḥ |
kumāratīrthe kaumāraṃ lauhitye hayaśīriṣam ||
ujjayinyāṃ vikramaṃ vidyāl liṅgasphoṭe caturbhujam |
hariharaṃ tuṅgabhadrāyāṃ dṛṣṭvā pāpāt pramucyate ||
viśvarūpaṃ kurukṣetre maṇikuṇḍe halāyudham |
lokanātham ayodhyāyāṃ kuṇḍine rukmiṇīpatim ||
bhāṇḍire vāsudevaṃ ca cakratīrthe sudarśanam |
ādyaṃ piṣṇupade [viṣṇu-?] vidyāc chūkare śūkaraṃ viduḥ ||
[253]        brahmeśaṃ mānase tīrthe daṇḍake śyāmalaṃ viduḥ |
trikūṭe nāgamokṣaṃ ca merukūṭe ca bhāskaram ||
virajaṃ puṣpabhadrāyāṃ bālaṃ cāmīkare viduḥ |
yaśaskaraṃ vipāśāyāṃ māhiṣmatyāṃ hutāśanam ||
kṣīrābdhau padmanābhaṃ ca vimale ca sanātanam |
śivanadyāṃ śivakaraṃ gayāyāṃ ca gadādharam ||
sarvatra paramātmānaṃ yaḥ paśyati sa mucyate |
aṣṭaṣaṣṭi tu nāmāni kīrtitāni mayā tava ||
kṣetrāṇi caiva puṇyāni kathitāni viśeṣataḥ |
etāni mama nāmāni rahasyāni prajāpate ||
yaḥ paṭhet prātar utthāya śṛṇuyād vāpi nityaśaḥ |
gavāṃ śatasahasrasya dattasya phalam aśnute ||
dine dine śucir bhūtvā kṣetrāṇy etāni yaḥ smaret |
duḥsvapnaṃ na bhavet tasya matprasādān na saṃśayaḥ ||
aṣṭaṣaṣṭi tu nāmāni trikālaṃ yaḥ paṭhen naraḥ |
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo mama loke sa modate ||
draṣṭavyāni yathāśakti kṣetrāṇy etāni mānavaiḥ |
vaiṣṇavais tu viśeṣeṇa teṣāṃ muktiṃ vadāmy aham ||
hariṃ samabhyarcya tadagrataḥ sthito
hariṃ smaran viṣṇudine viśeṣataḥ |
imaṃ stavaṃ yaḥ paṭhate sadā naraḥ
prāpnoti viṣṇor amṛtātmakaṃ padam ||
[254] tathā |
udadiśya stavavaraṃ brahmāṇaṃ bhagavān hariḥ |
punar apy āha tīrthāni yāni tāni nibodha me ||
gaṅgā tu prathamā puṇyā yamunā gomatī punaḥ |
sarayūḥ sarasvatī caiva candrabhāgātha carmiṇā ||
kurukṣetre gayāṃ caiva puṣkarāṇi tathārbudam |
narmadā ca mahāpuṇyā tīrthāny etāni cottare ||
tāpī payoṣṇī puṇye dve utsaṅge tīrtham uttamam |
godāvarī mahāpuṇyā sarvatra dvijasattama ||
tuṅgabhadrā mahāpuṇyā yatrāhaṃ kamalodbhava |
hareṇa sārdhaṃ prītyānuvasāmi munipūjitaḥ ||
tuṅgā ca dakṣiṇe gaṅgā kāverī ca viśeṣataḥ |
sahye cāmalakagrāme sthito ‘haṃ kamalodbhava ||
devadeveśa nāmnā tu tvayā brahman sadārcitaḥ |
tatra tīrthāny anekāni sarvapāpaharāṇi vai ||
teṣu snātvā ca pītvā ca pāpaṃ muñcati mānavaḥ |
ity evaṃ kathayitvā tu tīrthāni madhusūdanaḥ ||
brahmaṇe gatavān brahmaṇ brahmāpi svapuraṃ gataḥ |
tathā |
sūta uvāca |
śrīsahyāmalakagrāme devadeveśam avyayam |
[255]        ārādhya siddhiṃ saṃprāptāḥ saptatiṃ ca caturmukhaiḥ ||
tasya pādatalāt tīrthaṃ niścitaṃ paścimāmukham |
tac cakratīrtham abhavat puṇyaṃ pāpaharaṃ nṛṇām ||
cakratīrthe naraḥ snātvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
bahuvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate ||
śaṅkhatīrthe naraḥ snātvā vājapeyaphalaṃ labhet |
puṣyamāse tu puṣyārke tadyātrādi vasan mune ||
brahmaṇaḥ kuṇḍikāpūrvaṃ gaṅgātoyena pūritā |
sahyādrau patitā brahmaṃs tatra tīrtham abhūn mahān ||
nāmnā tu kuṇḍikā tīrthaṃ śilāvṛkṣasamīpataḥ |
tatra ye munayaḥ snātās te ‘pi siddhim avāpnuyuḥ ||
trirātropoṣito bhūtvā yas tiṣthet tatra mānavaḥ |
sarvapāpavinirmukto brahmaloke mahīyate ||
kuṇḍikātīrthottarataḥ piṇḍasthānāc ca dakṣiṇam |
ṛṇamocanakaṃ nāma tīrtham tad guhyam uttamam ||
trirātropoṣito yas tu tatra snānaṃ samācaret |
ṛṇais tribhir asau brahman mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā pitṝṇāṃ tu piṇḍasthāne ca yo naraḥ |
pitṝn uddiśya vidhivat piṇḍaṃ nirvāpayiṣyati ||
sutṛptāḥ pitaro yānti pitṛlokaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
pañcarāroṣitaḥ snātvā yas tīrthe pāpanāśane ||
[256]        sarvapāpakṣayaṃ prāpya viṣnuloke mahīyate |
tatraiva mahatīṃ dhārāṃ śirasā yas tu dhārayet ||
sarvatra tu phalaṃ prāpya nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyate |
dhanuḥpāte mahātīrthe bhakto yaḥ snānam ācaret ||
āyuryogaphalaṃ prāpya svargaloke mahīyate |
śatavindau naraḥ snātvā śatakratupuraṃ vrajet ||
sahasravindau mahātīrthe snātvā viṣṇupuraṃ vrajet |
varāhatīrthe viprendra sahye yaḥ snānam ācaret ||
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā viṣṇuloke mahīyate |
ākāśagaṅgā nāmnā ca sahyādres tīrtham uttamam ||
śilātalonnato brahman niryātā śvetamṛttikā |
tām ālabhya tu yaḥ snāti naro dvijavarottama ||
sarvakratuphalaṃ prāpya viṣṇuloke mahīyate |
vāgmatīsaṃgamaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ devasya dakṣiṇe ||
ekarātroṣito yas tu tatra snāto vicakṣaṇaḥ |
aśvamedhaphalaṃ prāpya viṣṇuloke mahīyate ||
brahmann amalasahyādrer yat tat toyaṃ vinirgatam |
taṃ tu tīrthaṃ vijānīhi snātvā pāpāt pramucyate ||
eteṣu tīrtheṣu naro ‘vagāhya
puṇyeṣu sahyādrisamudbhaveṣu |
datvā tu puṣpāṇi hariṃ ca dṛṣṭvā
vihāya pāpaṃ praviśec ca viṣṇum ||
[257]        sakṛt tīrthāni seveta gaṅgṃ caiva punaḥ punaḥ |
sarvatīrthamayī gaṅgā sarvadevamayo hariḥ ||
aho muñcaṃs turagamedhasahasratulyaṃ
yac cakratīrtham iti vedavido vadanti |
tatra snātā virajaso na punar bhavanti
pādau praṇamya śirasā madhusūdanasya ||
gaṅgāprayāgagayanaimiśapuṣkarāṇi
puṇyāni yāni kurujāṅgalayāmunāni |
kālena tīrthasalilāni punanti pāpāt
pādodakaṃ bhagavatas tu punāti sadyaḥ ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe nānātīrthamāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
[258]
24
atha mahāpathayātrādi
tatra brahmapurāṇe |
mahāpathasya yātrā ca kartavyā tuhinopari |
āśritya satyaṃ dhairyaṃ ca sadyaḥ svargapradā hi sā ||
yāvat paurandarau lokau na yātaḥ kāryagauravāt |
tāvat tuṣāramadhye tu kas tanuṃ tyaktum utsahet ||
yatas tuṣāradāhyāṃs tu muñcan prāṇān vicetanaḥ |
pradakṣiṇāvartaśikhaṃ paśyed dīptaṃ hutāśanam ||
sāṅkarṣaṇaṃ vapur viṣṇoś caṇḍāgniṃ bhavadāhakam |
vāyupurāṇe |
yo vāhitāgnipravaro vīrādhyānaṃ gato ‘pi vā |
samādhāya manaḥ pūrvaṃ mantram uccāreyec chanaiḥ ||
tvam agne rudratvaṃ sudhāmahodadhis tvaṃ
sarve mārutāḥ kṣipram īyire |
tvaṃ vātaiyāsisagarī yas tvaṃ
prasthimāyīrūpaḥ pātayan mām ||
ity evaṃ manasā mātraṃ samyag uccāraṇaṃ dvijaḥ |
agniṃ praviśate yas tu rudralokaṃ sa gacchati ||
[259]        agnis tu bhāvān kālaḥ kālo rudra iti smṛtaḥ |
tasmād yaḥ praviśed agniṃ sa rudram abhivartate ||
devīpurāṇe |
parameśvara uvāca |
yad idaṃ bhairavaṃ rūpaṃ kṛtaṃ bhūtakṣayaṃ prati |
anugrahāya bhūtānāṃ bhūdharendra bhaviṣyati ||
tasmin ye bhāvam āpannā matputrā mayi bhāvitāḥ |
bhavanti te ca bhartāro mama tulyā bhavantu te ||
bhuktvā bhogāṃs tathā te tu rājarāje pure ciram |
kramād anubhavaṃ yānti tatra mokṣaṃ paraṃ dhruvam ||
evaṃ tāsāṃ varo datto devadevena śūlinā |
narāṇāṃ bhartṛkāṇāṃ tu nityam ānandako ravaḥ ||
brahmovāca |
ye vai saṃbhāvam āpannāḥ kṛtvā manasi vai śivam |
bhairavaṃ yānti te rudrabhaktyā yānti paraṃ padam ||
ye vā snehād bhayāl lobhāt kautukād yānti bhairavam |
te ‘pi caitat prabhāveṇa bhuvanānāṃ mahīpatim ||
sevante kramayogena rudratvaṃ yānti te dvijāḥ |
atha vā bhairavaṃ rūpaṃ paṭagaṃ surapūjitam ||
kṛtvā prāyān mahābāho bhairavaṃ sarvakāmadam |
pañcaviṃśabhujaṃ devaṃ pīnāṅgaṃ surapūjitam ||
khaḍgakheṭadharaṃ kāryaṃ śūlodyatakaraṃ param |
gajacarmadharaṃ cānyau cakrodyatabhujaṃ tathā ||
[260]        khaṭvāṅgaṃ ca kapālaṃ ca vajraṃ ḍamarukaṃ tathā |
evaṃvidhena rūpeṇa saṃdaṣṭādharam ānanam ||
ajjakaṃ mindabhāvaṃ tu danturaṃ tu trilocanam ||
kurvīta bhairavaṃ devaṃ susurāsuravanditam |
nānā śivā śivair yuktaṃ nānābharaṇabhūṣitam ||
navayauvanaśobhāḍhyaṃ sarvaśobhāprakāśakam |
churikā nāgarājena vāsukiś copavītakam ||
kulikas tu jaṭābandhe śaṅkhapālena kaṅkaṇam |
takṣakaḥ padmarāgaś ca kāryau keyūramaṇḍale ||
padvakarkoṭakau nāgau nūpurau pādagau śubhau |
evaṃ devaṃ prakurvīta bhairavaṃ sarvakāmadam ||
tasya dvāḥsthau prakartavyau pīnāṅgau sarvalakṣaṇau |
śūlahastau śubhau devau gajavājimukhau parau ||
dvārakoṇe tu gaṅgāyā dvāre kuryāt tu bhairave |
udvegaṃ tanupīḍādīn prakuryād aśivaṃ bahu ||
maṭhakūpādibhedena bhayaṃ kartur yathā bhavet |
evaṃ cātra prasaṅgena kathitaṃ tava suvrate ||
kapālaśūlahastau tu utpalāṅkuśadhāriṇau |
dvāḥsthau devasya kartavyau sarvābharaṇabhūṣitau ||
bhairavaṃ cārdhavadanaṃ brahmaviṣṇvādibhir yutam |
śūlabhinnāndhakaṃ rūpaṃ dhāryamāṇaṃ tu kalpayet ||
evaṃ paṭagataṃ kṛtvā pūjayitvāparaṃ vibhum |
[261]        parāgaṃ sarvamantreśaṃ raktamālyāmbarādibhiḥ ||
ātmānaṃ bhūṣayitvā tu mudrālaṃkṛtapāṇinā |
sarvadevotthitaṃ devaṃ vrajed vīrasureśvaram||
vītaśokabhayonmohaḥ paramānandam āsthitam |
prāpya tad vai gṛhaṃ ramyaṃ bhogamokṣapradāyakam ||
pūjāṃ kṛtvā tu deveśaṃ gajāndhakamahāpaham |
ārohet paramaṃ sthānaṃ bhuktimuktipradāyakam ||
aghoraṃ cintayitvā tu rūpaṃ svacchandadāyakam |
tasya vaktrānale homam ātmāhutyānukārayet ||
devacakraṃ hutāśaṃ ca vayaṃ ghṛtatilādikam |
hotavyaṃ tena bhāvena parāṃ siddhisamīhakaiḥ ||
pātāṣṭakena vīreṇa yathāvat tan nibodhata |
pataṅga iva cātmānaṃ dīpte ‘gnau nikṣiped yathā ||
pātaṃ pataṅganāmānaṃ haṃsasaṃjñam ataḥ param |
saṃyamya pakṣasaṅghātaṃ kṛtvā vegavatīṃ tanum ||
taṃ pātaṃ haṃsanāmānaṃ sādhakecchāphalapradam |
mṛgo ‘ndharūpagartādilaṅghane vai yathā bhavet ||
samapādadvayas tadvan mṛgapātaḥ sa ucyate |
musalaṃ musalī bhūtvā pātaṃ yat tvadulūkhale ||
vimānadhvajamālādi śākhālolādikaṃ labhet |
vṛṣavan nardayitvā tu dhanvānaṃ kakudaṃ dvija ||
vṛṣapātaṃ vijānīyāc chakralokaphalapradam |
[262]        siṃho gajendranidhane yathā vikramate tanum ||
evaṃ tatkṛtabhāvas tu yātaḥ siṃhaṃ hradau mataḥ |
kṛtvā ca bhairavaṃ rūpaṃ sāyudhaṃ vigatajvaraḥ ||
śivānale kṣipet kāyaṃ taṃ pātaṃ bhairavapradam |
pataṅgāder yathā pātā vātmabhāvagatā dvija ||
tathā te phaladāḥ sarve kramato bhairavaṃ padam |
bhavanāni vicitrāṇi asaṃkhyeyāni saṃkhyayā ||
annapānāni yānīha kramaśaḥ saṃśritāni tu |
dīkṣādināpi saṃpannā ye ‘pi bhogavasaṅgatāḥ ||
te ‘pi bhuktvā ciraṃ bhogāṃs te yānti paramaṃ padam |
pāpo ‘pi hi pumāṃs tatra varṇāśramavivrjitaḥ ||
prabhāvād devadevasya bhuṅkte ca paramaṃ sukham |
nandī kedāradevaś ca tathā rudramahālayaḥ ||
bhairaveṇa tu tulyāni bhogān te mokṣadāni tu |
catvāri devaśārdūla sarvānugrahakāṇi tu ||
durātmano ‘pi mucyante gatvaitāny api ye narāḥ |
vicitrarūpasaṃpannāḥ sarvakāmasukhapradāḥ ||
kanyādviraṣṭavarṣāś ca pīnonnatapayodharāḥ |
bhuvaneṣu vicitrās tāḥ pātād bhuñjanti mānavāḥ ||
nāryo vā patanaṃ kuryus tadā bhuñjanti bhairavān |
puruṣān divyabhogādyān mama rūpān mahābalān ||
[263]        gāndharvaṃ ca tathā brāhmaṃ vāyavaṃ cāpi vāruṇam |
tathā vaidyādharaṃ sauraṃ saindraṃ cāndraṃ samaṃ sthitam ||
svakāmabhogasaṃpannaṃ patanād bhuvanaṃ bhavet |
svakāmato bhuñjayitvā cānte yānti paraṃ padam ||
vaiṣṇavāsthimayīmālāṃ kambukaṃ śāmbhavaṃ sadā |
dhārayed devadeveśo lokānugrahakāraṇāt ||
ye citradhātukāṣṭhottaṃ ratnaśailamayaṃ narāḥ |
pūjayanti kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ te prayānti śivaṃ padam ||
evaṃ gṛhe ‘tha śaile vā nadīvindyāṭavīṣu ca |
bhairavaṃ pūrayed yas tu sa labhed īpsitaṃ phalam ||
patraṃ puṣpaṃ maṭhaṃ kūpam ārāmāṇi ca bhairave |
kṛtvā ca tāni catvāri deyāni sukhasiddhaye ||
yad datvā sarvadevānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ |
vipro vā vedaviduṣo tat phalaṃ bhairavāl labhet ||
koṭidhā nātra saṃdehaḥ sarvātmā sarvayogataḥ |
kālikāpurāṇe |
hariścandra iti khyāto bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ ||
yasmād asmin kṛto hy eṣa prayāto ‘tra kṛtaṃ punaḥ |
tasmād bhṛgur ayaṃ loke puṇyo bhavati sarvaśaḥ ||
atra yo ‘bhyetya deveśaṃ bhṛguprapatanaṃ naraḥ |
sādhayeta sa gacched vai haralokam atiprabham ||
mahāpātakasaṃyukto vyādhinā samupadrutaḥ |
yo ‘tra sādhayate prāṇān so ‘pi mucyen mahābhayāt ||
[264]        yad vā prārthayate ‘nyac ca prādhānye nātra kiṃcana |
tat prāpnuyān naraḥ sarvam acirāt tatra vai smṛtam ||
ādityapurāṇe |
durvāsā uvāca |
kīdṛśais tat tapodānaiḥ purīṃ paśyanti mānavāḥ |
bhānor vā brahmaṇaḥ śambhor viṣṇor indrasya sarvaśaḥ ||
bhānur uvāca |
rājyārthe nihatā ye ca rājāno dharmatatparāḥ |
agnividyuddhatā ye ca siṃhavyāghrahatāś ca ye ||
prāpnuvanti ca te sarve purīm airāvatīṃ śubhām |
sākṣād dhi bhagavān agnir nāgasya vasate mukhe ||
siṃhavyāghragajendrāṇāṃ viṣṇur eva vyavasthitaḥ |
vidyudagnihatā ye ca siṃhavyāghrahatāś ca ye ||
nāgaiś caiva hatā ye ca te narāḥ puṇyakarmiṇaḥ |
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāṃdhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīlakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍe mahāpathayātrādimāhātmyaṃ samāptam |
iti lakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
tīrthakāṇḍaṃ samāptam
